# Working notes

Read this first when picking the project back up. Other docs: `README.md` (user-facing), `docs/AUTOMATION.md` (the auto-spec: skills, stats, gear, item rules)
and `docs/QUESTER.md` (the quest system). This file is the state of play: how it fits together, what is verified, what is not, and the facts that cost time to learn.
Last reorganised 2026-11-11.

**Standing rules and the tests that protect each one: `docs/RULES.md`. Read it before changing anything that touches team sync, quests, items, gear, gold or stats.**

## What it is

Qolbot (Go) replaces `D2Bot.exe`. It starts Diablo II under Wine (hidden Xvfb display on Linux), injects `D2BS.dll`, and runs
the Kolbot scripts in `d2bs/kolbot`. A web page (127.0.0.1:8080) controls everything. Two modes, chosen by the "Are you playing?"
toggle (`Config.NotPlaying`):

- **Playing (Yes):** you host a TCP/IP game; bots join (`D2BotTcpIpJoin.dbj`) and follow you (`Follower.js`): town chores, skill and
  stat points, gear upgrades, fighting, quest NPC visits. They pick up possible gear upgrades (to try on, identify and sell if they are not) and what the keeper rules keep; see docs/AUTOMATION.md "What bots do with items".
- **Not playing (No):** one bot is the **leader** (`roleOf` = host when `NotPlaying && Player == Character`). It hosts
  (`D2BotTcpIpHost.dbj` + `default.dbj`), runs the objective (Farm: chosen locations in act order; Quest: `Questing.js` ->
  `Quester.runQuests(false)`) on the chosen difficulty, and is the only bot that picks up loot. The others are followers.
  Start launches the leader first, waits for its `Game:` status, passes `team=` so the leader waits (max 4 min) for its team.

## Code map

| Where | What |
|---|---|
| `cmd/qolbot/main.go` | Config struct, Manager, profile start/stop, watchdog, prefix setup |
| `settings.go` | `roleOf`, `setPlaying`, farm catalog (`farmAll`), `setGoal`, playing/goal/difficulty state |
| `specplan.go`, `internal/spec/` | reads the `.d2s`, plans skills/stats/build, writes `d2bs/kolbot/data/<profile>.spec.json` per bot |
| `ini.go` | writes `d2bs/d2bs.ini` every start (starter + script per role); never hand-edit |
| `rpc.go` | `D2Bot.*` calls from scripts arrive as WM_COPYDATA from `cmd/d2bridge` (Win32 helper under Wine) |
| `save.go` | graceful stop: "prepexit" -> bridge `savequit` (ESC, click Save and Exit) -> poll the `.d2s` stamp -> PlugY `/save` fallback |
| `pickit.go` | Keeper tab rules -> `pickit/Generated.nip`; base-item rules; import of the loot filter; `autoequipFile(class)` |
| `botlog.go` | tails `d2bs/d2bs.log` per bot -> `logs/bot-<name>.log`; `reProblem` raises "script problem" console lines; keeps each bot's last activity line (`State.Activity`) and parses the pickit's "Picked up ..." lines into `PickedItem`s (newest 200 in memory, 60 sent in `/api/state` as `items`) for the Activity tab |
| `logfile.go` | logging, preflight (Wine / Xvfb / game path, with install hints), `clearLogs` |
| `web.go`, `web/index.html` | API and the single embedded page (`go:embed`). `QOLBOT_DEV=1 ./qolbot` serves the page from the file on every request |
| `prefixcmd.go` | private Wine prefix `~/.qolbot_wine_prefix` (`ownsPrefix` guards: only that or one inside the app folder is touched) |
| `botgame.go` | read-only checks of PlugY.ini / BaseMod.ini (World Event warnings); `plugyProblem`: bots REFUSE to start (via `gameProblem`, so web banner + every launch path) when PlugY is active (`ActivePlugin=1`) and either `ActiveSharedStashInMultiPlayer=1`, or `ActiveWorldEvent=1` together with `ActiveAutoSell=1` (AutoSell alone is harmless); repoints an old `botgame/` config |
| `joinwatch.go` | warns when a bot sits on Join Game 50 s, quoting the game's own `D2YYMMDD.txt` load errors |
| `d2bs/kolbot/libs/common/AutoSpec.js` | applies the plan (`apply()`): scripts, goal, farm, pickit lists, noPickup, PublicMode, auto-save, storage, report switches |
| `libs/bots/Follower.js`, `Questing.js` | following the leader; the Questing script that runs the Quester |
| `libs/common/Quester.js`, `QuestScripts.js`, `QuestNpcs.js` | the Quester: core and helpers (`runQuests`, `clearAndCapture`, ...); the 25 quests, one function each (added to the Quester); the quest NPC visits (added to the Quester; talk only when a reward is pending, repeat talks) |
| `libs/common/QuestTable.json`, `QuestTable.js` | THE quest table (ids, titles, scripts, NPCs, the choose-the-reward quests, what each quest gives): read by the Quester AND by Qolbot (`cmd/qolbot/quests.go`) |
| `libs/common/QuickTravel.js` | Quick Travel: `QuickTravel.sorc()` / `QuickTravel.portal(place, area, act)`: a Sorceress's portal instead of a walk (the boss scripts and quests call it) |
| `libs/common/QuestItems.js` | the quest consumables: room for them, use them up, log it |
| `libs/common/Report.js` | how a script reports: `Report.log/note/problem/fail(source, msg)` and `Report.mixin(this, "Name")`; the log line is always written under the script's own name, chat only when the report switches allow |
| `libs/common/Saver.js` | saving the character (`/save`): `now`, `soon(reason)`, `auto()`, `minutes()` |
| `libs/common/Plan.js`, `Throttle.js` | reading the plan file (`Plan.read/get`); once-per-period rate limits (`Throttle.due/reset`) |
| `libs/common/Team.js`, `Chores.js`, `Pack.js` | the leader's wait for its team; whether a town trip is worth making; the pack's lock matrix and moving keepers to the stash |
| `libs/common/AutoSpec.js` | ONLY `apply()`: puts the plan (template, skill/stat points, attack skills, equipment, objective) into Config |
| `scripts/jsmoke.py` | smoke test of the small libs/common modules in headless Firefox with the game's globals mocked (`scripts/jsmoke.py out.png`, look for ALL PASSED) |
| `libs/common/Misc.js`, `Pickit.js`, `Town.js` | gear swap rules, `Item.takeable/sellable` (both use `Config.Protect`), pickups, selling, gambling |
| `D2BotTcpIpHost.dbj` | host starter; screen 20 (difficulty) via `chooseDifficulty` |

Tests: `go test ./...` (pickit, autoequip lists, web page mocks, preflight, prefix ownership, logs, save, spec planner). Always
run `./build.sh` after Go changes before a live test (the launcher runs `bin/`); do a live start after touching code that runs
under `m.mu` (unit tests missed a deadlock once).

## Keeper rules and gear (two separate jobs)

- **Keep (Keeper tab, leader only, Farm and Quest):** `PickitRules` in `Config.PickitRules` -> `Generated.nip`. Sub-tabs (Gems & Runes, Uniques & Sets, Magic & Rares, Base Items; tabs above the card like Config): Uniques and
  sets (base tier "X or higher": Normal/Exceptional/Elite, no item-level rules because Hell drops are ilvl 80+), Runes and gems,
  Magic and rare (`cmd/qolbot/magic.go`: one column per item type or charm size, each with a primary and a secondary pool of stat codes and minimums such as `ias 20`, and how many of each pool an item needs; the Miscellaneous tab has an "Anything Else" text area of raw pickit lines, the `trophy` field; the starter entries come from the author's old Rares.nip and the shorter pickits, which are reference only and not loaded),
  Bases (base-tier checkbox "from X quality or higher" = `BasesOn`/`BaseMin`, ethereal armor and helm of a chosen base tier (default exceptional), per-row nickname + item code rows). Every line on the page is a checkbox; runes, gems, resist total and base tier have a dropdown that only counts when checked. No NIP is ever shown on the page; hand-made lists
  are hidden (`customPickitLists = false`). Followers pick nothing up while you are not playing (`plan.noPickup`).
- **Self-gearing (Autoequip, every bot):** since 2026-11-15 one list, `Autoequip/gear.nip` (`autoequipFile`, also in `_DefaultConfig.js`),
  whose single rule is `[tier] == qolgear.tier(item)` (libs/common/Gear.js: the score for the character's BUILD from its spec.json;
  see the 2026-11-15 entry at the end); `merc.nip` for the mercenary. The parser lowercases lines and splits on `[`, `==` and `#`,
  so tier expressions may not contain them (`autoequip_test.go`). Unique, set, crafted and socketed gear already worn is never
  replaced (Equipment tab, `Config.Protect`: the protected kinds are also never identified, worn, sold or picked up as upgrades; keep-list pickups ignore it).
  Item rules (docs/AUTOMATION.md "What bots do with items"): no sell/pickup-gear/jewelry options any more; `Town.keepVerdict` (upgrade > keep list > sell), `Item.putAway`; all UNTESTED in a real game (edited 2026-11-10, no JS engine here). Crafting is forced off in AutoSpec.apply; bots look at dropped gear at the end of `Town.doChores` and in Follower's 30 s equip block; unidentified protected items are ignored by the pickup (`Pickit.pickItems`/`fastPick`).
- Quest mode uses both: the keep rules for loot and the Autoequip lists for dressing the leader.
- Desirable bases start from `defaultBases()` (pickit.go): the 27 plain bases the user's `qol_ui_filter.cfg` exempts from hiding at filter
  level 1+ (kite/dragon shield, monarch, polearms, scythes, big swords and axes incl. thresher, light armors). Shown until the list is
  saved once (`BasesSeeded`); after that an empty list stays empty.
- NIP log format: `Valid entries: <non-tier>/<all valid>`; `0/4` on merc.nip means 4 valid tier rules.
- Magic & Rares leniency (`lenient` in the rules, off by default): when on, each numeric minimum becomes `(stat)*100 >= minimum*[charlevel]`, so a bot asks for its own level in percent of the number (level 10: 10%, at least one point of the stat; 99: all of it). `[charlevel]` is a keyword added to `libs/NTItemParser.dbl` (it becomes `me.charlvl`, 100 at 99). Skill bonuses, sockets, durability and level limits are never scaled. It is evaluated per item in game, so a level-up counts at once. Auto-equip (the Autoequip lists and `Item.autoEquip`) decides what to wear on its own and is untouched by these rules. UNTESTED in game.
- Desirable class items (Base Items page; `cmd/qolbot/classitems.go`, `classskills.go`): a checkbox with a base tier, and one button per class (Barbarian primal helms, Druid pelts, Paladin auric shields, Necromancer voodoo heads, Sorceress orbs, Assassin claws, Amazon weapons). Each opens an input box of that class's individual skills and a minimum (`hurricane 3`), "at least N of"; written as `[skill<name>] >= n` for magic, rare and crafted items of that kind. The skill list per class was generated from `libs/NTItemAlias.dbl` and `sdk/skills.txt` (`classSkillList`). I could not find single-skill bonuses for class items in this game's MagicPrefix/MagicSuffix/AutoMagic tables, so that part rests on the author's say-so and the other pickits (`[skillfrozenorb] >= 3` on orbs); UNTESTED in game.

## Activity tab (formerly "Status"; the address is /activity, /status still works)

Two columns. Left: a pod per running bot (class, level, role "Leading Farm" / "Following X", state, **zone**, last activity + age). Right: **Activity**
list (newest 60 of the newest 200 events, memory only): items kept for a reason (click for the item's stats), gear equipped, boss kills (with
zone), quests completed, level ups (achievement pod with confetti), stat and skill points spent. Potions and tier-only pickups are not listed:
an upgrade shows when the bot actually equips it.

How it is fed: `followBotLogs` tails `d2bs/d2bs.log` per bot. `activity.go` has `friendlyActivity` (log line -> plain sentence, everything else
dropped) and `parseEvent` (line -> Activity entry). The scripts print the lines on purpose: `Engaging X` / `Killed X (Area)` (Attack.kill and
Attack.clear in Attack.js), `Traveling to X` (Pather.useWaypoint), `Going back to town` / `Going gambling` (Town.js), `Equipped X (gear upgrade)`
(Misc.js autoEquip), `Leveled up to level N` and the zone (ToolsThread.js; the zone goes by RPC `D2Bot.zone` -> `State.Zone`). Stat and skill
spending come from AutoStat.js / AutoSkill.js prints (only when the Automation switches allow it: Safe mode turns them all off). Item stat text comes from the RPC `printToItemLog` that
`Misc.logItem` already sends: `noteItemDesc` matches it to the entry by bot, time (20 s) and name; no match = "stats unknown" in the modal
(unidentified or unlogged). The pane id must not be `status` (`window.status` shadows it): it is `stpane`. Colour codes in a "Picked up" line
arrive as U+FFFD, 0xFF or "ÿ". The page routes by URL (/status, /pickit/base-items, /config/game ...; the server returns the page for any path).

With `QOLBOT_DEV=1`, `/api/state` also carries a `demo` block (`devdemo.go`: four made-up bots, a dozen events of every kind) and the Activity tab shows it instead of real data, so its look can be edited with no game running. Real data is never replaced outside dev mode.

- REMOVED 2026-11-15 (the inventory window reads the save file now; see the entry of that date): Inventory view (click a bot card on the Activity tab): `tools/ToolsThread.js` `reportInventory` sends `D2Bot.inventory(json)` every 10 s when the bot's items moved (equipped, inventory, belt, cube, stash; stat text for anything above normal quality); Qolbot keeps the last JSON in `State.Inv` and serves it at `/api/inventory?name=`; the page draws it in `invdlg` (grids at the configured sizes, click an item for its stats). UNTESTED in a real game: it assumes `D2Bot.<anything>` is passed through like `D2Bot.zone`, and that `item.fname`, `item.description` and `item.sizex` behave as they do in the item logger. The dev demo has a sample inventory.

## Bot card statuses and their icons (the card text is `friendlyActivity` in `activity.go`; the icon table is `actIcons` in `web/index.html`)

A status is what the bot is doing right now and it changes every few seconds. The bots print these lines as things happen; things that happened
(kills, quests, level-ups, points, gear, drinking a potion) are Activity entries or nothing, never a status. The dev demo (`QOLBOT_DEV=1`,
`devdemo.go`) has one bot per row.

| Icon | Status | Printed by |
|---|---|---|
| ⚔️ | Fighting demons | `Attack.noteFighting` before each attack, at most every 4 s; also "Detected hostile monsters" |
| 👹 | Engaging X (a boss) | `Attack.js` |
| 🧭 | Traveling (a long walk, at most every 4 s), Traveling to X (an exit, or a farm script starting), Going back to town | `Pather.noteTravel`, `Pather.moveToExit`, the script label, `Town.visitTown` |
| 🗺️ | Taking waypoint to X | `Pather.useWaypoint` |
| 🌀 | Taking portal | `Pather.usePortal` |
| 💬 | Talking to X (an NPC) | `Town.initNPC` |
| 🗄️ | Opening the stash | `Town.openStash` |
| ⏳ | Waiting on teammates, Waiting for a portal, Waiting for leader (idle in town, every 8 s), Waiting for first report | the leader's team wait, the follower's request to the leader, `Follower.autoFollow`, no report yet |
| 🔌 / 🚪 | Disconnected / Joining game, Creating game, Choosing difficulty | Qolbot (`noteStarterStatus`, `rpc.go`): the starter's "Game: ..." status turning into "Join Game" while the bot should be running is a disconnect (an Activity entry too); the card then shows the menu status and the zone "Menu" |
| 👣 / 👀 | Following X, Following the leader / Searching for leader X | Follower |
| 📜 / 💾 / 🚀 | Embarking on quest / Saving the character / Team is here | script labels, the team wait |
| 📍 | Just arrived (set on every zone change; the first attack replaces it) | Qolbot (`rpc.go`) |
| ▶️ | any other status (a script with no label: "Running X"): every bot card has exactly one icon | |

## Logs

`logs/qolbot.log` (also `/log`), `logs/wine.log`, `logs/bot-<name>.log`, `d2bs/d2bs.log` (D2BS console incl. `SAY:` lines from the
`say()` wrapper in `default.dbj`). Older runs: `logs/qolbot.prev*.log`.

## Verified live, and not

VERIFIED (real games): join and follow; town chores; pickup-area clearing; graceful save (Save and Exit works without PlugY, 2-4 s per
bot); Ctrl-C and Stop saving; Xvfb display selection; prefix rebuild from scratch (~37 s); storage settings plumbing.

NOT VERIFIED in a real game (unit tests / mocks only): everything in not-playing mode (host start, difficulty screen click coordinates
264,297/340/383, leader waiting for team, Farm scripts, Questing idles forever after some quests), quest NPC names, FollowNoFight /
FollowDistance (Summon druid), caster.nip / melee.nip tiers and the Assassin claw line, the stat-based pickit rules (NIP parse
check: watch `Loaded NIP: Generated.nip ... Valid entries`), merc equips, macOS/Windows
builds (compile only).

Next planned test: farm mode on Normal with high-level characters, every location script, logs checked; then Quest. The user must stop
their own Qolbot first and name the leader and follower. Only characters they name may be started as bots (earlier rule: Darius, Temp).

## Live test history

### Farm, 2026-11-10 (Darius level 15 Paladin leading, Temp level 1 Barbarian following, Normal, Corpsefire)

WORKED: not-playing start (leader hosts, "team" wait, Temp joined, Farm script Corpsefire ran, items and kill reported, ForceSave "Saving."),
the run loop repeats (Run duration 60-200 s), Status tab fed live, saves of both characters changed (their saves were backed up before the test;
the backups are not in the repo). Not confirmed: the difficulty-select screen click (the characters were already at/near Normal).
SEEN: Game.exe exits with 0xC0000005 right as a run's scripts end (leader), then Qolbot restarts the bot (~1 min); the user's `PlugY.ini` still has the
World Event / AutoSell on, which crashes bots (warned at start; not changed because Qolbot never writes the game folder). "Paladin.Darius.js not
found!" is Kolbot's harmless per-character config probe (no longer raised as a problem). Test settings were restored in `qolbot.json` afterwards
(player Reform, Hell, Corpsefire + Mausoleum).

### Quest, 2026-11-10 (Darius leading, Xena following, Normal)

Questing started ("Attempting to clear and capture", the Forgotten Tower: Tamoe Highland -> Black Marsh), the leader fights and chickens when low, level 16 was
reported. NO quest finished in ~8 minutes, so the quest logic is still unproven end to end. FOUND AND FIXED: **portal loop** in `Attack.clearLevel`:
`Town.needHealing()` uses `Config.HealHP/HealMP = 100` (the user's "heal at every town visit"), which is true at all times (`<=` 100), so the bot went to town after
every room. Out in the field `Town.needFieldHealing()` now counts only life at or under 50% (or poisoned/cursed). Also: level-ups were never reported (`me.charlevel`
does not exist, it is `me.charlvl`), a bot's status kept the sentence from the area it had left (a zone change now sets "Just arrived", replaced by "Fighting demons" when the bot starts attacking). Test settings restored.

## Hard-won facts (do not rediscover)

1. **Wine prefix recipe:** a bare prefix crashes the game (stack overflow in a D2 DLL). `setupPrefix`: `wineboot -i`, copy the
   runner's vkd3d dlls if it ships any, Windows 7. No winetricks needed. System wine 11 works; GE-Proton is not required. The bots
   need their own prefix: the user cannot play while bots share theirs, and a shared prefix with Xvfb dies with `X BadWindow`.
   Never export/resolve `WINEPREFIX` from the parent shell (it once ran a prefix update on the user's real prefix); Qolbot always sets it.
2. **D2BS reads switch values only inside double quotes** (`-profile "Temp"`). All values are quoted in `startProfile`.
3. **Window title = profile name**; scripts address each other by it. The bridge sets the title by PID.
4. The first `Handle` message is lost; the manager repeats it every 2 s until the script's first call arrives.
5. **`-direct -txt` is required** (the mod's Data folder). No `-3dfx` (cannot combine with `-w`). `-ns` works for the bots (the old
   warning that it crashes was wrong in this setup).
6. **Crash cause: PlugY World Event / ActiveAutoSell** (and the BaseMod World Event). They crash D2BS-injected games (0xC0000417 in
   msvcrt) within minutes. Set both off in `PlugY.ini` and `BaseMod.ini`; Qolbot only reads them and warns. `PlugY.ini` is CRLF: edit
   with CR-aware regexes (`sed` eats the `\r` and PlugY then reads defaults).
7. **Saving:** killing a game saves nothing. `quit()`/`quitGame()` do not save. Save and Exit through the in-game menu does (needs a
   follower to hold still first: `prepexit`). When the host leaves, every follower's unsaved progress is lost, and followers rejoin
   a new host by themselves (~85 s). Periodic auto-save exists (`autoSaveMinutes`, default 5; the AutoSaveThread for the leader).
8. **Config.Inventory matrix:** 1 = the bot's cells, 0 = locked. Matrix size must equal the inventory size or `Compare` throws.
   Container sizes cannot be detected (`getBaseStat("Inventory")` returns garbage), so `storage` is a setting.
9. **Loader tolerance:** `AutoSpec.apply` must tolerate plan fields an older Qolbot did not write (one threw and broke the bot config).
10. `.d2s` header: name @20, status @36 (0x04 hardcore, 0x20 expansion), class @40, level @43.
11. **JS files** under `libs/common` are not UTF-8 (`grep -a`); `Misc.js`/`Town.js`/`Pickit.js` are latin-1 with mixed line endings,
    `Quester.js` CRLF. Edit as bytes. There is no JS engine here except headless Firefox (`new Function(src)` + mocked globals).
12. Never `pkill -x Game.exe` (kills the user's own game) and never `pkill -f` with text from your own command line. Stop bots through
    the Qolbot API or its PID.
13. Unknown URLs on the web server return the page with HTTP 200, so a stale running binary looks like "button does nothing":
    restart Qolbot after Go changes (dev mode only refreshes the page file).
14. Quest status bits: `getQuest(id,1) && !getQuest(id,0)` = reward pending. Quest credit needs the main player to have it too.
15. Wine/Go gotchas fixed with regression tests: stale Xvfb socket files made bots start visible (`TestStartXvfbSurvivesStaleDisplayFiles`);
    `writeSpec` held `m.mu` while calling a function that takes it (`TestWriteSpecDoesNotDeadlock...`).

## Rules from the user

- STAT RULES BY BUILD KIND (2026-11-16): every build ends with at least 180 strength. Melee and "ranged melee" (Javazon, Throw Barbarian, Bowazon): strength, dexterity and vitality equal, one point at a time, to 180, then vitality. Summoners (Summon Druid, Summon Necromancer): strength and vitality. Casters (every other build): strength, energy and vitality. Custom builds: strength to 180. The full table is in `docs/AUTOMATION.md`, "Stat rules by build kind"; the templates are `d2bs/kolbot/libs/config/Templates/<Class>/<Build>.js`. Auto Respec undoes stats that disagree with these (it did to Barabas on 2026-11-16): uncheck it for characters whose stats you set by hand.

- Never add or change files in the real Diablo II folder without asking. Qolbot reads `PlugY.ini`,
  `BaseMod.ini` and `qol_ui_filter.cfg` there and writes nothing.
- Copy of the save folder before tests; Normal difficulty with their high-level characters is acceptable.
- Web copy: "you" not "I"; Qolbot capitalised; Solarized colours only in the console; class pills; flag icon for the leader.
- README.md is the user's own: small edits only.

- THE PROFILE IS A PAGE, NOT A MODAL (2026-11-16, the user: "maybe we try making char profiles loading in the site itself and not in a modal"): the Profile button on the Bots tab (and a click on a bot's card) opens `/profile/<name>`, a page that replaces the Bots table with the Char / Merc / Stats / Skills tabs and a "< Bots" button; the address works when reloaded or opened directly, the browser Back button returns to the Bots tab, and any other tab closes it. `showInv(name)` now navigates (`profOpen`); `renderInv(name)` draws it (the old dialog code). The item stats window (`itemdlg`) and the hover tooltip are still popups. Same day (the user: "can you make stash a separate tab, can we try the normal light bg color"): the tabs are now Char, Stash, Merc, Stats, Skills (the stash pages moved out of Char), and the page uses the site's own colours (`--card`, `--ink`, `--line`, `--dim`) instead of the game's dark panel; only the item grids and slots keep their dark cells, because the item pictures were drawn for them. Then (the user: "make the bg on the actual icons a sandy tan color light shade. and maybe draw some kind of UI around those equipment slots"): the item grids and slots are sand (`#e8d9b4`, grid lines `#cdb98a`), and the paper doll has a drawn frame (a double border in `#8a7036` on a sand gradient, with corner studs, `.gdoll::before` and `::after`). Then (the user: "use a really dark sady color to blend those rough dark edges on the icons"): the cells behind the pictures became a really dark sand (`#443826`, grid lines `#584a33`, borders `#8a7036`) so the dark halos of the item art blend in; the frame around the slots stays light sand. The hover on an item is a warm sand glow (`rgba(214,172,92,.30)`), in the same range (the user: "amend the hover color to stay in that dark sandy range"). Also a trial. If it looks wrong, the old dark look is one CSS rule (`#invdlg.gdlg`, `.gwrap`, `.ggold`, `.gsec>.gh`). Built and deployed; the server answers `/profile/Barabas` with the page. NOT looked at in a browser.
- CHAR TAB REWORKED AGAIN (2026-11-16, the user: "on the gear equiped UI put 'Equipment' as the label. Put the characters name as the header in the white zone (remove 'is carrying'). put the gold amount in the UI beneath the inventory centered. make a column on the char tab that is 1/3 and stack all the UIs in there with cube on the very bottom. On the 2/3 column to the right of that move the table of the char stats"; then "remove 'from the save file, saved 39m ago'"): the panel around the paper doll is labelled Equipment; the white card's header is the character's name alone (no \"is carrying\"); the gold and the stash gold are centred inside the Inventory panel under its grid; the Char tab is a grid of one third and two thirds (`.cgrid`): the left column stacks Equipment, Weapon swap, Inventory, the Horadric Cube and, last, the Belt (the user: \"put belt at the bottom of that stack\"); the right column holds the character's stats table (it moved off the Stats tab, so there is no Stats tab now: Char, Stash, Merc, Skills); the \"from the save file, saved ... ago\" line is gone. Under 900 px the columns stack. Deployed; the served page's script parses.
- THE LEVEL IS A BIG GREEN NUMBER (2026-11-16, the user: \"remove the name from that sheet, put the level as a big forest green number\"): the Name row is gone and so is the Experience row (the user: \"remove experience row\"), leaving strength, dexterity, vitality and energy under it; the character's level is shown large (68 px) at the top of the stats area with \"Level - <class>\" in small dim capitals under it (the class stays, only the name went). The colour is the site's green (`--accent`, the user's rule that a colour word means the site's own CSS variable), not a fixed forest-green hex.
- THE STATS TABLE HAS FEWER ROWS (2026-11-16, the user: remove \"Stat points to spend, Skill points to spend, Life, Mana, Stamina, Gold carried, Gold in the stash\"): `invStats` now shows the name and class, level, experience, strength, dexterity, vitality and energy. The API still sends the other values (`char.unspentStats`, `life`, `gold` ...), only the page does not show them.

- THE BOTS LIST DID NOT LOAD (2026-11-16 11:2x, the user: "I think something is wrong bc the bots list won't load anymore"): my last edit of `index.html` put "the character's level" inside a single-quoted JavaScript string; the apostrophe ended it, the whole page script failed to parse ("missing ) after argument list") and nothing on the page ran. The server was fine (`/api/state` answered). I had not re-checked the script after that edit. Fixed (`character\\'s`). `scripts/jstest.sh` now also parses the page's script with gjs and fails if it does not; run it after every edit of `index.html`, and check the SERVED page (`curl` it and parse), not only the file.
- MERC TAB LISTS HIS STATS (2026-11-16, the user: "on the merc page, to the right of the equipment, list the stats on the merc"): `ReportMerc` has `def` (the defense of the worn pieces added up) and `stats` (every stat of the worn pieces, runeword and socketed ones included, added up and worded by `propLines`: the game's own wording, all resistances on one line, the same stat from two pieces added); the Merc tab shows them in a "Stats from his gear" panel to the right of the paper doll. Not shown: the merc's own base life, damage, resistances and level (the save keeps his experience; turning it into a level and the base values needs Hireling.txt's formulas, not done), and set bonuses. Stats that grow per level use the CHARACTER's level. Checked on the real saves: Azrael (Desert Mercenary, Defense) comes out with defense 783, +225% Enhanced Damage, a level 16 Meditation Aura, +35% Faster Cast Rate, 5% mana stolen per hit. Deployed.
- SUMMON DRUID SKILL ORDER (2026-11-16, the user: "on a summon druid build, wearbear only needs one and lycanthropy one, then max out the summon skills (not ravens), then max out lycanthropy"; the summon skills are the four the build already used, the user's choice): `Druid/Summon.js` AutoSkill.Build is now Werebear 1, Lycanthropy 1, then Summon Grizzly, Summon Dire Wolf, Summon Spirit Wolf and Heart of Wolverine to 20 each (the user: "i would choose HOTW instead of Oak Sage"; Oak Sage keeps its one prerequisite point), then Lycanthropy to 20 with the rest. AutoSkill adds each skill's missing prerequisites one point at a time (checked in `AutoSkill.js`; from the game's Skills.txt: Raven for the Spirit Wolf, Werewolf for Lycanthropy, the Spirit Wolf and Oak Sage for the Dire Wolf, the Dire Wolf for the Grizzly), so Raven stays at 1. Before: Carrion Vine 1, Wolf 5, Werebear 1, Lycanthropy 5, then 10, 10, 10, 10 and Lycanthropy 10 before the summons went to 100. Level locks: Spirit Wolf and Oak Sage 6, Dire Wolf and Heart of Wolverine 18, Grizzly 30. About 84 of a whole game's ~110 skill points; the rest goes to Lycanthropy. UNTESTED in a game. A character that already has points elsewhere: Auto Respec (skills) judges it against this order.
- BOT ROW CLICK CHECKS THE BOX (2026-11-16, the user: "on bots page, if i click on a row of a bot, make that count toward checkmarking it"): a click anywhere on a row of the Bots table that is not on a button, the warning icon or the box itself checks or unchecks that bot's box (the leader who is you, with the box disabled, is skipped). One listener on the table (`index.html`), the same `pick()` the box uses. Not tried in a browser; needs a build and a server restart.
- PROFILE WINDOW TABS: CHAR, MERC, STATS, SKILLS (2026-11-16, the user: "on bots page change Inspect to Profile"; "add to the profile tabs, and include Stats and Skills ... maybe include level requirements on the skills in a table"; she said a copy of the in-game skill UI "might be better not to so that modded games with unique skills still show alright"). The bots-list button says "Profile". The window has four tabs: Char (paper doll, swap, inventory, belt, cube, stash pages: the old content), Merc, Stats and Skills. Stats are the base values from the save without gear: level, experience, the four stats, points to spend, base life/mana/stamina, gold. Skills are a table per tab of the class's tree from the game's OWN tables (Skills.txt reqlevel + SkillDesc.txt page/row/column, mod first, so unique skills show too): skill, level needed (red when above the character's level), points. New: `d2items.ClassSkills`, `SkillTabName`, `spec.Save` mana/stamina/experience, `Manager.charSheet`, `/api/inventory` returns `char`. Checked against the QOL mod's real tables (`TestRealSkills`: 30 skills per class, the Barbarian's tree right, e.g. Whirlwind level 30). Built and the server restarted (2026-11-16, no game running): `/api/inventory?name=Barabas` returns `char` with the Barbarian's level 59, stats, base life 975 and the three trees with their points (Combat 28, Masteries 6, Warcries 32); the served page has the Profile button. The tabs themselves were NOT looked at in a browser.

- THE MODS HAVE A CLASS EACH, AND `.deposit` TAKES A LOCK (2026-11-16, the user: "for functions that use Plugy, QOL.dll, or Quartermaster.dll put those in separate classes so they can be controlled, and on .deposit it's important that somehow (you can orchestrate) the bots don't fire that command at the same time"; it followed "and these .deposit calls are staggered so no one bot initiates it at the same moment?": they were not, except at the first two minutes of a game, 145 of 400 sends of the day within 2 s of another bot's, every party trip). New: `PlugY.js` (`/save`, `/insertpage`, `/swappage`, the first-page click, `newStashPage`), `Qol.js` (`.reset stats`, `.reset skills`, the two mercenary options), and `Quartermaster.js` rewritten around a LOCK: a file per bot (`data/qm.<profile>.json`: `req`, `busy`, `done`, heartbeat `at`), the earlier request goes first, 1.2 s gap after each send, a 0.4 s look-again after taking it, a dead bot's record ignored after 10 s, 45 s (20 s on an emergency visit) at most to wait. Callers changed: `Saver` (3x), `ForceSave`, `Follower`'s "save" order -> `PlugY.save()`; `Cubing.newStashPage`/`stashFirstPage` and `Pack`'s fallback -> `PlugY.newStashPage()`/`firstPage()`; `Respec` -> `Qol.resetStats()`/`resetSkills()`; `AutoSpec` sets `Config.UsesQol` and the merc options through `Qol` (so they are off when "Using QOL?" is). The old turn-by-name stagger is gone. Each module does nothing and says so once when its Settings, Game box is unchecked. Tests: `scripts/jstest/quartermaster_test.js` (rules, and five bots reading the files at the same instants over 300 random starts: each sends once, never two together, at least 3 s apart, the last through in under 30 s), `mods_test.js`. NOT run in a game: whether the mod misbehaves at all when two bots send together was never seen; the lock removes the question. `docs/AUTOMATION.md`, "Game mods: one module each".
- WHEN A BOT TALKS TO A QUEST NPC, CHECKED (2026-11-16, the user: "on determining when a bot needs to talk to an NPC, i would just want to make sure it works properly. so like act 3 gidbin that quest will have bots talk to several npcs. yes i want all the bots to show as getting credit for quests at the same time, not just leaders and then by chance later the followers"; he agreed that "the leader now waits for the team's hand-ins before the next quest"). Findings: (a) the trigger is the game's own "reward waiting" bit (bit 1 set, bit 0 clear: `Quester.questPending`) or a quest item an NPC wants (`npcItems`); `Quester.questNpcs` then talks to EVERY NPC in the quest's list in order (`QuestTable.json` `npcs`: Gidbinn = Ormus, Asheara), up to 8 rounds each, re-reading the quest after every talk, and stops as soon as the quest is done; at most two sessions per quest per game. (b) The five saves on Nightmare today: the only quests open for anybody are Hell's Forge (reward waiting for Barabas, Xena, Sankt: the Cain hand-in), Tools of the Trade and Siege on Harrogath (reward waiting, but "manual": Charsi's imbue and Larzuk's socket are items only the player can choose, the bots say so and skip them) and Prison of Ice (bits set, no reward flag yet: the leader's quest in progress). Nothing a talk would settle is hidden from the trigger. (c) Why followers used to get credit "by chance later": the Normal Gidbinn completions were Darius 11-12 20:49, Barabas 11-13 18:32, Sankt 19:19, Xena 19:21: each in its own run. Now the leader's hand-in is followed by the team's (the call to town, each follower's `townQuestTalk`, the leader's wait), so the credits should come within seconds of each other. (d) NOT known: for a quest where each follower must carry the quest item itself (the Gidbinn dagger), whether the game gives every follower its own copy; Act 3 is already complete on Normal and Nightmare for all five, so it will show on Hell. Watch for `quest talk ormus round 1` and `quest talk asheara round 1` in each follower's log in the same minute as the leader's. Tests: `scripts/jstest/questnpcs_test.js` (a two-NPC quest, the retry cap, manual rewards skipped, another town's quest ignored, the follower's trigger and its two-minute throttle).
- QUEST NPC TALKS MOVE ON AFTER AN EMPTY ROUND (2026-11-16, live 14:31-14:33 Prison of Ice: every follower talked to Malah three more times, "pending true -> true, items used 0", before Anya completed it; the user: "the talking to malah was prob a programmed (try her 3 times) -- you can optimize that if you can detect which npc they need to talk to"). `Quester.questNpcs`: when another NPC of the plan still has something pending, a round that changes nothing ends the talks with this NPC at once; the waiting rounds ("come back later", 4 s apart) are kept for the last pending NPC and for hand-ins that expect something back. Not tested (no test of the retry loop exists), not verified live: to see it, a follower's log shows ONE `quest talk malah round 1` and then `Talking to anya`. DONE the same day: the Betrayal of Harrogath row of QuestTable.json was `"manual": true`, so `Quester.townQuestsOpen` skipped it and the followers never talked to Anya after Nihlathak (live 14:40-14:42: only Barabas did, the others had `...01110` where he had `...11110`); the user: "we want automated bot quest completion". The flag is removed; the table is read by Qolbot at its start (restart the server) and by the scripts at once. Not verified live. NOT done yet, awaiting the user: quest talks before `.deposit` and the pack tidy on arrival in town.
- QUEST STATES ARE RECORDED (2026-11-16, the user: "what would be ideal is if each quest knew how far into the quest they were and which npcs they need to talk to and when"; "start keeping a lot of states and let's try to make this perfect"). `libs/common/QuestStates.js` writes a line to `data/queststates.<profile>.log` (and the bot log as `QuestStates: ...`) whenever any of the 16 bits of quests 0-40 or the quest items (item type 39) in the pack change: time, difficulty, area, why, the NPC talked to last, `id: before > after`, items. It is called from `Quester.getQuestProgress`, when a quest is started or done, before and after every quest NPC talk (`Quester.talkToQuestNpc`) and when a quest item is used. It only records, nothing reads it, so it cannot change what a bot does. Tests: `scripts/jstest/queststates_test.js`. The next step is the stage table (quest -> bits/items -> the NPC to talk to) built from these lines; no table exists yet.
- THE PACK IS SORTED BEFORE THE CUBE IS UNLOADED, AND THE FULL-INVENTORY ALERT ONLY FOR WHAT STAYS (2026-11-16, the user: "when using the cube to temp store things -- when it's time to sort the inventory, deal with the items in the inventory first (to make room), then you'll have room to move the cube items into the inventory so they resume the normal ID flow"; "the yellow inventory full alert should only fire if the items in the inventory are the kind that will never get moved out -- such as stash or sale"). `Town.doChoresRun`: the unload used to be the FIRST chore, into a pack with no room. Now the pack goes through identify, sell and stash first (`Town.holdCube` makes `cubeToStash` wait during that round), then `unloadCube` moves the cube's items into the room that made, and identify, `clearInventory` and `stash(true)` run again, which also stashes what still does not fit from the cube unidentified (the "safest" rule above). `Town.cubeWaiting` counts what waits in the cube. `Item.noteFullPack(size)` raises the alert only when `Item.packStaysFull(size)`: with every item a town visit takes away (`tripItem`, not `userOwned`) removed from the pack, an item of that size still does not fit; a pack full of things for the stash or the shop is a passing state and says nothing. Tests: `cubepickup_test.js`. Not verified in a game.
- BACK TO THE OLD CUBE BEHAVIOUR, WITH A "FULL INVENTORY" ALERT (2026-11-16, the user: "i think your old behavior of 'items go in the cube if the inventory is full, and those items in the cube cannot be IDed but they will all automatically go into the stash' is the safest way to do it. but it should put an alert on the activity page saying the character has a full inventory"). SUPERSEDES the entries below about treating the cube like the pack: `Town.cubeToStash` again stashes EVERYTHING `canStash` accepts, identified or not (the `wantStash` filter is gone); the extra unload and identify passes (up to four), the tidy before the unload and the "items stuck in the cube" notice are removed, the one extra pass the original had is back. `Item.noteFullPack` (Misc.js) prints `Pack: NOTICE: <name> has a full inventory: ...` once per game the first time something goes into the cube because the pack has no room (`Item.cubeForRoom`, `Item.cursorToCube`); Qolbot shows it as the yellow box (`fullpack`, fatal.go `noticeFromLog`). The Activity entry for such an item says `Stashed [pack was full: not identified]` (a unique or set says what it is). The 2x4 notice (`bigspace`) stays. Not verified live.
- THE CUBE'S ITEMS NEED ROOM IN THE PACK TO BE WORKED ON (2026-11-16, the user: "you told me the bot can use cube for extra storage, but it will never treat those correctly if it doesn't have the space in the inventory to work with"). True: identify, sell and stash all work on the pack's cells, `Town.unloadCube` moves into free cells only. Now `Town.doChoresRun` runs `Layout.tidy` (the pack's items to the left, the free cells joined) BEFORE the first unload and after each pass, and when items are still waiting in the cube after the passes it prints `Pack: NOTICE: items are waiting in the cube and cannot be sorted: ...` (once per game; the yellow box `cubestuck`). Together with the 2x4 notice below. Not tested in a game; whether the game lets the bot identify or move an item while it is still in the cube is still unknown (a probe was offered).
- YELLOW NOTICE: NO CLEAR 2x4 SPACE (2026-11-16, the user: "if a char doesn't have a clear 2x4 space, there should be a yellow alert on the activity page"; I read 2x4 as the biggest item shape, two cells wide and four high, in a pack that is four rows: a polearm or two-handed weapon). `Item.checkBigSpace` (Misc.js), called at the end of `Town.doChoresRun` after `Layout.tidy`, asks `Storage.Inventory.CanFit` for a 2x4 item and prints `Pack: NOTICE: no clear 2x4 space in the pack: ...` once per game; `noticeFromLog` (fatal.go) now returns a kind (`cubepickup`, `bigspace`) and shows the yellow box (one at a time: a newer notice replaces the older one). Not verified live. If the user meant another size (the cube is 2x2, a stash page 10x10), say so.
- THE CUBE'S ITEMS GET THE PACK'S TREATMENT (2026-11-16, the user: "how about the bot treats items in the cube the same as items in the stash, and IDs them and sorts them accordingly?"; "maybe an item is a keeper without having to ID it, maybe it's not. if it needs to ID it, it will"). `Town.doChoresRun`: after the first round of chores the cube is unloaded into the pack, identified, sold and stashed again, as long as each pass gets something out (up to four passes; it was one extra pass). What needs no ID (a unique or set a keep rule keeps outright: `Misc.keptOutright`) is stashed as it is, the rest is identified first by the existing `Town.identify` and then kept, sold or stashed by its rule; `cubeToStash` no longer stashes what the stash does not want (see the entry below). UNTESTED in a game. POTIONS (the user: "meir is carrying too many potions"): `Chores.tune` sets the pack spares to 0 for a belt of three or more rows and for Quartermaster users (the log says `spares in inventory hp 0 mp 0`), so the 8 Greater potions Meir carries (4 healing, 4 mana, the belt is full with 16) are not planned; the `Town.clearInventory` potion cleanup is meant to drink anything above the buffer, and `.deposit` sends move nothing (items and belt potions are the same before and after every send). FOUND (same evening, from the new `Potions:` log lines: `spares allowed 4/4`): `Chores.tune()`, which sets the pack spares to 0 for a big belt or Quartermaster, was only called by `Chores.needed()`, and only `bots/Follower.js` calls that. A bot leading its own farm (default.dbj) never tuned, so it kept `_DefaultConfig.js`'s 4 healing and 4 mana spares: Meir's 8 Greater potions on top of a full belt. Now `Town.doChoresRun` calls `Chores.tune()` on every chores visit (and `Town.js` includes `Chores.js`, which only the follower script had loaded). The cleanup in `clearInventory` then drinks the spares above the new allowance. UNTESTED in a game: look for `Chores: potions: belt 4 row(s) ... spares in inventory hp 0 mp 0` and `Potions: cleanup: ... spares allowed 0/0` in a leader's log. The `Potions:` lines in `Town.buyPotions` and `clearInventory` stay as diagnostics. The spare allowance also fades with the level (the user: "the only players who should be filling their inventory with spares are low level char, so over time the allowed spares should drift to zero"): `Chores.tune` multiplies the belt's allowance by 1 up to character level 12, by (30 - level) / 18 between 12 and 30, by 0 from level 30; tests in `scripts/jstest/chores_test.js`; the numbers 12 and 30 are mine, the user did not give them. The Quartermaster exception in `Chores.tune` (a bot with Quartermaster kept no spares, on the 2026-11-13 assumption that `.deposit` takes the pack's potions) is REMOVED the same day: the user, "the .deposit function only removes rejuv potions", so healing and mana spares are never taken away and the spares follow the belt alone (3 or more rows: 0).
- UNIDENTIFIED RARES WERE STASHED BY THE CUBE-TO-STASH STEP (2026-11-16, the user: "so it makes no sense for a non ID rare item to be stashed"; "the activity's don't include any rationale for why this was kept"). `Town.cubeToStash` (for InfiniteStash, "add stash pages") moved EVERYTHING `canStash` accepted out of the cube into the stash. The cube also holds what the field put there to make room (`Item.cubeForRoom`, the cursor path): unidentified rares and junk that a town trip identifies or sells. Live 16:51-16:52: `Town: moved Blade Talons / Morning Star / Cutlass from my cube to the stash`. Now it also requires `Town.wantStash(it)`, the test the normal stash step uses (a keeper, or an unidentified item the protection list covers), so the rest stays in the cube for `Town.unloadCube` and the identify and sell steps. Items already stashed that way stay there. Also: the Activity tab's Stashed entries carry the reason (`Item.stashWhy`: `keeper: unique`, `keeper: set`, `keeper: rune`, `kept for a cube recipe`, `not identified, protected: ...`), `parseStashed` (botlog.go) reads it from `Stashed <name> [reason]`. Neither is verified live yet.
- FORCESAVE RAN FIRST, SO A FARM RUN'S LOOT WAS LOST (2026-11-16, found while chasing "Meir does not pick anything up" and "none of these are reflected in the activity tab"). Meir's Mephisto cycle was: new game, `ForceSave` (/save), Mephisto, game exits. A TCP/IP game that exits with quit() does not save (Saver.js), so every pickup of a run was gone: the item log showed 18 uniques and 8 sets kept in 40 pickups, the save had about 7 in the stash, and nothing reached the Activity tab. Cause: my own change that put the farm runs in the user's order (delete and set again, AutoSpec.js) moved them AFTER `ForceSave`, declared earlier by `_DefaultConfig.js`. Fix: AutoSpec.js deletes and sets `Scripts.ForceSave` again, after the goal block, so it is the last script. VERIFIED LIVE 16:50-16:53: `Run done: Mephisto`, `Starting script: ForceSave`, `SAY: /save`, and in the next game `Town: moved Blade Talons from my cube to the stash` (also Morning Star, Cutlass). Also: `Town.unloadCube`'s sibling (the cube to the stash step, Town.js) now prints `Stashed <item>` for an item that the field put into the cube (it did not, only Pack.stashed did), once per item (`Item.stashAnnounced`), so the Activity tab lists them. Not verified live yet: look for `Stashed <name>` after `Town: moved <name> from my cube to the stash`, and the entries on the Activity tab. Also still open: the cube pickup path (`Pack: NOTICE`) has not been needed since.
- PICKUPS STRAIGHT INTO THE CUBE, WITH A YELLOW NOTICE (2026-11-16, the user: "use solution 2" after Meir walked past loot with a 7x4 cube empty; "bots can use their cube to pickup stuff"; "in the event that items begin being placed directly in the cube, put a yellow alert on the activity page"). `Pickit.pickItems`: after the existing cube step (`Item.cubeForRoom`, which only moves the pack's trip items into the cube), if the pickup still does not fit in the pack and `Item.cubeTakesPickup` (a cube, in the field, `Item.cubeRoom`: no recipes, no infinite stash, a free spot) says yes, the bot picks it up (the game puts it on the cursor when the pack has no room) and `Item.cursorToCube` opens the cube, `Storage.Cube.MoveTo`s it, and drops it again if the cube would not take it (never leaves it in the hand). The first time per game it prints `Pack: NOTICE: pickups go straight into the cube ...`; `noticeFromLog`/`raiseNotice` (fatal.go) show it as the yellow box (`#noticebox`, `d.notice`, dismiss: `/api/notice/clear`) on the Activity page; it stops nobody. `Town.unloadCube` takes the trip items back out in town; a pickup that is not a trip item (a charm) would stay in the cube. The red box (both pack and cube full) is unchanged. FIRST LIVE REPORT (15:52): "still not picking anything up and still no warnings": `Item.cubeTakesPickup` went through `Item.cubeRoom`, whose `Config.InfiniteStash && !Town.noMorePages` clause is false-for-the-cube whenever "add stash pages" is on (AutoSpec sets `Config.InfiniteStash = plan.storage.addStashPages`; Meir: add stash pages true), so it said no for every pickup. It now follows `cubeForRoom`: a cube in the pack, in the field, a free spot in it. Not verified live. UNTESTED in a game, and the weak points are the two things I could not test: opening the cube with an item on the cursor, and the cursor handling itself; the log lines to look for are `Cube: picked up X straight into my cube` or `Cube: could not put X into the cube: dropping it again`.
- RED BOX WHEN A BOT CANNOT PICK UP ANYTHING (2026-11-16, the user, about Meir's "Not enough room" lines: "the error should present on the screen in a red box ... but only if the bot truly cannot pick up anything bc all their space is full -- INCLUDING THEIR CUBE"). `Item.reportFull` (Misc.js), called where Pickit prints "Not enough room": not one free cell in the pack, and none in the Horadric Cube (or no cube), at most every ten minutes, prints `Pack: FULL: no room in the pack and none in the cube ...`; `packFullFromLog` (fatal.go) turns it into the Activity page's red box through `raiseFatal("packfull")`, WITHOUT stopping the bots (the stash-full and no-scrolls boxes stop everybody; this one only shows, the user can say if it should stop). A single item that does not fit is not the box. Meir, live 15:22 (pack 34 of 40 cells): eight charms, nine spare potions, two tomes, the cube, a key and Mephisto's Soulstone (his Hell's Forge is done); the cube was already used (`Cube: moved N item(s)`) but only takes trip items (not charms, potions, tomes, quest items), so nothing was left to move. The earlier Activity entry "Pack Full" I made for every such line is removed. Not verified live.
- ACT CHANGES WAIT FOR THE TEAM; CHORE TRIPS ARE FREE (2026-11-16, the user: "seems like the leader should still be waiting for a ready from each teammate, so not sure why a change had to be made here. on an act change -- the gate normally exempt -- why is that?"; "the leader shouldn't have left the town if the team wasn't ready"; "if a party member needs to leave town during chores to a) buy maybe better potions somewhere or b) revive a dead merc or c) to hire a new merc then they can leave town without permission and come back to the original town, then report ready"). SUPERSEDES the two extra waits described just below (`Quester.waitForTeamHandIns` and the one at the end of `act4_hells_forge`): both are REMOVED; they repeated the town gate for a hop into the field and could hang the leader on a mate in another town. The exemption of town-to-town hops from the gate was the user's own words of 2026-11-15 (chore reasons, act change reasons) and was needed because a mate in another town would hold the gate for ever. Now `Team.waitToLeaveTown(targetArea)`: a hop to another act's town is gated like any departure: every mate in the game is in this town and says ready (a mate in the field is called to town; a mate in another town is on its way, a follower always goes to its leader's town; a mate already in the town I am going to does not hold me). A first version ignored mates in another town; the user: "this can't happen. it means the mate wasn't in the leader's town and wasn't ready. see how simple the system is?". `Quester.waitForTeamInTown` stays as the separate wait on the far side. Inside a chore trip (`Team.chore(fn)`, set by `Town.doChores` and `MercHire.maybe`, `Team.onChore`) no hop waits. The gate is called by `Pather.useWaypoint` for town targets (it skipped them before; announcing the leaving is still only for the field) and by `ActTravel.change` (the NPC route never went through it), so `Town.goToTown(act)` and `ActTravel.go` are both covered. Tests: four act-change tests and three chore tests in `team_test.js` (86 pass). NOT verified live; to see it: `Team: waiting in town until X are ready: we leave together.` just before an act change, and no such line before a chore trip.
- TWO PROBLEMS, ONE RULE (2026-11-16, the user: "there are two problems: 1) sometimes the bots do a full clear when they don't have to and 2) when the leader goes to town to finish his quest the followers just stand around and don't do anything to complete the quest"; then "whenever bots are in town -- even if they are followers of a real player -- they should consider what quests they are on and what NPC they should talk to to complete those quests"). (1) The user's `questClearAll` is OFF, so `Attack.clearLevel()` never ran; what took 2.5 minutes after the Hell's Forge smash was `Pather.getWP`, which ALWAYS walks to the area's waypoint and clicks it, even when the character has it (`had` only silences the log): the whole River of Flame back, the team trailing ("fell behind: waiting for them to catch up"). `Quester.clearAndCapture` now skips `getWP` when `getWaypoint(index)` says the character has it. (2) The followers arrived in the Act 4 town 30 s after the leader had left for Act 5 (his loop went straight on to the next quest, an act change, which the ready gate exempts: the user's own rule) and followed him; they never did Cain. `Quester.waitForTeamHandIns` (after every quest the leader finishes while in town: both "is complete" branches of `runQuests`) makes the leader wait for the team's "ready" (`Team.waitToLeaveTown`: a follower is ready only after its own chores and quest talks; a mate in the field is called to town). (3) The rule: the town quest talk no longer depends on the objective or on who leads: `Follower.townQuestTalk`, the follower's four-minute pass and the Chores "quest talk" reason lost their `teamGoal === "Quest"` / `partySync` gates, and `Town.doChores` hands in a waiting reward on every chores visit (a Farm-goal leader included). What a bot knows it must hand in is the game's own "reward waiting" bit (`Quester.questPending`, via `townQuestsOpen`, once per two minutes per quest); a "started" quest has no readable progress (`getQuestProgress` is only ever 0 or 100), so other NPC steps stay in the quest scripts. NOT verified live.
- HELL'S FORGE: TOWN AT ONCE AFTER THE SMASH (2026-11-16, the user: "make sure the quest script takes a portal to town immediately after smashing the soulstone and picking up the items that drop"; after the live result that only Darius and Meir completed it: the smash credited the whole team (bit 1 on all four) but the team never handed in to Cain, because `act4_hells_forge` ran `clearAndCapture(107)` for 2.5 minutes after the smash (13:16:35 -> 13:19:08) and only then went to town, where the leader handed in and went straight on to Act 5). Now: `smashForge` waits 2.5 s for the drops after a successful smash, then restores the weapons and picks up (`Pickit.pickItems`); the River of Flame clear is gone; the town part follows at once (`Town.goToTown(4)` from the field takes the portal and calls the team: "Party: <leader> is going to town: calling the team" is the line), the leader hands in to Cain and then waits for the team (`Team.waitToLeaveTown`); a follower hands in on arrival through `Follower.townQuestTalk` (a pending reward opens it: `Quester.townQuestsOpen`, once per two minutes). Expected log lines, in order: `the Hellforge: quest bits ... -> 0100000000000100`, `Party: ... is going to town: calling the team`, `Talking to Deckard Cain` (leader), `a quest of this town moved on: talking to the quest NPCs` and `quest talk cain round 1: pending true -> false` (each follower), then `Hell's Forge is complete` for each. Barabas, Xena and Sankt still have the reward waiting (`0010000000000010`): any run that has them in the Act 4 town with Cain hands it in. NOT verified live.
- RUNES WENT TO THE STASH BY HAND, NOT WITH `.deposit` (2026-11-16, the user: "why are bots not running .deposit and instead are depositing runes into stash"): `.deposit` was being sent by the chores' stash step (`Town.stash`) and on arrival, but `Pack.freePickupArea` (called from `Follower.js` at the start of a game: "a bot that joins a game should hold no keepers") moves the keepers of the pack to the stash itself, and runes count as keepers: live 13:23:45-53, Xena, Sankt and Darius "Stashed Tal Rune / Ral Rune / Ort Rune / El Rune" with no `.deposit` line before. Now `freePickupArea` calls `Quartermaster.deposit(true)` first (the same call as `Town.stash`), when "Using Quartermaster?" is on; what `.deposit` takes (potions, gems, runes) is gone before the rest is looked at. The other hand-moves into the stash (quest items, the cube, gear, the layout tidy) do not deal with runes. Also seen: `.deposit` often reports the same item count before and after ("70 before, 70 after") because nothing was left in the pack to take by then. UNTESTED in a game.
- HELL'S FORGE, THE EVIDENCE AND THE NEW FLOW (2026-11-16, the user: "on that quest i click on the forge. maybe the issue is something else, like the team already have done this portion of the quest and they just need to talk to an NPC to get credit"). Read from the five saves (raw quest words, quest 27): on NORMAL Barabas, Xena, Sankt and Darius all have `0x8001` (complete) from ONE smash by Darius on 2026-11-14 13:26 (Paladin, Normal): so one smash credits the whole team, as the user said. On NIGHTMARE nobody but Meir (her own earlier run, `0x1001`) has it: Barabas, Xena, Darius `{2,3,5}`, Sankt `{3,5}`, and no reward bit (bit 1) on any of them: the smash has NOT happened on Nightmare, so there is no hand-in waiting. The old log (Darius.log.1) shows what worked: stone placed by a click (`forge.interact()`, no hammer in hand: "Mephisto's Soulstone is on the Hellforge"), the hammer swapped in, and 5 seconds later (the third pass of the hit loop, whose every third pass was ANOTHER `forge.interact()` with the hammer in hand) the bits went `0001010000000000 -> 0100000000000100`. The versions of 2026-11-16 that left the stone in the pack and only hit it (160 hits in five runs) never could work, and the ones that did the old flow failed on Barabas (Nightmare, a Barbarian with a weapon in each hand) - so the difference to the success is the class (dual-wield), the difficulty, or the hit method. `smashForge` is now the old flow again plus: the second weapon (a Barbarian's) taken off for the hits and put back after, a plain left click as a second way, and a line per fourth pass with `me.mode`, `me.attacking`, the forge's mode and distance, the stone and the bits, plus the items on the ground by the forge at the end. Expected log lines: `Mephisto's Soulstone is on the Hellforge`, `the Hellforge Hammer is in my hand, right hand ..., left hand ...`, `the Hellforge hit N: bits ...`, and on success `quest bits A -> B`. TEST PLAN: run with DARIUS leading first (the class that succeeded); if it fails too, the difficulty or the quest state is the cause and the per-pass lines say what the character did. NOT verified.
- STAT BALANCE, DONE THE WAY THE USER ARTICULATED IT (2026-11-16, the user: "fix the stat balance issue -- it means you just didn't implement a system as I had articulated it"). Cause: `AutoStat.getHardStats` ESTIMATED the points spent as the stat the game shows minus what the worn items add; it was 11 strength, 6 dexterity and 8 energy too high on Barabas (95/75/222/18 where the save said 84/69/222/10), so the equal order stopped adding strength early. Now `AutoStat` counts the real points: Qolbot writes the save's base stats into the plan (`hardStats` = strength, dexterity, vitality, energy, and `hardStatsAt`, `specplan.go`), `AutoStat` counts every point it spends and keeps the count in `data/<profile>.hard.json` (a later game of the same run starts from it; a newer plan, i.e. Qolbot restarted and re-read the save, wins over an older record), `Respec` sets it back to the class base after `.reset stats`, and the old estimate stays only as the fallback (and when the record does not fit the character). `scripts/jstest/autostat_test.js` simulates a Barbarian in gear with 300 points: new code 125/125/125, the old estimate comes out short (the live bug reproduced). Respec's own judgement (`judgeStats`) uses the same exact counts. UNTESTED in a game; to see it, clear a character's `statsSpec` in `data/<profile>.respec.json` and restart, then read the save.
- GAMBLING COUNTS THE PACK AND THE STASH (2026-11-16, the user: "gambling not triggering anymore should be based on the total gold in the stash and pack together, not just the pack"): `Town.needGamble` now compares pack + stash gold with `Config.GambleGoldStart` (1,000,000), `Town.gamble` takes what it will spend (everything above `GambleGoldStop`, 500,000, in total) out of the stash first (`Gold.topUp`), loops while pack + stash is at least GambleGoldStop and the pack has gold, and stops when a whole pass bought nothing; the chores' last step stashes what is left. The thresholds themselves are unchanged: no character has 1,000,000 yet (the richest, Meir, 653,000 in the stash), so nothing will gamble until one does. UNTESTED in a game.
- \"AutoMule is not defined\" (2026-11-16 12:25 and 12:35, Sankt and Meir, `Error in TownChicken.js (pickit.js line 268)`): when a bot cannot make room (the cube is full too and the one-trip-a-minute limit holds), `Pickit.pickItems` reached kolbot's mule check, `AutoMule.getInfo()`, and AutoMule is not loaded here (no mule), so the script threw. Now guarded with `typeof AutoMule === \"object\"`. It surfaced because the cube-first change sends full packs down that path. UNTESTED in a game.
- NO ACTIVITY CARD FOR CUBE STORAGE (2026-11-16, the user: \"remove the 'mvoed from cube' alerts on activity log\"): the bot's line \"Cube: moved N item(s) from my pack into my cube to make room ...\" began with \"Cube: \", which `reCubed` reads as an item the cube made, so every pack-to-cube move became a card. `activity.go` now skips \"Cube: moved ...\" lines; real recipes (\"Cube: <item>\") still make cards (`TestCubeStorageLineIsNotAnActivityCard`). Built; deploy waits for a moment with no game running (the server cannot be restarted under a running game).
- THE NEW STAT RULES RAN LIVE (2026-11-16 12:10-12:18, the user chose to let Auto Respec reshape all five): VERIFIED: the respec (`.reset stats`) and the one-point-at-a-time spending (`AutoStat: Using 1 stat points in ...`) ran for all five with no disconnect, about a minute and a half for 300 points (Meir's 405 took longer: the leader waited in town for her Quick Travel portal meanwhile, as the no-time-limit rule says). Saved results: Barabas 113/118/144/10, Xena 122/117/121/25, Darius 115/125/125/15, Sankt 163/25/167/25, Meir 151/25/150/147 (12 unspent when read). Findings: (1) Auto Respec judges a character ONCE per build (`data/<profile>.respec.json` `statsSpec`): only Xena and Darius were judged at first; Barabas (10:50 today), Sankt (Nov 13) and Meir (Nov 14) were skipped until their `statsSpec`/`statsAt`/`statsMisplaced` keys were cleared and the team restarted. To reshape a character again, clear those keys. (2) The results are balanced but not exactly equal (Barabas vit 144 against str 113): AutoStat's own measure of a stat (`getHardStats`) most likely counts bonuses from worn gear, so characters in gear with +str/dex/vit end up with fewer hard points in those stats. Not fixed; Barabas's plan is 134/133/133, so he is not judged misplaced again (31 above vit is under the threshold).
- STAT RULES AND THE RESPEC THAT UNDID BARABAS'S STATS (2026-11-16). The user had set Barabas's stats by hand (145 str / 141 dex / 146 vit / 18 energy, 0 unspent) so he could wear good items. At the start of run 7 (10:50:17) Auto Respec judged 124 of 365 points "above the plan" (the old Whirlwind order: strength 95, dexterity 75, the rest vitality: plan 95/75/270/10), did `.reset stats` (40/26/25/18, 300 unspent) and respent by the template: 84/69/222/10. The user: "it did undo the changes i had made to barabas". That old order also explains why his gear was poor: a Barbarian with 95 strength cannot wear much of what drops. New rules from the user: melee fighters (Barbarian Whirlwind and Frenzy, Paladin Zeal and Smiter, Assassin Kick, Druid Fury) keep str, dex and vit equal, one point at a time, until str and dex are 180 ("enough for an Elite Monarch", 156 strength), then all points to vit; Summon Druid and Summon Necromancer: str and vit only, equal to 180, then vit. The templates generate the order in a loop (targets 21..180 by one); `plan_test.go` accepts that form. "Ranged melee" (the user, 2026-11-16: a category with the same rules as melee): Javazon, Throw Barbarian and Bowazon now use the melee order (balanced str/dex/vit to 180, then vit); Bowazon first stayed out (bows scale with dexterity only) and the user said "bowazons still need str to be able to wear good gear". CASTERS (the user, 2026-11-16: "let's refer to 'every other build' as casters, and I'm guessing it should be equal str/eng/vit until 180"): Sorceress x5, Necromancer Bone and Poison Nova, Trapsin, Fire and Wind Druid and Hammerdin now keep strength, energy and vitality equal, one point at a time, to 180, then all vitality; no dexterity (before: vitality first, strength 90-100, energy only for the Hammerdin). A risk worth watching: on 2026-11-13 energy lines were taken out of the caster templates because they put 50 points into energy and left Sankt with 187 life at level 25; equal vitality is not that, but a caster now has less life at a given level than with the vitality-first orders. `plan.go`'s custom builds raise strength to 180. Worked out on paper with Respec's own arithmetic: Barabas's old 365 points plan out at about 147/147/146, so a respec would call them fine. Strength reaches 156 at about level 79 under the equal rule. UNTESTED in a game: a 300-point respend now runs one point at a time (`AutoStat.useStats`, 150 ms + ping each), about a minute and a half, once; watch for a disconnect. Auto Respec still takes back stat points above the plan whenever a character has not been judged for its build: the user was told to uncheck AutoRespec once the stats are as wanted.

## Live verification ledger (read this BEFORE treating a report as a new bug)

A change is "Verified" only when a log line from a real run shows it; quote the line. Everything else is "Not verified". When a problem is reported, look here first: if a row covers it, the earlier change did not hold, and the row says what to check. One behavior change per live run; write the expected log line here before the run.

| Behavior (entry below) | Status | Evidence / what to look for |

| "noact 4" report, the team goes back for Mephisto (A TEAMMATE WITHOUT ACT 4) | Verified 2026-11-15 | 20:52:55 Darius's report, 20:53:11 "Darius cannot get into Act 4", 20:59:09 Mephisto dead, Act 4 open for Darius. |

The new in-session step has not run. Look for `a quest of this town moved on: talking to the quest NPCs` and the quest "complete" line for each follower. |
| Cube before a town trip | PARTLY verified 2026-11-16 (the pickup path), the leader's quest path fixed but not run | Verified: Meir 10:33:06 and 10:33:20 `Cube: moved 2 item(s) from my pack into my cube to make room`. Gap seen live 10:33:39: Barabas's trip came from `Quester.tidyUp`, which did not try the cube; it does now (expected line `Inventory is full: the cube took what a town trip would clear, no trip yet.`). Still to see: `Town: took N of M item(s) out of my cube` at the next visit. History: 2026-11-16 09:34:09: Barabas's full-pack trip came from `Quester.tidyUp` ("Inventory is full, going to town to clear it", after picking up the Hellforge Hammer), a second path the cube change does not cover (only `Pickit.js`'s "Trying to make room" path does). To do after the baseline: `Quester.tidyUp` tries `Item.cubeForRoom` first. Look for `Cube: moved N item(s)` before a trip, and `Town: took N of M item(s) out of my cube`. |
| New stash page = /insertpage, /swappage 1, click the first-page button at 159,402 (measured on a bot window) | FAILED 2026-11-16 10:14 (the click version: the team was stopped as "stash full"); /page 1 version not verified | Look for `Stash: clicking the first page button at 193,459`; a `page in view is not empty` warning means the click missed. |

| "I'm playing" mode: followers' town quest talks, lone quest items left for the player | Not verified | Needs a person leading. |
| Hell's Forge: the smash, the retry, the team credit | Not verified (failed 2026-11-16 09:33-09:37) | Failed live: Barabas put the Soulstone ON the forge (it left his pack) and hit it 10 times, bits `0001010000000000` before and after; the script returned true, the Quester logged "done with Hell's Forge" and the team went on to Terror's End. Xena, Sankt and Darius stayed open (the user: one hammer drops, ONE smash credits the team; "the bots have to click the forge several times with the hammer equipped and a stone in inventory"). 2026-11-16 09:55-09:56 second failure: 40 plain CLICKS (`forge.interact()`) with the hammer in hand: the stone left the pack by click 5 (a click puts it down), bits unchanged. The user: "as long as the stone is in inventory and you attack the forge with the hammer in hand, it will work -- you just sometimes have to hit it a few times". Changes: `smashForge` keeps the stone in the pack, equips the hammer, ATTACKS the forge (`Skill.cast(0, 1, forge)`: normal attack with Shift, up to 40 hits, never a click) and stops at once if the stone leaves the pack; `Quester.runQuests` retries a script that changed nothing in the game (3 attempts, then on to the next quest, back to it on the next pass: the user, "retry, then move on"); `cainSoulstone` gives a new stone even with the hammer in the pack and runs before the trip. Expected: `Mephisto's Soulstone is in my pack for the Hellforge`, `the Hellforge Hammer is in my hand, the Soulstone is in my pack`, `the Hellforge hit N: bits ...` every fifth hit with the stone still `in my pack`, `the Hellforge answered on hit N`; bad signs: `the attack on the Hellforge did not go out`, `the Soulstone left my pack on hit N`, `quest bits A -> B` with B different, then `Hell's Forge is complete` for Barabas and (after the town talks) the others. On failure: `the quest is unchanged in the game after attempt 1 of 3: trying again`. |

Now `Follower`: the drop does not throw while `this.errand` is set, and a repeated "portal <place>" is ignored while one is queued or being made; `QuickTravel` asks again every 30 s. Look for `still waiting in town for Meir's portal` followed by her announcement and `took Meir's portal`. |
| Hellforge: which way of hitting the forge the game accepts | NOT FOUND (3 attempts x 40 hits, 2026-11-16 10:22-10:28, run 5) | With the stone in the pack and the hammer in hand, 40 `Skill.cast(0, 1, forge)` (normal attack + Shift) from 3 away changed nothing, three attempts in a row; the screen of Barabas's window was captured (`DISPLAY=:2`, raised with a raw-X helper) and shows him 3 units from the forge but not whether the swings land. The next run tries FOUR ways in turn, ten hits each, each named in the log: `the Hellforge, method N of 4: <name>`; `the Hellforge answered on hit N, method M: <name>` names the one that works. If none does, the next thing to look at is the screen during method 3 or 4. |
| Hellforge: stones | Cain refuses a new Soulstone (2026-11-16 10:35:02, 10:35:36: "no Soulstone from him") | Every attempt so far put a stone on the forge or lost it (the old code, and method 4's click), and the quest bits moved once from `0001010000000000` to `0011010000000000` after the first attempt. The team churned (town, Cain, portal, "no stone") until stopped. Before any more attempts: find out the forge's real state by hand (is a stone on it?) and do not run the retry loop again, it uses stones. |
