## Recent changes, UNTESTED in a real game

- CUBE CARDS SHOW STATS (2026-11-15, the user: "when a Cube: ___ activity is logged make it clickable so I see the stats"): `Cubing.doCubing` sends `Misc.logItem("Cubed", item)` right before its "Cube: X" line; botlog.go accepts the "Cubed" title (`itemActions`) and `descFits` gives it to the "cube" entry; the page strips "Cubed" from the dialog title. The card was already clickable but had no stats. Go tests pass. UNTESTED in a game.
- EVERY CROSSING IS A TEAM CROSSING (2026-11-15, the user: "every transition between the zones and seams of the game are team crossings"). Live 20:25: after Mephisto the followers' red-portal errand began with a trip to town, which became a party trip; the town order dropped the errand; Barabas went through the red portal alone; the followers did not see the red portal as the way to the Pandemonium Fortress ("no way"), went to town and bounced between town and the lair (only Meir, who had the Act 4 waypoint, got there). Changes:
  - `Pather.usePortal`: the leader in the field walks up to a portal that is not a town portal (object 59) (the red portal, the Tristram portal at the Cairn Stones in the Stony Field: Tristram.js and journeyTo both take it by `Pather.usePortal(38)`, and followers find it by its target area in `checkExit`; the Arcane Sanctuary, the Worldstone Chamber, the Cow Level) and waits for the team there (`Team.gather`, near), as exits and field waypoints already did.
  - `Pather.useUnit`: the same for stairs and other objects that change the area.
  - The red portal (`QuestScripts` act 3 end): the team is gathered at the portal; later changed to the leader going first (see A TEAMMATE WITHOUT ACT 4).
  - `Follower`: "redportal" is taken by every follower still in Act 3 (not only those without Act 4); from Level 3 it goes straight to the portal, no trip to town; `checkExit` knows Level 3 -> Pandemonium Fortress (the red portal), so a follower left behind follows the leader through it.
  - Not changed: the walk-through exits, field waypoints and the Durance stairs already gathered the team before crossing.
  JS tests pass; UNTESTED in a game.
- ALL GOLD TO THE STASH ON EVERY CHORES VISIT (2026-11-15, the user: "xena sold an item that did not show the amount sold for"; "none of the bots are putting gold into stash gold"; "having any gold on their person seems meaningless -- make them stash all their gold every time they do chores"). Xena sold items for 0 gold at her cap (570,000 and 580,000); four of five bots had 0 in the stash, because `_DefaultConfig.js` sets `Config.StashGold = 1000000`, more than they can carry. Now `Town.stashGoldAt()` is 1: `needStash` and `stash` put all the pack's gold in the stash on every visit (Config.StashGold is not used). The shops take only the pack's gold, so `Town.doChores` first takes up to 100,000 out of the stash (`Gold.topUp`; before, only followers took 2,000); what is left goes back at the end. UNTESTED in a game.
- A follower's "task dropped" is no longer an Activity problem entry (`isProblemLine`): it is the drop working as meant.
- A TEAMMATE WITHOUT ACT 4 (2026-11-15, the user: "either way the team has to do mephisto again to go thru the red portal"). Live 20:41: Barabas started the game in Act 4; Darius had never been through the red portal and tried to go alone from the Act 3 town every 20 s; the town gate held him; the leader waited for him in Act 4 for good. Now: a follower without Act 4 goes for the red portal only from Durance Level 2 or 3 (the team is there), never alone from town: it waits in town and says "noact 4" on the message log (the user: "i don't think the leader has to wait. the followers can report it as such in the internal chat"). The leader's town gate does not wait for a mate that said so and is still in Act 3 (`Team.noActMates`); before its next Act 4 or 5 quest any such mate means the whole team goes back (`Quester.act3_guardian`: Mephisto, then the red portal crossing, leader last). Once per game (`act4TeamTried`). A gate exemption for a lone trip was tried and taken back out (it would have sent Darius into the Durance alone). UNTESTED in a game.
  - Live 20:52-21:00 (the first run with it): Darius said "noact 4" at 20:52:55, Barabas read it at 20:53:11 and took the team back (Meir's Quick Travel portal to Mephisto, all out within 4 s), Mephisto died 20:59:09, and at 21:00:03-09 all four followers went through the red portal first: Act 4 is now open for Darius. FAULT: each follower went straight back to Act 3 two seconds later (the leader was still in the lair: "Going to my leader's town"), and the leader crossed alone after the one timer. Changed (the user: "if the entire team is standing by the red portal in preparation for the crossing, then all of them should arrive within a few seconds on the other side"; "make it so when they do the crossing the leader goes first"; "then he's in pandemonium fortress and they stay there when they arrive"): the team is gathered at the portal, the leader writes the order, says "redportal" and goes through FIRST; the followers go right after it and stay with it in the fortress (the leader waits there: `arrivedInAct`). The leader-last loop, `Team.goingLast` and `Follower.acrossFirst` (a first fix) are gone. UNTESTED in a game.
- FOLLOWERS TALK TO THE QUEST NPCS BEFORE THEY SAY READY (2026-11-15, the user: "only barabas got quest credit, but if the whole team had talked to tyrael they all would have gotten it"). Live 21:21:52-21:23:01: Izual died with the whole team there, the party trip took everyone to town, Barabas talked to Tyrael (Fallen Angel complete for him; Meir had it already), and Xena, Sankt and Darius stood idle in town and left with him: Fallen Angel stayed "pending" for them. A party trip's chores (TownChicken -> Town.doChores) have no quest talks; the Follower's own chores do, but only on its own visits or every 4 minutes. Now `Follower.townQuestTalk`: a follower idle in town with the leader first talks to the quest NPCs (`Quester.runQuests(true)` for started quests, `Quester.questNpcs`) whenever a quest of this town moved on (`Quester.townQuestsOpen`), busy meanwhile (`Team.markBusy`), so the leader waits for it at the town gate. UNTESTED in a game.
- "I'M PLAYING" MODE AND QUESTS (2026-11-15, the user: "for real player mode, the bots just need to always pick up quest items (and use them if they can) and when in town talk to the proper NPCs. beyond that, the real player will lead them"). Already so: followers pick up quest items (Quest.nip), use them (`QuestItems.useAll` every 10 s) and see quest NPCs on every town visit (`Quester.questNpcs`). Changed: the town part of started quests (`Quester.runQuests(true)`, e.g. the Kashya hand-in) ran only when the Objective (hidden in that mode) was Quest; now also whenever the plan's partySync is off (a person leads). UNTESTED in a game.
- BOTS DID NOT FOLLOW A WAYPOINT WHEN YOU PLAY (2026-11-17, the user: "I went into the field via a waypoint and the bots did not come"). Darius and Meir logged "the leader's last order is "town": waiting in town" every 10 s. `data/order.json` held a team order "town" of Barabas from 14:21 (an earlier bot-led game); a TCP/IP game has an empty game name, so the old file's `game: ""` matched "this game" and `Team.currentOrder` returned it. With a real player leading nobody writes orders, so the stale one held every follower in town for good. Now `Team.currentOrder` returns null unless `Team.syncOn()`. Also (the user asked that no order of another game may affect a bot): `Team.currentOrder` drops any order whose `at` is older than this game's start (`Date.now() - (getTickCount() - me.gamestarttime)`, 15 s slack), so an older game's order never counts, bot-led or not. Unit-tested; UNTESTED in a game, and the running bots have the old code until the next game.
- FOLLOWERS PICK UP SALE ITEMS ONLY IN "I'M PLAYING" MODE TOO (2026-11-17, the user: "in real player mode, bots should only pick sale items not potentially keepers"). `specplan.go` set `plan.noPickup` (which sets `Pickit.questOnly`: quest consumables and the GoldSell.nip sale items, nothing that might be a keeper) only when you were not playing, so with you playing the followers picked up the baseline keeper lists as well. Now every follower has it; the leader bot (not playing) keeps its own lists. Go tests pass; UNTESTED in a game (needs `./build.sh` and a new game).
- APART FROM YOU FOR X SECONDS: TOWN (2026-11-17, the user: "in real player mode, if the bots are separated from the leader for X seconds they tele to town ... and they emit some kind of complaint"). Follower.js main loop: with a person leading (`!Team.syncOn()`), a bot in the field whose leader is in another area or more than 60 units away for `Team.limits.sync` (Settings > Behavior > Auto Regroup, 30 s) calls `Town.goToTown()` and reports an Activity problem "I lost you: going to town to meet up." (`this.apartSince`, shares the 30 s `lostAt` throttle with the older "no way to the leader" trip). The usual following takes it on from town. Not unit-tested (the Follower main loop has no harness); UNTESTED in a game. Watch that 30 s is not too short for a long walk after you teleport: the timer is the one setting.
- LONE QUEST ITEMS ARE YOURS (2026-11-15, the user: "if only one item drops, then I need to be the one to pick it up ... let's suppose the Gidbinn for example only drops one knife, i don't want the bots to pick it up"). With a person leading (partySync off), a follower leaves a quest item (`QuestItems.keep`) on the ground unless another copy lies there too (`Pickit.leaveForPlayer`): with one copy per player each bot takes one and the last is yours. Two bots looking at the same moment could both take one. UNTESTED in a game.
- THE CUBE BEFORE A TOWN TRIP (2026-11-15, the user: "make it so trips to town for reason of full inventory happens when bots have maxed out their cube also"). In the field, when an item does not fit, `Item.cubeForRoom` moves what a town trip would get rid of (`Item.tripItem`: to identify, junk to sell, keepers to stash; never quest items, gold, potions, scrolls, charms or tomes) into the Horadric Cube until it fits; only then the trip. In town, `Town.unloadCube` (start of `doChores`) takes those items back into the pack for identify, sale and stash, and again after the stash for what did not fit at first. This reverses the earlier "NOT done on purpose" choice (the cube's contents would be part of a recipe): `Cubing.emptyCube` already empties the cube before every transmute. Written after the 20:4x test run started, so that run does not have it. UNTESTED in a game.
- THE PORTAL ANNOUNCEMENT IS ALWAYS SAID (2026-11-15, the user: "still Meir is not reporting when she drops portals for quick travel"). It was a "task" line, silent with "Bots report when they engage in tasks" unchecked (the user's setting). `Speech.answers.portalOpen` is now kind "announce": always said, as a bubble over her head. speech_test.js updated. UNTESTED in a game.
- A NEW STASH PAGE IS PAGE 1 (2026-11-15, the user: "barabas seems to be making new stash pages without filling up the ones he has"; "/insertpage adds a page after the one you're looking at"; "you have to /insertpage then /swappage 1 then click back to page one", with a screenshot for the button). His save at 20:56: page 1 full, pages 2-6 with 1-2 items each. The stash opens on page 1; "/insertpage" put the new page after it as page 2, the item went there, and the next visit found page 1 full again. Now `Cubing.newStashPage`: "/insertpage", "/swappage 1" (the empty page becomes page 1, the full one page 2), then `stashFirstPage` clicks PlugY's first-page button (`sendClick(193, 459)` on 800x600, worked out from the screenshot: 588,827 in a 1920x1080 picture of a 1068x600 game; a wider game adds (width - 800) / 2, `me.screensize` is logged to check what it holds). A warning is logged when the page in view is not empty afterwards. A walk through all the pages and a plain "/insertpage" were tried first and taken back out. UNTESTED in a game. LIVE 2026-11-16 10:14:05 (run 4): the coordinate click at 193,459 missed (`me.screensize` read 0: the size of the bots' window is not known), the page in view was the full one, the item did not fit, `stashFullStop` stopped the whole team ("A stash is full ... no page can be added"). Now the way back to page 1 is PlugY's own `/page 1`: `/page 1`, `/insertpage`, `/swappage 1`, `/page 1`, then a check that the page in view is empty (log lines `Stash: a new page is page 1 and in view.` or `the page in view is not empty: trying the first page button` / `still not empty`); the click stays as a last resort only. Still not seen working in a game. 2026-11-16 (the user: "writing /page 1 won't move the current view to page 1, the only way to do it is to click the back button"): the `/page 1` version is gone; the click is back, now with a MEASURED position: a screenshot of Darius's stash on the bots' hidden display (`DISPLAY=:2`, 800x600 Xvfb, the game window 640x480 at +3+29, `me.screensize` 0 = 640x480) shows the first-page button centred at 162,431 on the display = 159,402 in the game's coordinates (my 193,459 and 142,499 were guesses from the user's screenshot at the wrong scale). `Cubing.stashFirstPage` clicks 159,402 (142,499 only when `me.screensize` is 1, a guess). Expected log lines: `Stash: clicking the first page button at 159,402 (screensize 0).` then `Stash: a new page is page 1 and in view.`
- THE DROP IS TOTAL, READY MEANS READY, THE MAKER OBEYS THE LAST ORDER (2026-11-15, after the live run 20:04-20:12, Barabas leading; the user agreed to all three). Live: after a trip Sankt's old field task ran on in town for 30 s (20:10:26-57; the first version only ended walks and fights between steps), he had said "ready" from the town thread anyway, Barabas sent the team out through Meir's portal, Sankt missed it, Meir (the maker) waited for him to go out, the regroup brought everybody back to town and Meir waited for ever while Barabas waited for her "ready" (deadlock, bots stopped 20:12). (1) Follower.js: while `dropTask` is set, `delay()` (wrapped) and `Pather.interrupt` throw `dropError`; the main loop's pass runs in a try and catches it (other errors are thrown on as before), so everything the main script was doing is abandoned and the next pass follows the newest order. (2) `PartySync.barrier`: a follower says "chores" instead of "ready" (its main loop says ready when idle in town, `Team.markReady`); TownChicken also says "chores" when a follower's town trip starts, so an older "ready" does not count. (3) `Team.sendOutFirst`: a follower that made the portal stops waiting when the leader's newest order is no longer "out", and does not go out (`Pather.usePortal` returns false). Tests: 2 changed, 1 new; 111 pass. UNTESTED in a game.
- GOALS ERASED IN TOWN, NOBODY LEAVES TOWN UNTIL THE LEADER SAYS (2026-11-15, after the live run 19:38-19:51, Barabas leading; the user: "they erase their goals when they go to town, and nobody can leave again until the leader says"). Live: the leader's town order paused a follower's main script but did not end its task: Sankt finished his own old trip and took his portal back out while the order was "town" (19:48:34); Meir and Xena walked on toward Travincal spots from Kurast Docks, missed the leader's "out", and Xena walked out of town alone. (1) TownChicken's `togglePause` says `droptask` when it resumes the main script in town after pausing it in the field; Follower.js sets `dropTask`, `Pather.interrupt` returns it, so `Pather.moveTo` and `Attack.clear` end at once; the main loop's next pass clears it. (2) `Team.mayLeaveTown(area)`, asked by `Pather.moveToExit`, `useWaypoint` and `usePortal` in town: a follower goes toward the field only when the leader's newest order is "out" or an errand to it (another town is free; the leader and bots without party sync are free). 1 new test (110 pass). UNTESTED in a game. Not done: an "out" order is still read only by the follower's main loop.
- THE LAST ORDER TRUMPS ALL (2026-11-15, after the live split 17:33-17:34: Xena in town, Barabas and Sankt alone in Travincal; the user: "yes, last order trumps all"). Three changes. (1) The order record `data/order.json` (Team.order / Team.currentOrder, rule R10 in docs/TEAM_SYNC.md): the leader writes every order there (out by exit/waypoint/portal/walk, town, stay, and an errand for one bot); every follower decision reads the newest order (Team.leavingCall, Team.returnCall, PartySync.townOrder in TownChicken, and the Follower in town does not leave toward the field while the newest order is "town" or "stay"). A follower carries out a "town" order once it is in the field even if it was in town or on a waypoint when the order came (that was the "answered while loading" of Barabas and Sankt). `Pather.usePortal` records "out" for every portal the leader takes out of town (its own too); TownChicken records "out" if the leader is out of town while the order is still "town". The 20 s / 30 s lifetimes of "leaving" and "proceed" are gone: a newer order replaces an older one. (2) `PartySync.session` = `me.gamestarttime` (one per bot, all threads), and `PartySync.ack` never answers the bot's own call (Xena's town chicken thread answered her own call at 17:34:07 and every later call was dropped by the mates). (3) `Quester.clearAndCapture` no longer ends with `Town.doChores()` (the leader went to town 3 s after leading the team into Travincal). Tests: team_test and partysync_test updated, 9 new; all 109 pass. UNTESTED in a game.
- ONLY THE LEADER GIVES ORDERS (2026-11-15 after the live mess 17:01-17:06, user: "the leader alone issues orders. nobody else does"; "the person who leaves the field can do so without permission. the leader then instructs the remaining party members to take that bots portal back to town"; a follower in town never calls anybody). Rule R10 in docs/TEAM_SYNC.md. Changes: (1) `PartySync.call` from a follower no longer writes `data/partycall.json` or says `chickentown`: it writes `data/<profile>.wenttown.json` ("Party: X went to town: telling the leader."), sets `currentId` and waits in town as before (barrier, `stayInTown`, follows the leader). (2) The leader's TownChicken thread, in the field, reads those reports every 1 s (`PartySync.wentTown`: fresh, under a minute; the mate's current session; once each), calls the team with `PartySync.call(me.area, urgent, mateName)` (the call's new `portal` field) and goes to town itself through `Town.visitTown`, taking that mate's portal. (3) A bot answering a call prefers the named portal (`Pather.preferPortalOf` in `useTownPortalNear`; any party portal close by if that one is not, else its own). (4) `Team.waitToLeaveTown`: only the leader calls mates in the field to town; a follower at its gate just waits. (5) `Team.sendOutFirst`: a follower that made the portal says no `proceed` and no "telling them again"; it only waits to go last ("waiting for X to go out first"). The other orders were already leader-only (`announceLeaving`, `escalate`, the regroup, `Waypoints.sendTeam`, the `proceed` in `Pather.usePortal` and in the `useWaypoint` wrapper, `Team.command`). Then the user's three answers on the open parts (same day): "what you refer to as the town gate is really just not doing anything until the leader has told a bot to do it ... the leader would not have asked her to do this if the party wasn't all ready"; "the leader is excempt from being ready himself. if he gives an order it means that he was ready"; "darius should not have been instructed to leave town if Meir's portal hadn't been created yet". So (6) `Team.waitToLeaveTown` is the leader's gate only: a follower returns at once (followers still say ready while idle in town, `Team.markReady`, Follower.js:438). (7) `QuickTravel.portal`: no 150 s give-up; the leader waits in town until the Sorceress (or waypoint helper) answers or leaves the game, and logs "still waiting in town for X's portal" every 30 s. Tests: partysync_test 3 new, team_test 1 new and 1 replaced (the old "EVERY bot passes the gate"); all 102 pass. UNTESTED in a game.
- Gate table from the user's memory (2026-11-12): act 2 behind quests from the Harem on (Palace Cellar 52-54 and the Arcane Sanctuary: Tainted Sun; Canyon: Arcane Sanctuary), act 3 Durance of Hate 100-102 (Khalim's Will), act 4 none, act 5 Worldstone Keep 128-132 (Rite of Passage, 39) and Nihlathak's temple 121-124 (Prison of Ice, 37; which quest is a guess). Boss portals use it too: `QuickTravel.portal` does not ask for a portal to a gated place ("walking"). UNTESTED.
- Waypoints behind quest gates are not tried (2026-11-12, user): `Waypoints.gates` (area -> quest): Palace Cellar 1 and the Arcane Sanctuary need Tainted Sun (11), the Canyon of the Magi needs the Arcane Sanctuary quest (12), Durance of Hate needs Khalim's Will (18) - the first three from what the logs showed (portals refused at 6 quests done, accepted at 7; the Canyon's waypoint was taken right after quest 12), the last from the game's rules, not seen. Every bot writes the quests it has done (`data/wpquests-<name>.json`, at most once a minute with its waypoints), so `Waypoints.receivers` leaves out a bot that has not met the gate, and the leader does not go to a place it may not enter (`gated`). Places not listed are still learned from a refused portal (`rememberBlocked`). UNTESTED live; change the table if a place turns out different.
- Act 2 findings (2026-11-12): (1) the Harem cannot be walked (bots stand at 10005,5043 in Harem Level 1, no path), so with the Palace Cellar waypoint known the Arcane Sanctuary quest goes around it (`Waypoints.has(52)`); (2) the Arcane Sanctuary cannot be walked without Teleport, so `act2_arcane_sanctuary` now uses Quick Travel to the Summoner (`QuickTravel.portal("summoner", 74)`, then `Attack.clear(15, 0, 250)`), the old walk stays as the fallback; (3) Jerhyn is talked to once per game by every bot (`permissions.p1`, QuestNpcs), not shown to be needed; (4) stuck leader: `TownChicken` makes the LEADER BOT leave the game after 2.5 minutes without moving outside town (the first version also hit every follower, because their Config.Leader is empty in that thread: it checks the Plan goal now); (5) quest items the quests need (QuestItems.keep: Staff of Kings, Amulet of the Viper, Horadric Staff, Khalim's parts ...) count as the bot's own like tomes, keys and the cube (`Item.userOwned`): never stashed or sold, so the Horadric Staff is in the pack for Tal Rasha's tomb. The assembly (`cubeAndCreate(91, 92, 521)`) and the socket (`keyAndKeyhole(152, 91)`) were already in the quest scripts. UNTESTED.
- Waypoint sync as a whole-team matrix (2026-11-12): for every area of every act the leader has opened, `Waypoints.having` (givers) and `Waypoints.receivers` (bots that lack it AND have reached its act: `canReach`, from the act town's waypoint in their list) are worked out; the log says "X can give it to A+B". `waitForTeam` waits for the receivers only. A portal that does not work costs at most 40 s (90 s for Quick Travel bosses) and is REMEMBERED per leader and quest count (`data/wpblocked-<leader>.json`, `rememberBlocked`/`blockedNow`) until the leader has done another quest: Darius could not enter Palace Cellar 1, the Arcane Sanctuary or the Canyon of the Magi by portal even with Radament done (17:13), the reason is not known (probably quest gates: the Canyon opens after the Summoner), so nothing is guessed (`Waypoints.gates` is empty). `Layout.apply` stashes a blocker in town when the pack is full (Darius: a Large Charm sat on the cube's cell). Log watcher: the harmless "<Class>.<Name>.js not found!" line is printed at every start.
- A follower never walks to a waypoint it lacks (2026-11-12, user: "hoofing it from another waypoint defeats the purpose of waypoint sharing"): `Pather.useWaypoint(area, true)` falls back to `getWP`, which walks there from the nearest waypoint the bot has. `Follower.lacksWaypoint(area)` now keeps both uses (town to the leader's field area, and field to the leader's area) to waypoints the bot actually has; otherwise it takes the leader's/party's portal or waits in town and asks for one. UNTESTED.
- Waypoint sharing and places the game may not let a bot into (user: e.g. the Arcane Sanctuary before its quest): `Waypoints.waitForTeam` gives up after 25 s if none of the bots that lack the waypoint is in the leader's area ("nobody ... has come"), and `QuickTravel.portal` already gives up on a portal that does not work (about 20 s) and does not ask for it again that game. So a closed zone costs about half a minute, not the whole 45 s wait or a loop. A follower's waypoint pickup now prints "Waypoint collected: <area>" too (Activity entry for every bot) and saves its waypoint list at once. Sharing is paused for the regroup while the leader does it (`wpsync1`/`wpsync0` messages to TownChicken). Why some followers arrive later than the leader: they take the portal to the PREVIOUS stop, find the leader gone, then try the waypoint they lack and walk from the nearest one they have (Barabas and Sankt walked Dry Hills to Far Oasis, 40-60 s). UNTESTED.
- Waypoint sync also on arriving in each new act (2026-11-12, user): `Waypoints.syncedActs` replaces the once-per-game flag; `Quester.arrivedInAct(act)` (after the act trip in `leaveAct`, after the red portal into act 4 and after Tyrael into act 5) takes the new town's waypoint (`Waypoints.touchTown`) and runs `Waypoints.sync` for that act. Quest completions of every bot reach the Activity list: `questwatch.go` watches each character's save and adds an entry for a quest that became complete in it (the leader's own line is not doubled). UNTESTED live.
- Waypoint sharing only when a game starts (2026-11-12, user): the sweep before an act change (`Quester.collectWaypoints`) is gone, together with its calls before Mephisto, Diablo and Baal. What stays: `Waypoints.sync` once at the start of a game, and the leader taking the waypoint of an area it walks into (`captureWaypoint`). Going UP an act always uses the act's NPC (`ActTravel.go`: Warriv, Meshif, Tyrael ..., never the waypoint), for the leader and the followers; going down still uses the waypoint. UNTESTED beyond act 2.
- Sisters to the Slaughter has an NPC now (2026-11-12, user's idea): `QuestTable.json` quest 6 lists `warriv`. The game keeps the quest with its reward pending until Warriv has been talked to (a bot that was in range when Andariel died has it pending but never complete: saves showed `801e` for Darius and Xena, `1019` for Meir), so EVERY bot, follower or leader, now goes to Warriv in its town chores (QuestNpcs: reward pending and not complete) and completes it for itself. Before only the leader's act trip did. UNTESTED for followers.
- Keeper defaults (2026-11-12, user): an absent `pickitRules` / `pickit` in qolbot.json now means the author's Keeper tab, embedded from `cmd/qolbot/keeperdefaults.json` (`keeperdefaults.go`; to refresh it copy those two settings from a qolbot.json). The default leader lists are Autoequip/merc.nip, QOL/Bases, Custom, Jewelry, Ladder, Misc, Potions, Quest, Rares (NOT Autoequip/melee.nip as before). The protected items list needed no change: it already was unique, set, crafted, socketed and runeword protected, rare, magic and ethereal not, plus Khalim's Will by class id.
- Program defaults (2026-11-12, user: "save my current settings as the defaults"): the settings the user had chosen are now what an absent setting means (`Config.Effective`): Quick Travel ON, Full Clear OFF, task reports OFF, banter in a bubble ON (`banterBubble` is a pointer now). Everything else already matched. An existing `qolbot.json` that sets a value keeps it. Not made defaults: the leader, goal, difficulty, farm runs and the keeper rules, which are a player's own choices.
- About page (2026-11-12, user is OCD about equal paragraph sizes): every About sub tab is a grid of pods (`.apods`, two columns, `grid-auto-rows:1fr`) with a short bold title and a body of 184-194 characters (three lines at the normal width); keep new copy in that band. The sub tab bar sits above, outside the white area. The copy was rewritten shorter; the user will audit it. The six pods that explained how Quest mode picks the leader and the difficulty were removed on request (2026-11-12): the Automations tab has AutoEquip, AutoStat, AutoSkill and one Quest Mode pod; the full rule is in docs/QUESTER.md and cmd/qolbot/dynleader.go.
- Auto Quit (chicken/death limit) is a FULL shutdown (2026-11-12, user): when any bot reaches the limit every running bot stops, the leader last (`stopForNotReady`, readiness.go); before, only the offender stopped unless it was the leader. Unit-tested.
- Page (2026-11-12): Safe Mode is an On/Off toggle like "Are you playing?"; clicking Settings or Keeper opens its FIRST sub tab; the bubble of a bot sits a little lower on wide screens so its wedge points at the name; "Pick Farm Locations (N)" has a grey count and sits at the right edge, the "N locations" link is gone.
- Quest mode asks for nothing (2026-11-12, user): no Difficulty select and no leader checkbox. The leader is the bot furthest behind in the quest table (its first quest not done, from the saves; the choose-a-reward quests do not count; ties: higher level, then name), the difficulty is the lowest one on which any bot still has a quest to do (`questDifficulty`). "Start selected" asks `/api/pickleader` first (it also sets the difficulty). While the bots run `dynamicLeaderLoop` (`cmd/qolbot/dynleader.go`, every 20 s) restarts the team (`handOver`, activity event "New Leader") when another running bot is strictly further behind than the leader has become, or when everybody has finished the difficulty (on to the next). Only the ordering and the difficulty choice are unit-tested; the handover itself is UNTESTED.
- Regroup time is a setting (Behavior > Party, `regroupSeconds`, default 30 s, 10-600): `Team.lost(Config.RegroupSeconds*1000)`.
- Auto Waypoint (formerly Waypoint Collection) is a TEAM matter (`common/Waypoints.js`): every bot writes its waypoints to `data/waypoints-<name>.json` (Follower every 10 s, leader at the sweeps), `Waypoints.lacking(area)` says who in the game lacks one. At the start of a game (default.dbj, after `Team.wait`) the leader runs `Waypoints.sync()`: every waypoint somebody has and somebody lacks is shared first: the leader takes it by waypoint, or a team mate that has it takes it and opens a portal beside it (`QuickTravel.waypoint`: leader asks "waypoint <area>", the ones that have it answer, the first by name gets "waypoint go <name> <area>"), a portal stays open and the leader waits (45 s) until the others have clicked it (Follower.gatherWaypoint). The Quester sweep before an act change uses the same. Waypoints nobody has are walked to. UNTESTED. The identify/town stuff: leaving a waypoint area by waypoint costs no scroll.
- The portal ping-pong (leader to town for a team mate that is already on its way out of town, who arrives in the field as the leader arrives in town): the regroup trip now only starts for a team mate that is LOST (apart for a minute AND not in any town: `Team.lost`).
- FOUND IN A GAME 2026-11-12 14:03: after Andariel died every later act was "not open" and Questing idled: `Pather.accessToAct` reads `me.getQuest(7, 0)` without asking the game for the quest state first (`sendPacket(1, 0x40)`, which `getQuestProgress` does). `Quester.actOpen(act, wait)` now asks first and polls up to 10 s after an act boss; used for the access check and the act changes. UNTESTED.
- Auto Layout (Settings > Inventory, which now also holds the old Equipment tab, `inventoryLayout`, default ON): `common/Layout.js`. Town.doChores now always buys both tomes (the identify tome never was bought before: `fillTome` only bought the TP tome and `checkScrolls(519)` said "20"), keeps a few keys for every class (`checkKeys`), and calls `Layout.apply()` at the end of the chores, which moves what stands in the way and puts the cube at 0,0 (2x2), the TP tome at 0,2, the identify tome at 1,2 and the keys at 2,0 (columns, rows; a 10x4 pack). The Horadric Cube, tomes and keys were never stashed or sold (`Item.userOwned`). Items picked up later may land in those cells until the next town visit. Smoke-tested with mocks only. UNTESTED in a game.
- A waypoint that cannot be clicked (rare): `Pather.getWP` steps to another spot beside it on tries 3, 6 and 8 and uses `wp.interact()` on every other try. UNTESTED.
- BUG I introduced and fixed 2026-11-12: `Quester.hasWaypoint` called `getWaypoint(-1)` for areas that have no waypoint (Barracks); the game answered with a "Diablo II Error" box about every 75 s. Only areas in `Pather.wpAreas` are asked now. `getWaypoint(-1)` crashes the game: never call it with an index that might be -1.
- Quester: a quest function that throws is now logged and reported (it used to be swallowed by an empty catch), a quest that is already complete in the game stops the retries, and a table row without a function (Tainted Sun, Summoner) is skipped with one line. Quick Travel for Radament (`portal radament`, `Radament.kill` split out of the script, Follower `travelWormhole` case "radament"). UNTESTED.
- Behavior > Quests > **Auto Waypoint** (`questWaypoints`, default ON: the goal is a fresh character that gets every waypoint and every quest): with it checked the Quest leader takes the waypoint of every area it walks into (`Quester.captureWaypoint`, hooked through `Pather.onArrive` at the end of `moveToExit`; only if the waypoint is within 80 steps) and, before moving to the next act, every waypoint of the act it has not got (`Quester.leaveAct`; the game answers "have I got it" through `getWaypoint`). Works in all five acts: the sweep (`Quester.collectWaypoints`) runs when the act is left (Andariel, Duriel) and BEFORE the last fight of acts 3, 4 and 5 (Mephisto, Diablo, Baal), since those lead on by portal; if the leader is already in another act `Pather.getWP`/`journeyTo` has to walk back (not verified that journeyTo crosses acts). UNTESTED.
- Act changes: the `this.idle()` at the end of the Andariel, Duriel, Mephisto and Tyrael quest scripts is gone (the leader sat in town forever after Andariel); the leader now goes to the next act through its NPC with `common/ActTravel.js` (moved out of Follower.js, which uses it too). UNTESTED.
- Casters pick quiet spots: `Attack.getIntoPosition` (casting distance 10 or more, in the field) chooses among the 8 nearest candidate spots the one with the fewest monsters within 8 (`Attack.crowd`), so a teleporting Sorceress does not land in the pack. UNTESTED; if casters now stand too far from targets, look here first.
- Necromancer Summon template: `FollowNoFight = true`, `FollowDistance = 14`, and Follower.js re-raises the army (`Precast.doPrecast` + `ClassAttack.raiseArmy`) every 15 s in the field, so a summoner stands back like the Summon Druid instead of meleeing. UNTESTED.
- Party Regroup is an Activity event (`reRegroup` in activity.go, parsed from the Team.regroup line). The regroup has no cooldown; `Team.gather` makes the leader wait (45 s) for the team before it changes area. 2026-11-12: gather fired at nearly every area change with 15-20 s waits; followers lag a whole area behind.
- Checks without a game: `scripts/check.sh` runs `go vet`, `go test`, `scripts/jsparse.py` (every script under d2bs/kolbot parses) and `scripts/jsmoke.py` (the common modules with mocked D2BS globals, 59 checks). Both Python scripts run headless Firefox through `scripts/headless.py` and get the result back over a local HTTP POST; they exit non-zero on failure. They prove syntax and module logic only, NOT behaviour in a game.
- Walking: `Pather.walkTo` stops waiting once the position moved and counts retries only without progress; `Pather.useUnit` waits up to 3 s, nudging the position, before "Unit not found". UNTESTED in a game (baseline to beat: 8-10 "move retry" lines per Countess+Andariel run).
- Walking, second round (2026-11-12): the first hardening still failed 2 of 2 runs ("Failed to move to Countess" in Tower Cellar, "Failed to move to Catacombs Level 4": three retries within 6 s while fighting). `Pather.moveTo` now defaults to 6 retries and, from the second failure on, calls the new `Pather.sidestep` (click a free spot a few steps aside). Live check, Vanity leader + Meir on Normal, Countess+Andariel: 2 runs, no errors, 3 and 8 "move retry" lines, 198 s and 233 s (baseline 8-10 lines, 202 s). Two runs is a small sample.
- Follower chat commands ("portal <place>") are queued in `Follower.errands` and run from the main loop (`runErrands`), not inside the chat handler (avoids the "Talking to undefined" re-entrancy). UNTESTED.
- Settings > Safety, "save before leaving when in danger" (`saveOnChicken`, default off, needs PlugY commands): `Saver.beforeLeaving` sends /save and waits 1.2 s before a chicken quit (`ToolsThread.exit(true)`). UNTESTED; whether it helps is unknown.
- Sorceress templates: the Static Field point (`[42, 1, false]`) was removed from Meteorb and Lightning (their `CastStatic` is 100 = never cast, so the point was wasted; AutoSkill adds real prerequisites by itself). FrozenOrb keeps it (`CastStatic = 60`).
- Merc upgrades: `Item.autoMercEquipCheck` now also asks `mercCanWear` (a Desert Guard cannot wear shields in the script table, nobody wears rings), so the bots stop picking up and keeping gear their mercenary will refuse. Before, such items piled up in the pickup corner and were swept into the stash on the next join (Meir stashed three kite shields). Check the `Item.mercWeapons` table if a mercenary turns out to wear more than it says.
- Activity entries for items: a kept pickup ("Picked up" line, 🏆 or the item icon, "Keeper"), a stash ("Stashed <item>" printed by `Town.stash` and `AutoSpec.freePickupArea`, 🗄️, "Stashed"). Both carry the quality colour, so Qolbot reads the raw log line (`rawD2BSText`) for them.
- Kill watching (ON by default, `"watchKills": false` in qolbot.json turns it off; docs/AUTOMATION.md "Kills the bots report as they watch"): `tools/ToolsThread.js` `watchKills` (it first lived in PickThread.js, which these bots never load: default.dbj hooks fast pickit itself) (a boss dying, `spectype & 0x05`: super unique or unique, no champions; scanned every 2 s), Qolbot dedupes by monster id in `addEvent`. Followers loading the Keeper rules while you play ("followerKeepers") and "Saw drop" reports were tried and REMOVED: a follower picked up something it should not have. UNTESTED in a game.
- Config, Quests tab: `questClearAll` (default true). When false, `Quester.clearAndCapture` never calls `Attack.clearLevel` (the quest scripts kill their own targets). UNTESTED.
- Quester audit (docs/QUESTER.md): `Quester.error()` idles the leader forever (the 3-attempt retry never runs after an error), the catch around a quest function is empty, `act2_tainted_sun` and `act2_summoner` are in the table but have no function, no partial progress, quest state is per difficulty, `grindLevels` is dead code.
- Follower, leader out in an area with no waypoint (e.g. Tamoe Highland): `useWaypoint` throws "Invalid area" and the old code then tried the leader's portal, which went nowhere ("area 1 -> 1", 4 trips in 6 s, then the "I keep going out" chat line; seen in logs/bot-Xena.log 2026-11-10 18:23). Now the stuck follower says "I cannot reach you. Open a town portal ..." (repeated every 45 s) and the LEADER bot's `tools/TownChicken.js` thread (a `chatmsg` listener, only when `Config.Leader` is empty) pauses default.dbj, calls `Pather.makePortal()` and resumes; until it does the follower waits in town (it never walks out alone: user rule). UNTESTED in a game.
- Follower, leader goes to town while the follower is out walking or fighting (2026-11-11): the follow check only ran between blocking calls, so a bot kept walking/fighting toward where the leader had been. Now `Follower.leaderMoved` is installed as `Pather.interrupt`; `Pather.moveTo` (per path node) and `Attack.clear`/`clearList` (per loop) return early when it is true (bot in the field, leader's area is a town), and it resets the `follow` timer so `autoFollow` runs on the next pass. `this.following` stops it cutting short the follow trip itself; a failed trip pauses the interrupt for 15 s. Only the leader-to-town case is covered (not leader moving between field areas). UNTESTED in a game (no JS engine here).
- Quest Progress (Bots tab, 2026-11-11): with Objective Quest (not Playing), check two or more bots and a "Quest Progress" button appears under the list. `GET /api/partyquests?names=` -> `cmd/qolbot/quests.go` reads the quest block of each character's .d2s (after "Woo!": 6 bytes, then 96 bytes per difficulty, one 16-bit word per quest id, bit 0 = complete, the same test as `Quester.getQuestProgress`) and the page shows a bots-by-quests grid per difficulty. It shows the last SAVED state (works for stopped bots). Quest table copied from `Quester.quests` (`questTable`): keep them in step. Layout checked against one real save only; page UI untested in a browser.
- Quester, reward-only quests (2026-11-11): Tools of the Trade (3), Siege on Harrogath (35) and Betrayal of Harrogath (38) have a last step only the player can do (Charsi's imbue, Larzuk's socket, Anya's personalize). Their complete bit stays clear until it is claimed, so `runQuests` redid the whole quest every run (the old `progress >= 50` check never fired: progress is only 0 or 100). Now it skips them while `questPending(id)` (reward bit set, complete bit clear). UNTESTED in a game (no JS engine here). Party analysis still shows them as not done.
- Questing, 2026-11-11: after the reward-only skip, a leader with no quest left finished `runQuests` in ~13 s and the game restarted in a loop (looked like disconnects). `Questing.js` now idles after `runQuests` returns ("no quest is left to do"), and `runQuests` logs once per game why each quest is skipped ("Quester: quest skipped: ..."). The cause of the quick finish on Meir (Hell) is NOT known: her save shows Act 2 Hell quests unfinished, so check those log lines. UNTESTED in a game.
- FOUND 2026-11-11: the "Questing quits fast" loop was NOT a Quester bug. Meir's game was created on NORMAL although Hell was requested (Vanity logged `diff 0`; the skip lines matched her Normal quest bits). The host starter never logged "Select Difficulty", so screen 20 / the click did not happen. Added `Host: no action for screen N` (D2BotTcpIpHost.dbj, once per screen) and `Questing: playing the quests on <difficulty>` to find out. Difficulty selection is still UNVERIFIED.
- Difficulty FIX 2026-11-11 (UNTESTED): `Host: no action for screen 18` was only the splash. The starter calls D2BS's NATIVE `login(profile)`, which clicks the difficulty screen itself and never returns to the script, so `chooseDifficulty` in D2BotTcpIpHost.dbj never runs. D2BS reads the difficulty from the profile key `SPDifficulty` (0 Normal, 1 Nightmare, 2 Hell; the DLL has a `spdifficulty` field), default 0 = Normal. `writeD2BSIni` now writes `SPDifficulty=` for every profile from the Objective's difficulty (the ini is rewritten on every save of the config). Check the next game with `Questing: playing the quests on <difficulty>`. If it is still Normal the key name is wrong.
- Difficulty on the Status cards (2026-11-11): `ToolsThread.js` sends it as the 2nd argument of `D2Bot.zone(name, diff)` (OOG.js) whenever the area changes; `rpc.go` stores it in `State.Difficulty` (cleared on stop); the pod shows it before the zone ("Hell • Sewers Level 3"). UNTESTED in a game; the dev demo shows made-up values.
- Lost progress, 2026-11-11 (Meir, Hell, Radament): the leader's game ended at 21:22:16 (chicken: "Pausing", script ended) and the quest finished at 21:21:31 was not in the save. In Quest mode the leader NEVER logged "AutoSpec: saving the character (every 2 min)" (followers do; Meir did as a follower), so `tools/AutoSaveThread.js` is not saving, cause UNKNOWN. Now: `Quester.runQuests` calls `AutoSpec.saveNow(false)` right after a quest completes, and the thread prints "AutoSaveThread: running, AutoSpec <type>" and its errors. UNTESTED in a game. Save-on-stop (Save and Exit) works; only a game that ends by itself loses the unsaved part.
- Quester idled forever in Radament's Lair, 2026-11-11 21:28 (Meir, Hell): Radament had been killed in an earlier game whose save lost the quest completion, so the Book of Skill did not drop again, `pickAndPlunder` called `error()` (which idles for good) and the bot never reached Atma. `pickAndPlunder` now has an `is_optional` argument (used for the book): a missing item is logged and the quest goes on. Other `error()` calls still idle forever (see the audit). UNTESTED in a game.
- Quest consumables need room (2026-11-11, user rule: the Book of Skill, Potion of Life, Scroll of Resistance must always be picked up and used): `QuestItems.makeRoom(w, h)` (new module `libs/common/QuestItems.js`: consumables, keep list, find, useAll, makeRoom, pickUp, own logging; Quester.js only calls it) (town chores / town trip, then drinks the spare potions in the pack, then drops junk the bot would sell anyway; never quest items, tomes, keys, cube/runeword parts, charms, Pandemonium items or the player's own). Called before the Radament fight, before `pickAndPlunder` takes any quest item (`QuestItems.pickUp`; a consumable is used at once) and before each quest NPC talk. If nothing can be freed it says so (`problem`) and goes on. Not covered: a reward handed over when the pack is full mid-talk with no earlier check. UNTESTED in a game; all edited scripts parse in headless Firefox (`new Function`).
- Quest item logging (2026-11-11): `QuestItems.useAll` prints `QuestItems: using the X (unspent skill points a, stat points b)`, then `QuestItems: used the X (skill points a -> a2, stat points b -> b2)` or `QuestItems: the X would not be used, it stays in the pack`, so a Book of Skill / Potion of Life / Scroll of Resistance is traceable in logs/bot-<name>.log. The "used" and "would not be used" lines are also Activity entries (kind `questitem`, `parseEvent` in activity.go, "Quest Item Used" / "Quest Item Not Used" with what it gave, e.g. "skill points 0 to 1"). The skill/stat numbers are the UNSPENT points only (AutoSkill/AutoStat may spend them a moment later). UNTESTED in a game.
- Reporting (2026-11-11): scripts that are not AutoSpec log under their own name through `libs/common/Report.js` (`Report.log/note/problem(source, msg)`: the log line `Quester: ...` / `QuestItems: ...` is always written; chat only when the player's report switches (`Config.QuietChat/QuietProblems`) allow). Qolbot reads those lines for the Activity tab (`stripPrefixes` knows `Quester: ` and `QuestItems: `; a quest entry logged AND said in chat is added once, `addEvent`). `AutoSpec.note/problem` are no longer used by the Quester.  Older logs keep the old `AutoSpec (quiet):` text.
- Radament / Book of Skill, 2026-11-11 22:23: after the kill the fight ended with town trips, so `pickAndPlunder` looked for the book while the bot stood in TOWN ("the item is not there"), which proved nothing about a drop. `act2_radaments_lair` now walks back to where Radament died (waypoint 48, exit to 49, preset 355) when `me.area !== 49`, then picks up. `QuestItems.makeRoom` order is now: town chores, spare health and mana potions, junk, and only then rejuvenation potions (the first run drank 3 Rejuvenation and 3 Full Rejuvenation Potions among 14). The Book of Skill gives 1 skill point, not 2 (earlier text was wrong); Meir's save adds up to 86 = 77 levels + 3 Den + 4 Izual + 2 books (Normal, Nightmare), so the Hell book is not in her save. UNTESTED in a game.
- Point audit and restore (2026-11-11): `cmd/qolbot/audit.go` reads each character's .d2s once, on the first page load (`auditFor`; "Rescan characters" or a restore resets it) and compares what it holds with what its level and finished quests should have given (a quest counts when its complete bit is set): SKILL points = level-1 + Den of Evil 1 + Radament 1 + Fallen Angel 2 per difficulty; STAT points = class start (80/80/80/85/85/80/85) + 5 per level + Lam Esen's Tome 5 per difficulty; LIFE = class base + per level + per Vitality (`lifeClass`: Druid and Necromancer 1.5 per level) + Alkor's Potion of Life 20 per difficulty (The Golden Bird, quest 20; whole potions only). A shortage puts a warning sign next to the profile name on the Bots tab; its modal shows the numbers and the quests counted and has the button "Restore the missing points" (`POST /api/restorepoints?profile=`, `restorePoints`): only for a STOPPED bot, it adds exactly the shortage to the unspent skill points, unspent stat points and life (`spec.PatchAttributes`: fixed-width attribute fields, checksum recomputed and checked against a real save), keeps a copy of the old file in `backups/` (git-ignored), checks that the new file audits clean, then replaces the file. THIS WRITES TO THE USER'S REAL SAVE FOLDER: approved by the user for this feature (2026-11-11). Checked against the 8 real saves: all add up (skill, stat and life; Amazon life is 1 point off, Druid .5) except Meir, 1 skill point short (the Hell Book of Skill). NOT visible in a save: the Scroll of Resistance (+10 resists, Prison of Ice; no attribute or quest bit shows it), Malah's Potion (cures frostbite only), Charsi's imbue, Larzuk's socket, Anya's naming (choose-an-item rewards: the quest word shows "reward waiting", the Quest Progress sheet lists them). `QOLBOT_SAVES=<folder> go test ./cmd/qolbot -run TestScanRealSaves -v` prints every character. The restore itself has only run on synthetic files and a scratch copy of a real save, never on a real one in place.
- Save on level up (2026-11-11): `ToolsThread.js` calls `Saver.soon("level up")` when it sees a new level; it sends `/save` without waiting (so the thread is not held up), at most every 15 s, and not when autosave is switched off. UNTESTED in a game.
- Lost Book of Skill, concluded 2026-11-11: Meir picked the book up at 21:20:36 (the item is named "Right Click to learn skill of your choice", which is why it was missed in the logs), the game ended at 21:22:16 on a LIFE CHICKEN and that exit did not keep the pack or the Atma completion. Radament was killed 3 times (21:20, 22:23, 22:59); the book was only taken and read the third time. No other player took it (all pickups are Meir's pids). Safeguards now: save after each finished quest, save after a quest item is used (`QuestItems.useAll` -> `Saver.soon`), save on level up. Open question: the in-game quest check reported "complete" at 21:21 and 22:25 while the file said otherwise, and the kill state WAS in the file after the chicken. UNTESTED in a game.
- ROOT CAUSE of the leader never autosaving, 2026-11-11: `AutoSaveThread: running, AutoSpec undefined.` (the diagnostic line, every run). The thread called `Config.init()` inside `main()` and relied on the class config to include `common/AutoSpec.js`; in that thread it never did, so `typeof AutoSpec === "object"` was false and nothing was ever saved (Meir's saves: only quest completions and stops; 25 minutes with none). `/save` itself WORKS: the Radament completion saved by my `/save` at 22:59:58 survived the chicken + 0xC0000005 crash at 23:00:46 (the next game said "already complete"). Fix: `include("common/AutoSpec.js")` at the top level of `AutoSaveThread.js` and `ToolsThread.js` (the level-up and quest-item saves ran behind the same guard and would have been skipped too). Then saving moved out of AutoSpec into `libs/common/Saver.js` (below), which the thread now includes alone. Check the next run for `AutoSaveThread: running, saving every 2 min` and `Saver: saving the character (every 2 min)` on the leader. UNTESTED in a game; both files parse in headless Firefox.
- Saver (2026-11-11): saving the character lives in `libs/common/Saver.js` (`Saver.now(ack)`, `Saver.soon(reason)`, `Saver.auto()`, `Saver.minutes()` from the plan's autoSaveMinutes), not in AutoSpec. Callers: default.dbj (Qolbot's stop), Follower.js and AutoSaveThread.js (every few minutes), Quester.js (quest finished), QuestItems.js (item used), ToolsThread.js (level up), AutoSpec.js (items moved). Log lines now start `Saver:`. AutoSpec.js still holds things that are not its job (see the AutoSpec note below). UNTESTED in a game; all edited files parse in headless Firefox.
- AutoSpec split (2026-11-11, user: AutoSpec had become a catch-all): `AutoSpec.js` went from 566 to ~170 lines and keeps only `apply()`. Moved: save functions -> `Saver`; `read` -> `Plan`; `due/timers` -> `Throttle`; `note/problem/fail` -> `Report` (the three one-off wrappers in Follower, Quester and QuestItems are gone: `Report.mixin`); `waitForTeam` -> `Team.wait`; `tunePotions/needPotions/choresNeeded` -> `Chores`; `buildLockMatrix/freePickupArea` -> `Pack` (its two copies of the stash book-keeping are one `Pack.stashed`); `questReport` -> `Quester.logState`. Log prefixes are now the script's own (`Follower:`, `Team:`, `Chores:`, `Pack:`, `Town:`, `Pickit:`, `Saver:`, `Quester:`); `stripPrefixes` (activity.go) knows them and still reads the old `AutoSpec (quiet):` lines. No file outside AutoSpec.js refers to the removed `AutoSpec.*` functions any more (grep). Checked: every edited script parses in headless Firefox, and `scripts/jsmoke.py` runs 35 checks on the new modules (all pass). The callers (Follower, default.dbj, Town, Pickit, Misc) have only been parsed, not run: UNTESTED in a game.
- Not-ready watch (2026-11-11): `cmd/qolbot/readiness.go`. `updateChickens`/`updateDeaths` (rpc.go) call `noteSetback`; 3 setbacks within 30 min by default (Settings tab > Behavior > Safety, kept as `chickenLimit` and `chickenWindowMinutes` in qolbot.json, 0 = off; applies at once; `/api/play` validates 0-99 and 1-240) stop the bot (`stopProfile`; a leader stops everybody through `stopMany`, followers first) and add an Activity entry `notready` ("Stopped: may not be ready", with the zones, difficulty and the bot's last "Life Chicken (a/b) to X" line, kept from its printToConsole). Meir's real log would have tripped it at 22:53 (22:40, 22:44, 22:53). Counts only running bots; the count resets after a stop. Unit tested (readiness_test.go); not seen in a real game.
- Quest entries are verified against the save (2026-11-11): the Quester says "complete" when `getQuestProgress` (a fresh 0x40 packet, then `me.getQuest(id, 0)`) is true. That is the game's in-memory state, NOT the character file, and for Radament's Lair it was ahead of the file twice. `verifyQuest` (quests.go) waits for the bot's save, reads the quest word of the file for that quest (`questIDByTitle`, the difficulty from the bot's state) and sets `ActivityEvent.Verified` to `saved` or `notsaved`; the Activity tab shows "in the save" or "NOT in the save" next to Quest Complete. `Quester.logBits(id)` also logs the sixteen in-game bits of a quest when it reports complete (`Quester: quest 9 bits (0-15): ...`), to find which bit the server waits for. Unit tested; UNTESTED in a game.
- Revert-on-load hypothesis (user, 2026-11-12): the game may take a quest completion back when the character loads if the reward (the Book of Skill) was not used. It fits all three Radament attempts (complete + book unread or missing -> gone after reload; complete + book read -> kept) and cannot be told apart from "the server never recorded it" with the logs we have. To settle it: Qolbot logs `saved file before start, quest words ...` for the point quests just before each bot starts (`logQuestWordsAtStart`), and `Questing.js` logs the game's own bits for the same quests at the start of the run (`Quester: quest N bits (0-15): ...`). Complete in the file but not in the game = reverted on load. Not seen in a game yet.
- PHENOMENON, quest reward items (observed 2026-11-11, Meir, Radament's Lair on Hell; mechanism NOT proven): the bot's in-game check said "complete" after talking to Atma, but the character file only had the quest as complete when the Book of Skill had been READ first. Attempts with the book unread (picked up but not used) or never taken were back to "kill done, talk to Atma" after the game reloaded; the attempt that read the book first kept it, through a crash. The book dropped again on each kill until it was taken (the wiki: no second book once the quest is complete). Best guess (user's): the game re-derives such quests from whether the reward item was used and takes the completion back on load if not; the other explanation is that the server never recorded it. `Quester: quest N bits` and `saved file before start` log lines are there to tell them apart. Practical rules that follow: a quest consumable must be picked up AND used at once, the character must be saved right after, and "Quest Complete" on the Activity tab now says whether the save has it. A missing reward is normally earned by running the quest again; the restore button is the fallback.
- Followers and quest consumables (2026-11-11): a follower of a leader bot used to pick up NOTHING (AutoSpec stubbed Pickit), so it could never take its own Book of Skill. Now `Pickit.questOnly` (set from `plan.noPickup`) lets it pick up the quest consumables only (`QuestItems.isConsumable`), `Pickit.pickItems` makes room for one when the pack is full (`QuestItems.makeRoom`, then looks again) and reads it at once, `Follower`'s main loop calls `QuestItems.useAll()` every 10 s for rewards the game put into the pack itself, and `Quester.runQuests` does the same before each quest. UNTESTED in a game (parsed in Firefox only).
- Meir test edit (2026-11-12, user's request): one point taken out of Ice Blast (19 -> 18) in her save to test the restore button; her original file is in `backups/Meir-before-skill-test-20261112-093055.d2s`. The audit then shows 87 of 88.
- Point audit fix 2026-11-12: the restore failed on Meir ("attribute 5 ... not in the file"): a save stores only the attributes that are NOT zero, so a character with no unspent skill points has no entry for them. `spec.PatchAttributes` now rebuilds the attribute block (inserting a missing attribute in id order, which moves the later sections by a few bytes), corrects the file size field and the checksum, and refuses a block that does not re-encode to the same bytes. Tried on a scratch copy of her real save: +1 unspent skill point, everything else equal, tail of the file identical, checksum valid. A row with a problem has a light red background (`tr.audit`). The audit runs once on the first page load, and again after Rescan characters or a restore.
- Backups of character files (the point restore's `backups/*.d2s`) are git-ignored (`/backups/` and `*.d2s`) and are deleted by the Clear logs button (`clearLogs`, which also removes the folder when empty) and by `clear.sh`; the loot history is still kept. Tested on a throwaway folder.
- Team teleport option (2026-11-12), STEP 1 only: Quests tab checkbox "Bots with Sorceress party members will make use of Teleport" (`teleportTeam` in qolbot.json, in the plan as `plan.teleportTeam`, disabled while "clear every zone" is checked). Qolbot writes the running Sorceresses into `data/bots.json` as `sorcs`; `Quester.teleportReady()` returns a Sorceress that is in the game when the option is on, `questClearAll` is false and this bot cannot teleport itself (else null). NOTHING USES IT YET. The idea (user's): the existing Follower chat command `<Sorc> portal <place>` (`openWormhole`: town, waypoint, teleport to the boss area, make a portal, take it to town, say "Portal is open."; places: countess, mausoleum, pit, andy, at, summoner, duriel, temple, meph, diablo, halls, baal) lets the leader jump to a boss instead of walking the zones. The quest functions walk zone by zone with `clearAndCapture`, and the boss scripts (Countess.js ...) start from town + waypoint, so using the portal needs a per-boss "already there" tail. Scope not decided.
- Team teleport option, STEP 2, pilot (2026-11-12, user chose Andariel and Countess): `Quester.portalFromSorc(place, area)` says `<Sorc> portal <place>` (the Follower chat command), waits up to 150 s for her "Portal is open." (or "No such place." / "I am not able to travel quickly."), then takes her portal from town (`Pather.usePortal(area, sorc)`); false and nothing lost when it cannot (the caller walks as before). Only when `teleportReady()` (option on AND "clear every zone" OFF AND a Sorceress in the game AND this bot cannot teleport itself). Used by `act1_forgotten_tower` (portal "countess", area 25, then `Countess.kill()`) and `act1_sisters_to_the_slaughter` (portal "andy", area 37, then `Andariel.kill()`). `Countess.js` and `Andariel.js` were split into the approach and `Countess.kill` / `Andariel.kill` (the "already there" part, shared by both routes, no copy). Also: with "clear every zone" off the walking route now kills Andariel too (clearing the levels used to be what killed her). Followers reach the leader after a portal through the usual "I cannot reach you" request (the leader's TownChicken opens a portal). UNTESTED in a game (parsed in Firefox only).
- Team teleport option, RULE (user, 2026-11-12): the option (Quests tab checkbox, always editable) switches the whole thing; with it on, FARM mode always uses a Sorceress party member's portal (`plan.goal === \"Farm\"`), QUEST mode only when \"clear every zone\" is off (`plan.goal === \"Quest\"` and `questClearAll === false`); nothing for a bot that has no objective. `Countess()` and `Andariel()` themselves ask for the portal first (that is the Farm route; `Quester.noPortal[place]` stops a second ask after a failure, so the quest route's walking fallback is not delayed again). `scripts/jsmoke.py` has 46 checks incl. these rules. UNTESTED in a game.
- Quick Travel, UI and keep-together (2026-11-12, user): the Quests tab line is \"**Quick Travel:** Bot parties including a Sorceress will make use of Teleport\"; it and \"clear every zone\" grey each other out (`qExclusive`) and `/api/config` refuses both on (`quickTravelConflict`, tested), so with the option on the quest rule (clear off) always holds and Farm mode simply uses it. Keep-together: followers use `usePortal(leader.area, null)` when the leader has no portal of its own to where it is (a Sorceress's portal the leader took); with the option on, `TownChicken.js` (leader bot only, no `Config.Leader`) calls `Team.separated(60000)` every 5 s and `Team.regroup()` (town, wait up to 90 s for the team, back out by its own portal) at most once per 3 min. The regroup applies ONLY with the option on (the user said \"all farm and quest modes\" for the Quick Travel hooks; making it the default for every run would be a bigger change: ask). `scripts/jsmoke.py` has 53 checks. UNTESTED in a game.
- Quick Travel TESTED in a game (2026-11-12, Meir the Sorceress following, Vanity the Amazon leading, Normal, Farm: Countess then Andariel): the leader asked `portal countess`, Meir went (waypoint, exits, Tower Cellar 5), opened the portal and said \"Portal is open.\"; Vanity took it 22 s after asking, Meir followed him through, the Countess died 10 s after arrival; the same for Andariel (14 s); the whole run took 117 s. Two bugs found and fixed by that test: (1) the leader must go to the boss's ACT town first (`portal(place, area, act)`; both characters were parked in Act 4); (2) the follower's \"leader is in town, stop walking\" hook (`Pather.interrupt`) also fired during the Sorceress's errand and cut her walk short (`this.errand`), and `openWormhole` now checks it really reached the area before opening a portal (`wormholeAreas`, \"I could not get there.\"). UNTESTED in a game, same pattern: Pit, Ancient Tunnels (only without chest/Dark Elder), Summoner (only without Fire Eye), Nihlathak, Mephisto, Duriel (any of the 7 tombs), Diablo (to the star: expects area 108), Baal. NOT hooked up: Mausoleum/Blood Raven (the script kills her before the Mausoleum the portal leads to), Radament, the Forgotten Temple. `Duriel.js` has CR-only line endings: edit it as bytes.
- Refactors done (2026-11-12, user approved 1-4; the Follower split is on hold): (1) one quest table: `QuestTable.json` replaces `Quester.quests`, `Quester.npcQuests` and Go's `questTable`/`pointQuests`/aliases (`loadQuestTable`; the Go tests read the real file); (2) `Town.clearInventory` uses `QuestItems.isProtected` (no second list); (3) `Quester.js` 2,040 lines -> 698 + `QuestScripts.js` (994) + `QuestNpcs.js` (270) + `QuickTravel.js` (105), checked line by line that no code was lost; (4) Go: `Config.Effective()` (effective.go) is the one place that applies the setting defaults (it was worked out again in specplan.go, web.go, save.go, settings.go), `m.savePath(character)`, and the API handlers moved out of `serveWeb` into `webapi.go` (`web.go` 604 -> 232 lines). `scripts/jsmoke.py` runs 53 checks on the JS modules.
- The team stays together, ALWAYS (2026-11-12, user; this rule is above Quick Travel): the leader bot with an objective (Farm or Quest) goes to town when a teammate has been in another area for a minute, waits up to 90 s for everyone, and goes back out by its own portal (`Team.regroup` from `TownChicken.js`, no limit on how often: each trip logs who was apart and a running count, because a lot of trips means something else is broken; 2026-11-12 the 3-minute cooldown was removed; the leader also waits for the team before changing area, `Team.gather`). It is not tied to Quick Travel any more. A Sorceress on a Quick Travel errand only places the portal and comes back.
- Quick Travel regression test on the FINAL code (2026-11-12, after the refactors): the first attempt failed twice in one game (\"not in the right place after Meir's portal (area 1)\": the leader never changed zone; the walking fallback then also failed with \"Failed to move to Countess\" and \"useUnit: Unit not found. ID: 18\", which is the old walking route, not Quick Travel) and the second game worked. Cause: the leader looked for her portal from wherever it stood, and `usePortal(area, null)` does not walk to the town's portal spot on its retries (only an owner name does). Fixed in `QuickTravel.portal`: `Town.move(\"portalspot\")`, then `usePortal(area, sorc)`, twice. Rerun in a fresh game: Countess and Andariel both by portal in the FIRST round, 127 s. The Qolbot used for the tests was stopped and `qolbot.json` put back (only `teleportTeam` false instead of absent). The walking fallback errors (Countess \"Failed to move\", Andariel \"useUnit\") were seen only when the portal failed; they are not investigated.
- UI and Quick Travel switch (2026-11-12, user): Activity cards: the line under the name never wraps (`.pod .actrow>span:first-child` nowrap + ellipsis, the age stays; the full text is the tooltip). About tab has sub-tabs History (the old text), Automations, Protections, Assumptions (short, for peace of mind; routes /about/<slug>). The Settings Quests tab is merged into Behavior (old /settings/quests still routes there): Quests (Full Clear) and Teleport (the Quick Travel checkbox, caption \"This setting won't apply to Quest Mode if Full Clear is on.\"); one Save button (`saveBehavior`). Quick Travel is now ONE master switch for both modes (`plan.teleportTeam`, default OFF): Farm mode uses it when it is on, Quest mode when it is on and Full Clear is off (`QuickTravel.sorc`). The earlier \"Farm mode always\" rule and the Full Clear / Quick Travel exclusion (`quickTravelConflict`) are gone. The Protections page says what the code really does: only charms (those the bot did not pick up itself), tomes, keys and the Horadric Cube are left alone in the pack (`Item.userOwned`); anything else in a bot's pack is its own unless its kind is protected.
- Quick Travel, the dig into its failures (2026-11-12): tally of in-game rounds (Countess + Andariel each, Vanity Amazon leading, Meir Sorceress following, Normal): two rounds failed because of bugs (act town, `Pather.interrupt`), then 2 failures in the first game and 2 successes in the second game on the refactored code (leader could not find her portal; she had answered while still in her first town chores, so `Talking to undefined` appears in her log: the chat command runs inside the interrupted chores, harmless), then 2 of 2 after the portal-spot fix (3 of 3 rounds in the first game). `QuickTravel.portal` now keeps looking for her portal for ~20 s (8 looks, portal spot first, her name) and logs `portals in view: Owner>area dDistance` each look, so the next failure has evidence. The WALKING route (Quick Travel off), measured solo and with Meir following: Countess + Andariel in 158 s and 202 s, with `move retry` lines in every run (up to retry 3) and an occasional \"Failed to move to Countess\" / \"useUnit: Unit not found\" when the retries run out; that is the old kolbot walking code under Wine, not Quick Travel. By portal the same two bosses took 114-127 s. Not done: a second portal request after a portal that was answered but could not be taken.
- Stopping: `Manager.stopMany` / `saveAllAndWait` stop the followers first, wait until they are stopped, then the leader (the host must leave last or the followers lose unsaved progress); used by Stop selected (`/api/stopmany`), Stop all and Ctrl-C. Stopping the leader while other bots are still running is refused (`Manager.stopBlocked`, the page shows the message): stop them first or use Stop all.

None of them talked while following. Each one finished only when it became leader and ran the quest itself: Barabas 15:49, Darius 16:58, Sankt 17:01, each with one talk to Asheara and no field work. He was in Spider Forest alone, "Fighting demons" 17:05:44-17:06:10, while the others stood in town. Most likely the resumed walk does not pass the town gate again; not checked in the code yet.
- Auto Respec (2026-11-13, user request; Automation tab, Settings; `autoRespec`, default OFF): `Respec.maybe()` (libs/common/Respec.js, called from `Follower.doChores` before `AutoSkill.init`) takes ALL skill points back once through PlugY's "Unassign all skills points" button when the bot is in town and a quarter or more of its points (at least 6) sit in skills its build neither wants nor needs (prerequisites counted), or when the build was chosen by hand on the Bots page (`plan.respecForce`; then any misfit counts). It asks Qolbot with `D2Bot.respec()`; `rpc.go` sends the bridge `input respec x y` (d2bridge: T, click, T, window messages like savequit) and the script checks that the unspent skill points went up. One try per character and build (`data/<profile>.respec.json`), never in custom mode unless forced, needs AutoSkill and PlugY. STATS are NOT respecced (PlugY needs Alt held for those clicks). The button is the X in the top right of the skill page (the user told me; `respecButton` in cmd/qolbot/respec.go, 595,34 in the 640x480 window; T opens the page, verified on a screenshot; the square icon at the bottom left is the game's own close button), but the CLICK has not been tried on a character. UNTESTED in a game.
- Whirlwind template (2026-11-13): Whirlwind needs character level 30, so a level 26 respec of Barabas put 27 points into prerequisites and war cries and none into Whirlwind. The template's Whirlwind entry is now `satisfy = true` (points wait for level 30) and the attack skill is Concentrate until Whirlwind is known. Applies to characters that have not spent their points yet. UNTESTED.
- Level up first (2026-11-13, user's choice): in Quest mode with nobody playing, when the chicken limit trips, `beginLevelUp` (cmd/qolbot/levelup.go) does NOT shut everybody down: it stops and saves the team, then restarts it (`handOverWhy`, highest level hosts) on a leveling Farm run (`levelUpFarm`: Countess + Andariel on Normal and Nightmare, Mephisto + Pindleskin on Hell; a guess, check the XP) until the weakest bot has gained 3 levels (`levelUpGain`, read from the saves by `levelUpLoop` every 30 s) or 3 hours passed, then goes back to the quests with the normal leader/difficulty choice. `m.levelUp` overrides `goalNow` and `farmSelection` while it lasts, so the saved goal never changes, and the dynamic leader loop is off meanwhile. A second trip of the limit DURING the detour stops everybody for good. Setting `levelUpFirst` in qolbot.json (default true, no page control yet). Pure parts unit-tested; the restart flow is UNTESTED live.
- `POST /api/input?profile=X&data=key 84` (testing aid, localhost): posts a key or click to a bot's game window through the bridge. Used on 2026-11-13 to open the skill page (T) on a waiting leader and screenshot the hidden display (`import -display :2 -window <id>`; the display number and window id come from `xwininfo -root -tree`).
- Detour banner (2026-11-13, user: it was confusing to see Farm mode when the bots had been started in Quest mode): `/api/state` carries `detour` (`detourLocked`, levelup.go: kind, title, text, since; the weakest bot's level is refreshed by `levelUpLoop` every 30 s) while the leveling detour runs; the Activity tab shows it in a box above the bot cards and the leader's role reads "Leading level hunt" / "Leading gold hunt" (the hunt's `label`), "Leading waypoint hunt" while the team shares waypoints, else "Leading quest" / "Leading farm" (see the Hunts entry below). The saved goal in `/api/state` stays Quest. Gold runs can use the same `detour` shape. Needs a manager restart to load (the page is embedded); `m.levelUp` is memory only, so a manager restart during a detour ends it.
- Broke bot (2026-11-13, user: "it's bc barabas is broke"): `TownChicken.js` left the game whenever the bot had no town portal scrolls, even with no gold to buy any, so Barabas did 12 games in 20 minutes with no XP. It now leaves only with at least 100 gold (and Town.fillTome now buys single scrolls from 100 gold when the tome exists: it refused below 450, which was the real loop); otherwise it says so once per 10 minutes and carries on. Idea from the user, not built: bots give gold to a broke teammate, and money-making runs when the team is short (needs each bot's gold reported to Qolbot first: the save reader does not expose gold). UNTESTED.
- Respec in the Activity list (2026-11-13, user): `Respec.js` prints "Respec: done, N skill points to spend (were M)." or "Respec: failed: ..."; `activity.go` turns them into a "Respec" / "Respec failed" entry (kind `respec`, rendered by the page, one in the dev demo).
