- Stopping a leader: the manager's bridge "kill" did not end Barabas's Game.exe twice on 2026-11-13 (status stuck on "stopping" after "could not confirm a save; stopping it anyway"); it had to be ended by its PID. Cause not found.
- Settings page (2026-11-13, user): AutoRespec greys out (disabled, dimmed) while AutoSkill is unchecked, `syncRespec()` in index.html; the server already ignores AutoRespec without AutoSkill (`p.Respec = p.AutoSkill && ...`). The page is embedded: loads after a manager restart.
- Skill allocator knows when skills open (2026-11-13, user: "the allocator determines: I know whirlwind is the skill with the most important points, but it's not available yet, so I will spend points elsewhere for now"): `AutoSkill.levelsAway(skill, count)` = character levels until the next point of a skill may be spent when only the level stands in the way (required level + points already in it - character level; prerequisites must be in place). `skillToAdd`: when a MAIN skill (the entry wants 5 or more points) is that close (at most `bankWindow` = 5 levels), the points are KEPT for it instead of going down the list (log: "AutoSkill: Whirlwind opens in N level(s): keeping the skill points for it."); further away they go down the list as before, one-point utility skills that are not open yet are just skipped. All templates get this; the Whirlwind template's entry is `satisfy = false` again (the earlier `true` was a blunt version of the same idea). UNTESTED in a game (no JS engine here); the arithmetic is the same as `skillCheck`.
- Gold sharing (2026-11-13, user idea; `libs/common/Gold.js`, hooks in `Follower.js` main loop and `Team.waitForTeam`): a bot in town with less than 400 gold (pack plus stash) says "needGold" in chat (Speech answer); every other bot in the same town with more than 600 + 200 gold gives once per request: half of what is above 600, 200 to 1000, taking it out of the stash when the pack lacks it, walking to the asker, `gold(amount)` to drop, then walking off to the portal spot. The asker picks up gold lying in town for 3 minutes after asking. Log lines "Gold: gave N gold to X." / "Gold: received N gold." become Activity entries "Gold Shared" / "Gold Received". If a gold pickup mod takes the dropped gold back for the donor the donor logs "problem: ... came straight back to me": then the mod must not pick up in town (the user offered to change it). No page setting yet. UNTESTED in a game.
- Auto Respec judgement (2026-11-13, user: "with autorespec on, you'll have to make some judgement about when the bot choose to use it"): `Respec.simulate` runs the real `AutoSkill.skillToAdd` on paper (hardPoints/added/banked swapped out and restored) with all the character's points at its current level; `Respec.judge` counts the points that sit somewhere the paper plan would not have put them. Respec when at least 6 are misplaced AND a quarter or more of the points AND the paper plan places 60% or more of them; or, with a hand-chosen build (`respecForce`), at least 3 misplaced. Not worth it: one log line an hour. docs/AUTOMATION.md "Auto Respec" says so. UNTESTED in a game.
- Gold from the stash (2026-11-13, user: "given plugy, you can remove gold from the plugy gold side"): `Gold.topUp(2000)` runs before `Town.doChores` in `Follower.doChores` and takes gold out of the bot's own PlugY stash (`gold(n, 4)` after `Town.openStash`) when the pack has less; donors already used the stash. The SHARED gold side is NOT reachable: PlugY.ini here has `ActiveSharedStashInMultiPlayer=0`, and Qolbot refuses to run bots with it on (botgame.go `plugyProblem`: two bots writing one shared file), so there is no shared stash or shared gold in the bots' TCP/IP games. UNTESTED.
- FOUND 2026-11-13 (user: "it looks to me like barabas isn't waiting in town for the party members"): the leader's game ended 20 s after the waypoint sync, every time, with "I am out of town portal scrolls. Leaving the game to restock in town." The leader's start-of-game path in default.dbj never ran town chores (followers do in Follower.js), so it never bought scrolls. Now default.dbj runs `Gold.topUp(2000)` and `Town.doChores()` once in town before `Team.wait()`. Together with the fillTome fix (single scrolls from 100 gold). UNTESTED.
- Sturdier leader (2026-11-13): `pickLeader` breaks a tie in the quests by FEWER CHICKENS since Qolbot started (`chickenCounts`) before the higher level, and the leveling detour uses `pickLeader` instead of the highest level: Barabas (lowest life, no usable attack skill) kept leading and chickening. The count lives in memory, so it starts again at every manager restart. In the binary from 12:00; the running manager has the old one.
- Gold requests repeat (2026-11-13): the leader asked the moment its game started, before the followers had joined, and nobody heard it (Barabas stayed at 35 gold while Xena gave 1000 to Sankt). `Gold.ask` now repeats every 25 s, up to 4 times per game.
- `Gold.settle(25000)` after `Team.wait()` in default.dbj (2026-11-13): a leader short of gold waits up to 25 s in town for its team to answer (it used to leave 5 s after the team arrived, before anybody could give). UNTESTED.
- `Gold.settle` buys town portal scrolls (`Town.fillTome(518)`) as soon as the gold has arrived (2026-11-13: Xena gave Barabas 1000 gold at 12:06:46, but his chores were over, so he still had no scrolls and Andariel/ForceSave failed with "makePortal: No scrolls"). UNTESTED.
- Gold.settle hold raised from 25 s to 45 s (2026-11-13 12:09): the donor was busy with her own chores and dropped the gold at second 27, after the leader had left; the gold lay on the floor and was lost when the game ended (about 3000 gold lost this way: Xena gave Barabas 1000 three times). Open: a donor cannot know whether the asker is still in town when it drops; a donation could be made only to a bot that is standing in the same town for at least N more seconds, or handed over through the stash page.
- TEST DAY 2026-11-13, state at 12:30 (user stopped it: "endless shutdown loop because barabas has no money and no scrolls"). The user confirmed the pickup mod does NOT pick up gold in town, so gold sharing works as designed: Xena gave gold 14 times (300 to 1000 each), Barabas received 1000 once and bought scrolls, but the whole team ran out of gold (Barabas 68, Sankt drained). Without scrolls the LEADER cannot make a portal: the waypoint sync ("sharing Spider Forest: makePortal: No scrolls"), Countess, Andariel and ForceSave all throw and the game ends after ~55 s, then restarts: an endless loop with no XP. Several donations were lost on the floor when the asker's game ended first. WHAT IS STILL OPEN: (1) the leader must have scrolls: pick a leader that has them, or do not start a run without them; (2) Qolbot does not know any bot's gold or scrolls (the save reader does not expose gold): bots should report them by RPC so the leader choice, the cards and a loop breaker can use them; (3) a loop breaker: N games in a row that end on "makePortal: No scrolls" / "cannot pay" should stop the team with a message instead of looping; (4) money runs (user idea). What worked live today: one-act-at-a-time travel, leader chores at game start, gold asking/giving/picking up, the respec judgement ("not worth it" lines), the leveling detour trigger and the farm (Countess killed in 13 s; Andariel reached).
- Fatal events (2026-11-13, user): `fatal.go`. A fatal event stops EVERY bot (`stopForNotReady`, leader last), adds an Activity entry (kind `fatal`, 🛑), and shows a RED BOX at the top of the Activity page (`/api/state` `fatal`: kind, title, text, bot, since) until it is dismissed (button, `POST /api/fatal/clear`) or a bot is started (`/api/start` clears it). Nothing restarts by itself afterwards. Two kinds today: `noscrolls` (a bot's log line "TownChicken: problem: I am out of town portal scrolls and I cannot pay for any ...", `fatalFromLog`; a bot that CAN pay just leaves the game to restock and that is not fatal) and `chickens` (the chicken/death limit of readiness.go; in Quest mode the first trip is the leveling detour, not fatal, the second one is). A repeat of the same kind within two minutes is ignored while the box is up. Unit-tested (`fatal_test.go`). Add more kinds in `fatalFromLog`.
- HUNTS (2026-11-13, user: "I would say build this, but no need to report it to the bot cards ... make the leader show Leading gold hunt, Leading waypoint hunt, Leading quest or Leading level hunt"): `levelup.go` is now both hunts. LEVEL HUNT = the old leveling detour (chicken limit trips in Quest mode, nobody playing). GOLD HUNT (new): `maybeGoldHunt`, checked every 30 s by `levelUpLoop` when no hunt runs: if every running bot has a fresh purse report and the team holds under `goldLow` (1000) gold per bot on average (pack plus stash), and no gold hunt ended in the last hour, the team restarts (`handOverWhy`) on the same easy Farm list until EVERY bot holds at least `goldGoal` (10000, `poorestGold`), with no time limit (user, 2026-11-13: "lasts until every bot has 10k at least"; "the threshold to start a gold hunt is 1k": read as 1000 per bot on average, not "any bot under 1000"); a chicken trip during a gold hunt turns it into a level hunt; if nobody has scrolls or the gold for any it is the fatal event `noscrolls` instead. PURSE FILES: `ToolsThread.js` writes `data/<profile>.purse.json` ({gold, stash, scrolls, at}) every 30 s in a game (`purse.go` reads it; the last known state stays when the bot is stopped). LEADER CHOICE (`pickLeader`) skips a bot that has no scrolls and under 150 gold (`purse.canLead`) unless nobody can. SCROLL FUND: `Town.needRepair` no longer spends the last 300 gold of a bot with fewer than 5 scrolls. Leader label: `State.Phase` = "waypoint hunt" from the log line "Waypoints: Syncing the team's waypoints" to "Waypoints synced" / "Starting script". Gold is NOT shown on the cards (user). NOT built: followers scooping up gold, because Config.LowGold is 0 (the pickit takes no gold) and the gold the bots do get comes from the pickup mod outside town. Unit-tested: labels and `canLead`; the hunts themselves are UNTESTED live.
- Gold donors (2026-11-13 13:38): reserve 1500 -> 600, least 300 -> 200. At the first run after the hunts were built Xena held 1708 gold and 30 scrolls but could not give Barabas (68 gold, 0 scrolls) anything, since only 208 was above her reserve; the fatal event `noscrolls` then stopped everybody at 13:37:47 (the follower's "cannot pay" line). The leader's `Gold.settle` now also holds in town while a teammate's request is pending and it has gold to spare. UNTESTED.
- Gold hunt BEFORE waypoint hunt (2026-11-13, user): the waypoint sharing at the start of a game needs a portal, so scrolls; a broke team failed or went fatal right there. (1) `huntAtStart` (levelup.go) runs when the user starts a Quest team (`/api/pickleader`, before any bot launches): from the purse files (any age) a team holding under 1000 gold per bot starts on a gold hunt, with a leader that can lead; if nobody can, the fatal event is raised and the start refused. (2) `plan.hunt` ("gold" or "level", from `m.levelUp`) makes default.dbj SKIP `Waypoints.sync()` during a hunt; it happens when the quests resume. UNTESTED live.
- Gold received (2026-11-13 13:41): Barabas got 500 gold from Xena in town, walked out with no scrolls, TownChicken left the game to restock and the unsaved 500 gold was lost with it (he was back at 68). `Gold.collect` now buys scrolls (`Town.fillTome(518)`) and calls `Saver.soon("received gold")` the moment gold arrives. UNTESTED. Also seen: the `noscrolls` fatal fired again at 13:42:07 on Barabas as a follower.
- Gold donors (2026-11-13 13:45, user): a bot with under 1000 gold (pack plus stash) gives NOTHING (`Gold.minToGive`); above that the old rule stands (half of what is above the 600 reserve, 200 to 1000). Read literally as a threshold to be allowed to give, not as a reserve; so a bot at 1208 still gives 300 and ends at 908. Say if the reserve itself should be 1000.
- Gold hunt clears the way (2026-11-13, user: "might make a gold hunt do full clears on their way to those bosses"): `plan.hunt === "gold"` sets `Config.HuntClear` (AutoSpec.apply); `Pather.moveToExit` then runs `Attack.clearLevel()` in every area it passes THROUGH (not the last one, which the boss script handles): Countess = Forgotten Tower and Tower Cellar 1-4, Andariel = Catacombs 3. Only for the gold hunt, not the level hunt. Slower per run, more kills and gold. UNTESTED in a game.
- Picking up gear to SELL (2026-11-13, user: "not sure how they are instructed to pick up gear for purposes of sale"): normally bots pick up only possible upgrades (armor, weapons, rings, amulets) and keeper items, and what is neither an upgrade nor a keeper gets sold afterwards; nothing makes them pick up gear JUST to sell it, because kolbot's rule for that (`Pickit.checkItem`: anything worth 10 gold per square while `me.gold < Config.LowGold`, result 4, sold by `Town.clearInventory` as "LowGold sell") was off (`Config.LowGold` 0). On a GOLD HUNT (`plan.hunt === "gold"`) AutoSpec now sets `Config.LowGold = 10000`. The rule is restricted to `Item.takeable` gear (so never a protected kind, which would be picked up and never sold) and gold. Gold itself comes from the pickup mod. UNTESTED in a game.
- Sell pickup refined (2026-11-13, user: rings and amulets are almost always good for gold, 10 gold per square is no help at higher difficulties): (SUPERSEDED by the GoldSell.nip entry below) on a gold hunt `Pickit.checkItem` picked up EVERY ring and amulet (item types 10 and 12) and other takeable gear only when its sell price (`getItemCost(1)`) is at least 100 (Normal) / 300 (Nightmare) / 800 (Hell); the per-square rule is gone. Keeper items: the leader (the only bot that loots while nobody plays) already picks up what the keeper rules keep and the chores stash it; the same on a hunt (user: "if a bot finds a keepable item in the course of a gold hunt, please pick it up for stash"). UNTESTED.
- GoldSell.nip (2026-11-13, user: "maybe make a nice NIP for Gold (might already be one)"; pickit/gold.nip only takes gold piles of 500 or more): `pickit/GoldSell.nip` is the list of what to pick up to SELL on a gold hunt: rings and amulets (magic and up), rare and crafted gear, magic gear of item level 60 or more. It is added to the LEADER's pickit lists only while a gold hunt runs (`writeSpec`), after every keep list. `Pickit.checkItem` turns a match whose `rval.line` starts with "GoldSell.nip" into result 4 (sell) when `Item.takeable(unit)` (never a protected kind), else 0; a keeper that matches an earlier list is kept as before because NTIP.CheckItem takes the first match. This REPLACES the hardcoded rules of the previous entry: `Config.LowGold` is back to 0 and the per-square branch is the original kolbot text. The syntax (`[quality] >= magic`, `[itemlevel]`, the type names) is kolbot's; if the file fails to load the log says so (`NIP file doesn't exist` / a parse error at start). UNTESTED in a game.
- Leader choice also prefers a bot with at least 4 scrolls (2026-11-13 13:58): after a manager restart Barabas (1 scroll, 448 gold) was picked as leader because `canLead` only asks for one scroll or 150 gold and the chicken counts had reset. Order now: quests behind, stocked with scrolls, fewer chickens, higher level.
- Leader choice order (2026-11-13 14:00): quests behind, stocked with scrolls (4 or more), more gold (steps of 500), fewer chickens, higher level, name. After a manager restart the chicken counts are gone and Barabas (higher level) kept being picked.
- GoldSell.nip first load (2026-11-13 14:00): d2bs.log said "Unknown property: itemlevel File: GoldSell.nip Line: 16/17" (the lines were skipped). In kolbot NIP the item level is `[level]` (the parser maps it to item.ilvl); fixed. CHECK d2bs/d2bs.log after the next start for "Unknown property ... GoldSell.nip".
- Using Quartermaster? (2026-11-13, user: the bots fill their packs with rejuvenation potions; Quartermaster is a mod whose chat command ".deposit" stores common items): Settings, Game has a checkbox "Using Quartermaster?" (`quartermaster` in qolbot.json, `Effective.Quartermaster`, default OFF; `/api/play` and `/api/state`); the PlugY checkbox is labelled "Using PlugY?" again. `plan.quartermaster` -> `Config.UsesQuartermaster` -> `Quartermaster.deposit()` (libs/common/Quartermaster.js) at the TOP of `Town.stash`, so before anything is stashed and even when nothing else needs stashing, at most every 20 s; it sends `say(".deposit")` and logs "Quartermaster: .deposit sent (N items before, M after)" (check that N drops). Town.stash only runs on a chores round, which every game's first town visit is. UNTESTED: that the mod reacts to say() the way PlugY does to /save. The server is stopped; nothing of this has run.
- Quartermaster on every town arrival (2026-11-13, user: "with that turned on it just means bots should run .deposit every time they are in town"): `Quartermaster.tick()` (Follower main loop every 2 s, `Team.waitForTeam`) sends .deposit once per arrival in town; `Town.stash` still sends it first (throttle 20 s). WHY THE PACK FILLED (user's question: why did they never offload those potions into the stash): kolbot never stashes potions on purpose: `Town.ignoredItemTypes` ("Items that won't be stashed") lists 76-81 (healing, mana, rejuvenation, stamina, antidote, thawing), `canStash` rejects them and `needStash` only looks at stashable items; the belt is where potions live; the pickit (QOL/Potions.nip) takes every rejuvenation potion, so with a full belt the extras stay in the pack and nothing sells or stashes them. Without Quartermaster a fix would be to remove 78 from ignoredItemTypes (not done).
- Quartermaster race (2026-11-13, user: "that mod might have a race condition issue if multiple bots write .deposit at the exact same moment"): bots arrive in town together, so each waits for its TURN before sending: `Quartermaster.turnDelay()` = (index of my profile in the sorted list of my profile and `Team.expected()`) x `slotMs` (4000) + a per-name offset under 700 ms, counted from the arrival (`arrivedAt`, ignored when older than a minute); it gives up when it leaves town meanwhile. Three bots: 0, 4 and 8 seconds. This only separates bots that know each other (data/bots.json); if the mod still misbehaves, the next step is a lock held by Qolbot (the bot asks over RPC, the manager hands out turns). UNTESTED.
- REJUVENATION POTIONS ARE STASHED (2026-11-13, user: "I def want rejuv potions to get stashed ... remove rejuvs from that no stash limitation"): item type 78 is out of `Town.ignoredItemTypes` (Town.js). Other users of that list: `Pickit.fastPick` (it now also fast-picks rejuvs, which the pickit rules decide anyway), the LowGold branch (off), MiniShopBot (unused), identify (no potions). Only the PACK is stashed (`Item.botItems`), the rejuvs in the belt stay. HOW POTIONS WORK (user's question): belt columns hp, mp, mp, rv (_DefaultConfig); `Chores.tune` sets MinColumn = half a column, HPBuffer = 4/2/0 spare healing potions in the pack for a 1/2/3+ row belt, MPBuffer = 2/1/0 (casters at least 1), PotionGold 100 below level 15 else 1000; spares above the buffer are used up in town (`Town.clearInventory`: items[0].length > HPBuffer); rejuvs have no buffer. PICKUP: QOL/Potions.nip takes up to 3 of each healing/mana kind that fits the level and up to 3 rejuvs (`[maxquantity] == 3`, `NTIP.CheckQuantityOwned` counts belt and pack); the Keeper tab checkbox `r_rejuv` (default true in keeperdefaults.json) writes `[name] == rejuvenationpotion` / `fullrejuvenationpotion` with NO limit into Generated.nip, which is what filled the packs (two sources for one thing: QOL's limited rule is never reached first). UNTESTED.
- REJUV RESERVE vs ACCUMULATION (2026-11-13, user: wants rejuvs both as an emergency reserve and as a pile that keeps growing; "I don't know how the bot would be able to differentiate"): a COUNT does it. `Town.rejuvCounts` (belt/pack/stash), `Town.rejuvTopUp` (below `Config.RejuvReserve`, default 4 = a full belt column, it takes the difference out of the stash with `Storage.Inventory.MoveTo`), and `Town.stash` keeps the first (reserve minus belt) rejuvs of the pack and stashes the rest. The belt is never filled by the bot (`Storage.Belt.MoveTo` does not know the columns and would put a rejuv in the healing column, which `clearBelt` would then take out again); pack rejuvs are drunk like the spare potions ('Drinking potion from inventory'). OPEN: with Quartermaster on, `.deposit` may store the reserve too, and the bots cannot take it back (no known .withdraw). Reserve size has no page setting. UNTESTED.
- Quartermaster has a WITHDRAW chat command (user, 2026-11-13), but the user does NOT want the bots to use it for now: do not add a withdraw call (not even for the rejuv reserve); the command's name is not known here. The reserve problem noted above (`.deposit` may store the reserve too) therefore stays open.
- Quartermaster safeguards (2026-11-13, user: ".deposit will instantly remove from their inventory all potions (as well as gems and runes): will the bot have an issue?"): GEMS AND RUNES are fine (bots never cube/craft/make runewords; keepers are picked up only to be stashed; quantity limits just let a bot pick up a duplicate again). POTIONS: (1) a bot with a belt of under 3 rows (`Storage.BeltSize() < 3`) does not deposit, because it keeps spare healing/mana potions in the PACK (`Chores.tune`: HPBuffer 4 / 2, MPBuffer 2 / 1) and would rebuy them at every town visit; (2) `Quartermaster.deposit` counts the belt's potions before and after and, if the belt LOST any, logs "Quartermaster: problem: .deposit also took potions out of my belt" and sets `Config.UsesQuartermaster = false` for the game; the log line now shows the belt count. (3) the rejuv reserve in the pack is deposited too (open, no withdraw by order). CHECK on the first live deposit: items before/after, belt potions before/after. UNTESTED.
- Quartermaster and ESSENCES (user, 2026-11-13: ".deposit will also remove essences"): no problem for the bots. The Pandemonium items (keys pk1-3, organs, the token, the four essences) are collected FOR THE PLAYER only (Keeper tab "Pandemonium"; `QuestItems.pandemonium`, `Pickit.safeQuestCodes`): followers never take them, `QuestItems.isProtected` keeps a bot from selling or dropping them, and no script uses them (the bots do not run Uber Tristram). After a deposit they sit in the mod's storage; the player takes them out with the mod.
- Quartermaster and internal tracking (user, 2026-11-13, after confirming the belt is not touched: would items vanishing confuse the bot's idea of which cell holds what?): there is no long-lived cell map. `Storage.Reload()` rebuilds every container from `me.getItems()` at the start of `FindSpot`, `Compare`, `MoveTo` and the other queries (Storage.js lines ~122, 228, 247); charm memory is keyed by signature and `Item.pruneCharms()` runs in every `Item.botItems()`; Layout.js places by what is in the pack now. The only hazard is a unit reference held across a deposit; `.deposit` is sent from the idle loops and the top of `Town.stash`, before the item list is built, in the main script's thread. CHECK after the first live deposit: errors right after the "Quartermaster: .deposit sent" line.
- Holy Grail lines in the Activity list (user, 2026-11-13: the mod reports new Holy Grail items in the chat, "it would be nice if those are captured in the event tracker"): `Quartermaster.listen()` (a `chatmsg` listener, installed by `tick`/`deposit` when Quartermaster is on) prints every chat line that matches /grail/i as "Grail: <text>" (colour codes removed); `activity.go` turns it into an Activity entry (kind `grail`, name "Holy Grail", key "grail:<text>" so the three bots' copies show once; page row with a trophy; one in the dev demo; unit-tested). UNKNOWN: the mod's real wording, and whether a SCRIPT hears it at all (a message the client prints locally may not arrive as a chat event). To find out, `Quartermaster.deposit` logs everything heard in the 1.5 s after `.deposit` as "Quartermaster heard: ..." (or "nothing in chat"). CHECK on the first live deposit, then adjust the match (a sample line from the user would settle it).
- Holy Grail wording (user, 2026-11-13): the mod says "Congratulations! You are __% complete on your Holy Grail quest." (with a number). It contains "Holy Grail", so `Quartermaster.listen`'s /grail/i matches; `activity.go` shows the entry's detail as just the percentage ("47% complete", `rePercent`; the full line stays in the bot's log as "Grail: ..."). Dedupe key = the whole line, so several bots hearing the same line show one entry; if each character gets its OWN message ("your"), bots at different percentages each show theirs. Still unknown: whether a script receives it as a chat event.
- HOLY GRAIL LISTENER REMOVED (user, 2026-11-13: "when I say that is a chat output it's not really a chat output -- in fact it might crash the bot"): `Quartermaster.js` has NO chatmsg listener and logs nothing it hears; the "Quartermaster heard" and "Grail:" lines of the previous two entries are gone from the script. The parsing in `activity.go` ("Grail: ..." -> a Holy Grail entry with the percentage) is kept, harmless, in case a safe way to get the text is found. WATCH: a bot that exits with code 0xC0000005 right after "Quartermaster: .deposit sent" would mean the mod's print itself crashes the bot (the game already exits that way when a bot quits a game, so compare the timing).
- QUARTERMASTER TEST 2026-11-13 14:49 (user: have a bot type .storage into chat and see what happens): new TESTING AID `POST /api/say?profile=X&text=.storage` (the bot's default.dbj `copyDataEvent` accepts `say:<text>` and does `say(text)`). Xena (alone in town) said ".storage": she did NOT crash or exit (still running minutes later); the mod answered with text drawn on the screen ('You have the following items:' ...) that is NOT in D2BS's print list (the bots' own lines, top left), shows for a few seconds only and is in no log, so a script cannot read it: (a) no crash seen for this kind of output, (b) D2BS does not report it. `.deposit` itself (and its Holy Grail line) was NOT tried in this test. Xena's belt has 2 rows, so my small-belt guard skips her deposits (no 'Quartermaster:' line).
- Small-belt guard REMOVED (user, 2026-11-13: turn the Quartermaster features back on; the grail progress cannot be detected, but the deposit works): every bot with "Using Quartermaster?" deposits on each arrival in town, whatever its belt (Xena and Sankt have 2 rows, Barabas 3). Accepted cost: a small-belt bot's spare healing/mana potions in the pack are deposited and bought again at the next chores. The belt-loss safety stays (the bot stops using .deposit for the game if its belt lost potions). The Holy Grail line stays undetectable on purpose (no listener, see above); the activity.go parser for "Grail: ..." is unused.
- FIRST LIVE DEPOSITS 2026-11-13 15:04 (Quartermaster, all three bots, no small-belt guard): `.deposit` worked and nobody crashed: Sankt 50 -> 30 items, Xena 66 -> 35, Barabas 56 -> 52 (me.itemcount before/after the 1.5 s wait). BUG FOUND IN MY CODE: the belt potion count read 0 for everybody because belt items report `mode === 2` (as Town.clearBelt's `me.getItem(-1, 2)` says) and not `location === 2`; so the belt-loss safety was blind and `Town.rejuvCounts().belt` was always 0 (the reserve kept 4 rejuvs in the PACK as well as whatever the belt held). Both now count `mode === 2`. Also: the gold hunt started at 15:04:36 (the team held 2685 gold, under 3000) and handed over to Xena.
- REJUV RESERVE vs QUARTERMASTER (2026-11-13 15:05): the top-up WORKED live ("Rejuvs: took 2 rejuvenation potion(s) out of the stash for the reserve (4 on hand)"), but with Quartermaster on every arrival in town deposits the pack's rejuvs again, so the top-up only shuffled potions from the stash into the mod's storage. `Town.stash` now skips `rejuvTopUp` when `Config.UsesQuartermaster` is on. The reserve then is whatever sits in the belt (the deposit leaves the belt alone: confirmed live, 8 belt potions before and after). Without Quartermaster the reserve/accumulation count policy works as described above.
- DEPOSIT CHURN COSTS REAL GOLD (2026-11-13 15:05): after each `.deposit` Xena's pack spares (HPBuffer 2 / MPBuffer 1 for a 2-row belt) were gone, the next chores bought them again, and she went from 1064 to 214 gold in one visit (about 850) while the team was on a gold hunt. `Chores.tune` now sets HPBuffer = MPBuffer = 0 when `Config.UsesQuartermaster` is on: a Quartermaster bot keeps no spare potions in the pack, the belt is its supply (it still refills the belt when a column is half empty). Takes effect at the next chores of each game. Trade-off to remember: a character with a 1-row belt (a low level one) has little supply without spares.
- QUARTERMASTER AND EMERGENCY TOWN VISITS (2026-11-13 15:25): during the Countess run Xena's walk was cut by a town chicken; the chores of that visit sent `.deposit` after waiting for its TURN (about ten seconds of the leader's slot in the team order), and as soon as she was back the script said "Failed to move to Countess" and went on to Andariel. Fixes: `tools/TownChicken.js` sets `Quartermaster.skip = true` (that thread never deposits), and the turn wait only applies in the first 120 s after the thread loaded the module (the team arriving together at game start); later visits send at once. Cause of the failed walk not proven, only the timing.
- ACTIVITY: CHARM WITHOUT STATS, AND "STASHED" WAS A PICKUP (user, 2026-11-13): (1) the entries the user saw as "stashed" (a Large Charm, a rejuvenation potion) were KEEPER PICKUPS: bot logs show "Picked up Large Charm (ilvl 15) (Generated.nip #76)" 15:28:07 and "Picked up Rejuvenation Potion (ilvl 1) (Generated.nip #48)"; there is no "Stashed" line in this run at all (and the "Successfully placed Rejuvenation Potion at X: 0 Y: 0" lines were the reserve top-up from the stash). So a rejuv is picked up as a keeper and `.deposit` takes it at the next arrival: as designed. (2) The charm's entry had no stats because it is picked up UNIDENTIFIED (the pickup text has no stats) and identified in town minutes later (`Town.js` keepVerdict logs "Kept <item>" with the stats), while `noteItemDesc` only accepted text within 20 s of the entry among the last 8 events. Now a later "Kept ..." line with a LONGER description updates the same bot's most recent entry of that item within the last hour (`botlog.go`, tested in `TestIdentifiedTextReachesAnEarlierPickup`). Needs a manager restart to load.
- GOLD DROP UNEXPLAINED (2026-11-13 15:33): the team went from about 5640 gold (15:21, after the first sales) to 3534 (15:33): Xena 4442 -> 1859. Her log shows "initNPC: reviveMerc" at 15:21:14, "buyPotions" at 15:21:10 and 15:24:58, and "Can't afford repairs" at 15:25: the mercenary revival is the likely big spender, NOT proven (the purse files keep only the latest value). `ToolsThread.js` now prints "Purse: <old> -> <new> gold (in town/in the field, N scrolls)" whenever the total (pack plus stash) changes by 300 or more between two reports (every 30 s): look for it in the bot logs next time. NOTE the scroll count jumped to 47 for Xena (a tome normally holds 20): scrolls on the ground are picked up by a keeper rule, or the tome is larger here; not checked.
- "Failed to move to Countess" again (2026-11-13 15:36, area 22 = Tower Cellar Level 2): not Quartermaster (the first one, at 15:25, followed a town chicken). After a fight the bot stood at 12521,6584 and three walks (to 12520,6600, 12636,6562, 12642,6555) each used up their 3 retries; the script gave up and the loader went on to Andariel. `Pather.moveToExit` now gives `moveTo` 8 retries instead of 3 while `Config.HuntClear` is on (a gold hunt), so `Pather.sidestep` (from the second failure on) gets more tries. The underlying walking problem (see the 2026-11-12 entries) is NOT solved. UNTESTED.
- Activity page alerts (user, 2026-11-13): the box above the bot cards that described a running hunt is GONE (`detourbox` removed from index.html); the ONLY alert at the top of the Activity page is the red fatal box ("the bots have turned off because of X"). The leader's card still says "Leading level hunt" / "Leading gold hunt" / "Leading waypoint hunt" / "Leading quest" (it reads `detour.label` from `/api/state`, which still carries the description). The grey CPU line is unchanged. Loads after a manager restart (the page is embedded).
- WHO PICKS UP WHAT (2026-11-13, user: "in any mode farm or quest, followers can continuously pursue their gold making agenda ... if there is a quest item all bots will free up the necessary space to pick it up, and the leader in a farm mode holding space for keeper items can still pickup gold making items but will drop what he needs to pickup the keeper"): (1) FOLLOWERS (`plan.noPickup`, i.e. nobody playing) used to take quest consumables only (`Pickit.questOnly`); now `Pickit.sellItem(item)` (= `checkItem(item).result === 4`, a GoldSell.nip match that is `Item.takeable`) is a second thing they take (the four questOnly checks in Pickit.js), and every follower loads `GoldSell.nip` (`specplan.go`: baselinePickit + GoldSell.nip); they sell it in their own chores (`Town.clearInventory` case 4), so each follower earns its own gold. (2) The LEADER loads GoldSell.nip ALWAYS now (it was only on a gold hunt). (3) Keepers beat gold items on the leader through the code that was already there: `Pickit.canMakeRoom` counts sellable pack items (results 0 and 4) as room that can be made and sends the leader to town to sell them (at most once a minute). (4) Quest items: unchanged, every bot makes room (`QuestItems.pickUp`). Not done: a follower never picks up keepers or upgrades while nobody plays; a follower whose pack is full of gold items only empties it on a town visit (it follows the leader there). UNTESTED live.
- LEADER AND GoldSell.nip, REVISED (user, 2026-11-13: the leader need not load the Gold NIP outside a gold hunt, it already picks up what might be a keeper and sells what is not; also mind the protect list): `specplan.go` loads GoldSell.nip for the leader ONLY while a gold hunt runs again; followers load it in every mode. The PROTECT LIST (Settings, Equipment) is already honoured: `Pickit.checkItem` turns a GoldSell match into result 4 only when `Item.takeable(unit)`, which requires `Item.isReplacable` (the protected kinds: unique, set, crafted, socketed, runeword, and rare/magic/ethereal when the user checks them), so a protected item is neither picked up for sale nor sold; the crafted lines of the file therefore do nothing while crafted is protected.
- INVENTORY DIALOG GOLD (user, 2026-11-13: "when I click on the bot cards in the activity screen, I don't see the big gold numbers you are reporting"): the dialog does show gold (`inv.gold` pack, `inv.bank` stash) but the report `D2Bot.inventory` was only sent when ITEMS moved (`reportInventory` signature), so the gold was old (Barabas 987 shown vs 1411 in the purse file, Xena 4784 vs 7660). `ToolsThread.js` now adds the gold in hundreds (pack and stash) to the signature, so a change of 100 or more refreshes the report (at most every 10 s). The team totals I quote (sums of the three bots) are shown nowhere. THE BIG SINK, SEEN LIVE: at 16:00:29 Xena talked to Hratli (the Act 3 smith) and her gold went 7660 -> 4793 (`Purse:` line at 16:00:57): a full repair of her gear costs about 2900 gold (an earlier drop of 2583 between 15:21 and 15:33 was probably the same); a mercenary revival costs a lot too (`initNPC: reviveMerc`). These two, not potions or scrolls, are the costs that can eat a gold hunt's income.
- FALSE ALARM OF THE BELT SAFETY (2026-11-13 16:09:17, Sankt): "Quartermaster: problem: .deposit also took potions out of my belt (8 -> 0)" came in the same second as Xena's third chicken and the level hunt's restart: the game was closing, `me.getItems()` reported no belt items while `me.itemcount` stayed at 22 before and after, so nothing was taken. The safety now also needs the item count to have fallen by at least as much as the belt did. The user's statement that .deposit leaves the belt alone holds (every other deposit: belt count equal before and after). The potion bills (about 2475 gold at 16:06) were NOT caused by it: that visit's `buyPotions` came at the start of a new game, BEFORE the game's first deposit.
- LEADER ORDER CHANGED (2026-11-13 16:14): quests behind, stocked with scrolls (4 or more), FEWER CHICKENS, more gold (steps of 500), higher level, name. Gold used to come before chickens, and Xena (the richest) was handed the level hunt right after chickening three times in seven minutes. The chicken counts are in memory (since the manager started). In the binary, not in the running manager.
- GOLD REQUESTS NOBODY HEARS (user, 2026-11-13: "they'll ask for help or gold and nobody is even in town to answer, they've moved on into the field"): `Gold.donorHere()` (Gold.js): a bot asks only when a teammate (`Team.expected()`) is in its own area (`getUnit(0, name)`) AND that teammate's purse file (`data/<name>.purse.json`, readable by every bot) says it has at least `minToGive` (1000) and more than reserve plus least; otherwise it does not ask at all (no chat, no ask counter used). `Gold.settle` (the leader's 45 s hold) waits for its own gold only while a donor is there, after a first 8 s grace for arrivals. The other request, "I cannot reach you, open a portal" (a follower in town while the leader is in the field), is answered from the field by the leader's TownChicken thread and was not changed. (An earlier version of this entry was written by mistake before the edit had been applied; the edit failed once and was redone and checked.) UNTESTED.
- SALES LOG THEIR PRICE (2026-11-13; Xena's pack gold went 1934 -> 10364 in one town visit and no line said why; the user had not touched the bots): `Town.clearInventory` (the two sell cases, junk and "LowGold sell") now prints "Sold <name> for N gold." (gold before and after the sale, after a short delay). The unexplained jump is still unexplained: no sale line existed then, the stash read 0, only a bot or the mod can have produced it.
- ACTIVITY: ITEMS WITHOUT STATS (user, 2026-11-13: where items are not ID'd but say Keeper, do not make them clickable; only when there are stats to see; mark them "Potential keeper"): `hasStats(i)` in index.html is true when the entry has a description that does not contain "Unidentified". An item entry (and an Auto Equip entry) is clickable only then; an entry without stats that says "Keeper" now says "Potential keeper". When the bot identifies the item in town, the later "Kept <name>" text (`noteItemDesc`, see the charm entry above) fills the entry in and it becomes a clickable "Keeper" again. Loads after a manager restart (the page is embedded).
- THE "I CANNOT FIND YOU, OPEN A PORTAL" SPAM (user, 2026-11-13: "I wonder if the portal thing is getting spammed ... they don't wait long enough between these things"): CONFIRMED from the logs: today's "SAY: I cannot find thee / Thou art beyond my reach" lines: Xena 78 (32 less than 60 s after the previous one), Sankt 74 (21), Barabas 11 (none). Causes: the follower asked every 45 s and `askedAt` restarts with every new game; the leader's TownChicken thread answered every 20 s, and each answer is `Pather.makePortal()`, a town portal scroll. Now: the follower asks at most every 120 s (`Follower.js`), the leader opens a portal for a teammate at most every 90 s (`TownChicken.js`). Cost: a follower waits longer in town when the leader has changed area. UNTESTED.
- PORTAL REQUESTS, SECOND LOOK (user, 2026-11-13: "instead of limiting the frequency we need to figure out why they aren't sticking together, or if momentary separations are fine"): from today's logs: 123 of 165 requests came MID-GAME with the requester in TOWN and its teammates in the field (only about 11 right after a game start). Two patterns: (1) Sankt: the leader moves on through the Tower Cellar, the follower goes to town "to meet up", tries `useWaypoint` to the leader's area (Tower Cellar levels have no waypoint), no portal is open, it asks: a normal momentary separation. (2) Xena: her own chicken/heal trip alone ("This prey may be beyond my ability...", "Follower: Going to town"), 15 s of chores, then "Taking waypoint to <leader's area> / Taking portal" every 5 s for over a minute: a TIGHT LOCAL RETRY LOOP while waiting for a portal (no chat; the chat request itself is every 45 s). The trips start with the followers' own chickens (low life: Sankt 190, Xena 274). My earlier 90 s limit on the leader's portals made the wait longer, so it is back to 25 s, and the follower's repeat request is 60 s (first one at once). NOT done: the retry loop itself (it should wait for the portal instead of spinning every 5 s) and the root cause (followers that chicken alone). UNTESTED.
- BACK THROUGH THE PORTAL I CAME BY (user, 2026-11-13: "if a bot goes to town to heal or clear inventory or repair, it's fine and he can come back thru the portal he took"): `Follower.js`: when a follower goes to town because the leader did ("Follower: Going to town.") it remembers `leftFrom = me.area` (and `leftAt`); back in town with the leader out in the field it first tries `Pather.usePortal(leftFrom, null)` (any portal that leads where it left: the leader's or its own, whichever the portal list has), then the old order (an exit, a waypoint it has, a portal leading where the leader is NOW, else it asks). Before, the only portal it considered was one that leads to the leader's CURRENT area, so a leader who had gone deeper made the follower spin (Xena: "Taking waypoint ... / Taking portal" every 5 s for over a minute). The TownChicken thread's own heal trips already return by their own portal ("Resuming"), unchanged. Not done: the 5 s retry itself, and the trip "Going to town to meet up" (a bot that is lost). UNTESTED.
- CHICKEN HISTORY SURVIVES A RESTART (2026-11-13 16:40): `chickens.go`: every chicken (RPC updateChickens) is appended with its time to `d2bs/kolbot/data/chickens.json` (git-ignored by /d2bs/kolbot/data/*.json), `loadChickens` reads it at startup, `chickenCounts` for the leader order counts the last 3 hours (`chickenMemory`), the file keeps 6. Before, the count lived in State.Chickens and a manager restart forgot it, so the richest bot was handed the lead again after three chickens. The file was seeded once by hand from the real "updateChickens" lines of logs/qolbot.log (last 3 hours); nothing was invented. Unit-tested (window, prune, restart).
- BUG: GOLD HUNT OF ONE (2026-11-13 16:37): right after the restart only the leader (Barabas, 34 gold, followers not started yet) was running; `maybeGoldHunt` took the running bots as THE team (average 34 gold < 1000) and started a gold hunt, restarting Barabas alone on the farm. Now it waits until `teamIsUp(running, m.expectTeam)` (every bot the leader was started for is running); tested (`TestTeamIsUp`). The same wait protects any start where the followers come a few seconds after the leader.
- PORTAL REUSE (user, 2026-11-13: scrolls are used up very fast; "a town event they ask first: is there a portal already? and take that one"; "a chicken portal is always fine to use their own scrolls"): `Pather.useTownPortalNear(maxDist)` (Pather.js): an open blue portal to this act's town that belongs to me or a party member (`getPortal(town, null)`) within `maxDist` (35) is taken instead of opening a new one; `Town.goToTown` tries it first (every caller: chores, regroup, quests, the follower's "lost, going to town to meet up" trips, 452 of them today, each of which opened its own portal and spent a scroll), `Town.reusePortals = false` in the TownChicken thread so its emergency/heal trips open their own portal as before. NOTE ON TERMS (user): a Life Chicken QUITS the game (`Life Chicken (...)` then the game exits); the town chicken (`Go to town and heal (chicken)`) is the portal trip. DESIGN STILL TO BUILD, in the user's words (discussion, nothing built yet): in BOT-ONLY runs only, when any bot goes to town (a portal trip, not a quit) ALL bots go to town; they wait there until all are ready (a file flag per bot rather than chat: data/<profile>.ready.json, so the "bots report what they're doing" switch does not matter); the quest leader waits 5 seconds after the others and goes through last; with a human playing only the bot that needs it goes. UNTESTED.
- PARTY GOES TO TOWN TOGETHER, BOT-ONLY RUNS (user, 2026-11-13: "if a bot portal chickens the other mates take his portal and they sync up in town before coming back"; "the quest leader waits 5 seconds before he goes through, making him the last"; "this bots-always-stay-together is only in bot only runs, in real player runs a single bot can leave and come back"): `libs/common/PartySync.js` + the TownChicken thread + `Town.visitTown`. FLOW: a bot starting a TOWN CHICKEN portal trip (the heal trip; a Life Chicken that quits the game is NOT one) calls `PartySync.call()` = writes `data/partycall.json` {id, from, area, at} and its own `data/<profile>.sync.json` {id, ready: false}. Every other bot's TownChicken thread, in the field, sees a fresh (under a minute) call from a party member (`PartySync.pending`), waits until no attackable monster is within 14 units (`safe`), pauses its script and runs `Town.visitTown(false)` with `Town.reusePortals = true` (it takes the caller's open portal if within 35 units, else opens its own). A bot already in town acknowledges at once (`ack`, ready). In town each does its chores, then `PartySync.barrier()` (called by `visitTown` before the return): writes ready:true and waits until every other bot of the game that is in my party (`Team.expected()` + `Misc.inMyParty`) has ready for the same id, at most 90 s; the LEADER (the bot with a plan goal) then waits 5 s more, so it is last. Return: \`Pather.usePortal(preArea, me.name) || Pather.usePortal(preArea, null)\` (a mate has no portal of its own there). Switch: \`plan.partySync\` = \`cfg.NotPlaying\` (Go: specplan.go), so a human's game is untouched. Cost/risks: any bot's heal trip now costs the whole party a town visit (Sankt with 190 life heals often); a mate that is in a different area than the caller opens its own portal (a scroll) and returns to its own area; the 90 s barrier waits for a mate that is dead or stuck. UNTESTED in a game, lives in the chicken thread (a bug there can quit bots): look for \"Party:\" lines in the bot logs.
- AUTOSTAT: NO ENERGY (user, 2026-11-13: "make sure these guys get a lot of vitality; in most cases investing in energy is a waste"): from the saves: Sankt (Necromancer 25) str 32 dex 25 vit 68 ENERGY 75 (50 over the base 25), max life 187; Xena (Assassin 26) str 35 dex 75 vit 75 energy 25, life 265; Barabas (Barbarian 27) str 55 dex 75 vit 75 energy 10, life 307. The templates' `Config.AutoStat.Build` had `["e", 50]` and `["e", 75]` in Javazon, Druid Summon, Necromancer Bone/PoisonNova/Summon and Sorceress Blizzard/FrozenOrb/Lightning/Meteorb/Meteor: those 2 lines are removed from all 10 (their points now go to the next entries: vitality and strength). Left alone: Hammerdin's `["e", 30]` (a little mana while leveling, on purpose). The melee templates (Bowazon, Kick, Frenzy, Throw, Whirlwind, Zeal) have `["d", 50]` and `["d", 75]` whose comments said "energy" (a copy-paste from the casters): `d` is DEXTERITY (AutoStat.js: case "d"); behaviour unchanged, comments corrected; whether those 55 dexterity points are worth it (Barabas and Xena both have 75) is open. Points already spent stay spent. UNTESTED.
- PLUGY STAT UNASSIGN (user, 2026-11-13: "using plugy you can subtract points from these things if you hold down alt ... same locations to press when you add points, just hold alt to subtract"): PlugY.ini here has ActiveStatsUnassignment=1, KeyUsed=18 (Alt); its tooltips say "+Alt: Unassign, +Shift: all remaining points". So AutoRespec could also give stat points back (Sankt's 50 energy points), not only skill points. NOT BUILT: the bridge posts window messages (`key`, `click`), and PlugY may read the real key state (GetKeyState), which posted messages do not change; it needs a test with a screenshot, plus the position of the stat buttons on the character screen (C). The skill respec has the same untested click.
- AUTORESPEC COVERS STATS TOO (user, 2026-11-13: "you would use autorespec as the governing setting to allow subtracting stat points, and you could readjust those if you determine that a char needs to be re-stated"): `Respec.maybeStats()` (called in Follower.doChores before AutoStat.init) works out on paper where AutoStat's own build (`Config.AutoStat.Build`) would put ALL the character's stat points starting from the class base (`Respec.baseStats`, by classid: str/dex/vit/energy; `judgeStats`), compares with the hard stats (`AutoStat.getHardStats`) and, when at least 10 points and a fifth of the points or more sit ABOVE the plan, asks Qolbot with `D2Bot.respecstat(stat, n)` to take them back: `rpc.go` -> bridge input `unassign x y tens ones` (opens C, Alt+Shift clicks of 10 points, Alt clicks for the rest, closes C). Once per character and build (`statsSpec` in data/<profile>.respec.json); needs `plan.respecStat` (= Auto Stat on + AutoRespec on + PlugY); the page greys AutoRespec out only when BOTH Auto Skill and Auto Stat are off. INERT UNTIL MEASURED: `statButtons` in cmd/qolbot/respec.go is empty, so the RPC refuses to click ("the position of that stat button is not known yet"), and it is NOT KNOWN whether PlugY accepts a posted Alt/Shift as held (no X11 headers here to send real key events). Test plan: a stopped team, one bot in town, a screenshot of C to measure the buttons, then Sankt (energy 75, plan 25 = 50 points to take back). Expected verdicts from the saves: Sankt yes (50 above), Barabas and Xena no (their dexterity 75 is in the template).
- SALES IN THE ACTIVITY LIST (user, 2026-11-13): "Sold <name> for N gold." (the line `Town.clearInventory` prints) becomes an Activity entry (kind `sale`, a coin icon, "N gold" or "sold" when the bot's gold had not updated yet); `activity.go` `reSold`, page row, dev demo, `TestParseEventSold`. - RESPEC PLUMBING BUGS FOUND LIVE (2026-11-13 17:51, Sankt's first stat check): (1) `D2Bot.respecstat is not a function` (and `D2Bot.respec` would have failed the same way): the D2Bot object in libs/OOG.js only has the calls written into it (zone, vitals, inventory ...); `respec` and `respecstat` are now defined there (sendCopyData with func and args). (2) `maybeStats` wrote its once-per-build record BEFORE acting and a failed attempt kept it, so Sankt's attempt was used up: it now does nothing, and does not record, while `plan.statButtons` is false (Go: `statButtonsKnown()`, true once a position is in `statButtons`), and only logs the verdict once an hour. Sankt's record file was deleted by hand (my own artifact). The verdict itself was right: Sankt 35/26/68/75 against the plan 55/25/99/25: 51 of 124 stat points above the plan (the energy).

- PARTY CALL ONLY FROM TOWN (2026-11-13 17:54, the first live "Party:" lines): Xena (17:54:02) and Sankt (17:54:04) each logged "Party: ... is going to town to heal: calling the team." and quit the game a second later: the heal trip and the Life Chicken (quit) fire in the same low-life moment, so a call made BEFORE the trip came from a bot that was already leaving (the party-membership check in `PartySync.pending` is the second line of defence). `TownChicken.js` now only sets `PartySync.wantCall = true`; `Town.visitTown` calls `PartySync.call()` right after `goToTown()` succeeded, when the bot is in town and its portal is open. Two Life Chickens in the same second also show how fragile Xena (274 life) and Sankt (190) are. UNTESTED.
- THE LEADER WAITS FOR MATES THAT QUIT (user, 2026-11-13: "I see barabas fighting in flayer jungle with other bots joining the game; he should be in town waiting for them"): `Team.separated` skips a mate that is not in the game at all, so after a Life Chicken (quit, Qolbot restarts the bot, it rejoins in town about a minute later) the leader questing on alone never noticed. `PartySync.missing(ms)` = mates of data/bots.json (the Wanted bots, restarting ones included) with no entry in `getParty()` for ms; the leader's TownChicken thread (bot-only runs, leader only, nothing hostile within 14, a town portal scroll) calls `PartySync.hold(names)` after 8 s: it goes to town (own portal), does its chores, waits until nobody is missing (3 minutes at most), 8 s more for them to load, and goes out again through its own portal; the mates, finding the leader in town, follow it out. At most one such wait every 5 minutes. UNTESTED live: look for "Party: X left the game; going to town to wait for them."
- PARTY SYNC, TWO BUGS FROM THE FIRST LIVE TRIP (2026-11-13 17:54-17:56): (1) Barabas went to town at 17:54:39 for a call that Sankt had made at 17:54:04 BEFORE quitting; the restarted Sankt (same name, joined by then) made the call look valid (fresh, caller in my party). (2) Barabas then waited the whole 90 s for Sankt, whose readiness file still said ready:false for that call and who never answered it (a bot does not answer its own call). Now every call and every readiness file carries the SESSION of the thread that wrote it (`PartySync.session`, set when the module loads in the town chicken thread of a game); `pending` accepts a call only if the caller's CURRENT file has the same session; `init` (at the start of each game, before the main loop) writes {id: 0, ready: true, session}; `ack` also answers a call made by my own older session. UNTESTED.
- BLADE OF THE OLD RELIGION, "Unable to open chest" (2026-11-13 17:56): the log said `chest undefined at undefined,undefined`: while `Quester.pickAndPlunder` cleared the area before the altar the leader was taken to town and back (the party trip, 17:54:39 to 17:56:25), and the `chest` unit it held was invalid after the area change. It now fetches the chest again (`getUnitHelper(2, object_id)`) before each try and when `chest.x` is undefined. The quest function's own 3 attempts remain. UNTESTED.
- STALE UNITS ACROSS A TOWN TRIP, THE GENERAL PROBLEM (user, 2026-11-13: "that bug you're fixing on the altar might be something relevant to other quests"): an audit of Quester.js, QuestScripts.js, QuestNpcs.js and QuestItems.js (a script that fetches a unit, runs something long, then uses the unit again) found 13 candidates; those in TOWN cannot be hit (the town chicken's party trips only start in the field); the ones in the field are `pickAndPlunder` (the chest, fixed with a re-fetch) and `equipAndAttack` (the object hit five times after a walk: re-fetched after the walk). GENERAL PROTECTION: `PartySync.busy(ms)` writes data/<profile>.busy.json; `pickAndPlunder` and `equipAndAttack` say "busy for 60 s" and the town chicken thread's NON-emergency trips (answering a party call, the leader waiting for mates) check `PartySync.isBusy()` and wait; a low-life heal trip never waits. Other quest sequences in the field that hold units (the Quester's own clearing, `cubeAndCreate`, `keyAndKeyhole`) are not marked yet. UNTESTED.
- FIRST FULL PARTY TRIP, LIVE (2026-11-13 17:58:43-17:59:07): Barabas called the team after reaching town; Sankt and Xena went through his open portal ("Town portal: using one that is already open"), did their chores, all marked ready at 17:59:01, the mates went back out and the leader last at 17:59:07: about 18 s from call to ready. FLAW: the leader leaves town 5 s after the mates, so the Follower in the field saw "the leader is in town" and followed it back ("Follower: Going to town." 17:59:04, "Taking portal" x3). Fix: `PartySync.barrier` of a non-leader writes `data/<profile>.afterparty.json` {until: now + 25 s}; `Follower.autoFollow` returns early when `leaderInTown && !me.inTown && PartySync.afterParty()`. (Follower.js also includes PartySync.js now.) UNTESTED.

- PartySync.barrier no longer waits the full 90 s for a mate that never answered the call (it is out fighting): after 40 s only mates that answered are waited for (seen 2026-11-13 18:08: Xena waited 96 s for Barabas). Untested script change: loads at the next game start.

The leader-hold check below it still uses `safe()`. Untested script change, loads at the next game start.

- Town.identify logs "Town.identify: nothing to identify or sell among N item(s): <name (id/unid) (protected) rN>" (at most every 2 minutes) when it decides to skip the vendor, so a bot that "never checked its gear" can be diagnosed from the log (user, 2026-11-13, about Barabas). - Town.identify now also visits the vendor for an IDENTIFIED item the pickit says to sell (result 4, the GoldSell list): before, an identified item only triggered the visit when `Config.LowGold > 0` (it is 0), so gear Cain had identified for free was never sold (user, 2026-11-13: Barabas carried gear he could have sold and did not check it). Untested script change.

- Gold hunt grace (2026-11-13, user: do the sell chore before the gold run): the hunt is judged from the LAST session's purse files, before the new session's start-of-game chores (identify + sell, see the Town.identify entries) turn gear into gold. Between 90 s and 5 min (`huntGrace`) after a hunt starts, if the team's gold is back at `goldLow` per bot, the hunt ends ("not needed"). Needs a manager restart; the running manager still has the old rule.

Most likely PlugY reads the real Alt/Shift state, which posted window messages do not set. The button positions are measured but unproven. The attempt also wrote `Sankt.respec.json` as "done"; deleted by hand so he retries. Open: a real key event (XTEST on the bots' X display from the manager) or a PlugY setting.

(Settings, Game tab; `qol`; user request 2026-11-13): when on, every Auto Respec uses the chat commands of the user's own mod instead of PlugY clicks: `Respec.maybe` says ".reset skills", `Respec.maybeStats` says ".reset stats" (one command, no button positions; the plan's `statButtons` is true under QOL). Auto Respec now works with PlugY OR QOL (`plan.respec`/`respecStat` in specplan.go).
