- Settings, Game tab: the product names in "provided by PlugY / Quartermaster / QOL" are links (PlugY site, the Quartermaster and QOL repos), user request 2026-11-13.

- Waypoint sync wasted its time (2026-11-13 19:10, user: "they are cycling through all the waypoints but nothing is collected"): `Waypoints.has` for the bot itself read the live `getWaypoint`, which is false for EVERY waypoint until the waypoint menu has been opened in this game. The menu did not open (silently), so the leader looked like it lacked 23 waypoints it already had (its saved list was complete) and the team visited each for nothing. Now `has` falls back to the saved list while the menu is not loaded, and `touchTown` logs "the waypoint menu did not open". The Activity entry for a helper's portal is now "Waypoint Portal" with the area name (was "Quick Travel | waypoint3"); "Waypoint Collected" stays for real collections. Untested script change.

- AutoSkill bank rule fixed (2026-11-13, user: Darius's skill points made no sense): a skill's next point needs one more character level than the last, so a main skill that "opens in a level" can take ONE point, not the 17 Darius held (Blessed Hammer, level 24; he had 8 single points and 17 unspent). Now only one point is kept back; the spare ones go to the next entries of the build. (`this.remaining > 1` in `AutoSkill.skillToAdd`; paper simulations, where `remaining` is 0, still hold.) Untested script change.
- Leaders now run Auto Respec too (`default.dbj`, before AutoSkill.init / AutoStat.init): only the Follower script called `Respec.maybe/maybeStats`, so a leader (Sankt, 50 stray energy points) never judged. `Respec.js` includes Throttle and Plan itself now.
- Blade of the Old Religion (2026-11-13 19:19): COMPLETE after the script talked to Ormus and Asheara first (bits 0010101010000000 -> 1010101110000100). The user's guess was right: the quest was already partly done (the altar gave nothing because the Gidbinn had been found and returned); the old order tried the altar first and threw.

- Followers now finish their own quests (2026-11-13, user: "only the leaders are completing quests"): the Follower's first town chores of each game call `Quester.runQuests(true)` (the town-only mode: only NPC talks, every quest script keeps its field work behind `!is_town_only`) for the quests the bot has not completed; Quest mode only, once per game (`libs/bots/Follower.js`). Before, a follower talked to a quest NPC only when a reward was "waiting" (`Quester.questNpcs`), and a quest like Blade of the Old Religion has none waiting: Xena stayed "not done" while Sankt completed it (19:19) and only finished on her own run (19:21). Untested script change; expect an extra minute or so in town at the start of a follower's game.
- `.reset stats` (QOL, 2026-11-13 19:20-19:21, Sankt): sent twice with say(), the unspent stat points stayed 0 and the plan comparison did not change (34/26/73/75 both times), although the user typed it by hand and it works. Cause unknown: the mod may need something the bot does not give (a place, a wait, other wording), or the answer takes longer than the 10 s the bot waits.

- Follower quest talks narrowed (2026-11-13, user: "not needlessly talk to every NPC, just the ones it needs to when a quest is incomplete"): `Quester.runQuests(true)` from the Follower (`Quester.townStartedOnly`) now only runs the town part of a quest that has STARTED for this character (`getQuestProgress` above 0, below 100: the game has set some state bits) and only once per progress value (`Quester.townQuestSeen`). It runs on every town visit that `Chores.needed` flags ("quest talk": a started, incomplete quest of this town's act whose state is new; re-checked at most every 4 minutes, `Quester.townQuestsAt`). Replaces the once-per-game rule written earlier today. Blade of the Old Religion read `0010101010000000` (started) before the talk that completed it. UNTESTED: no JS engine here; watch the next follower games for "Quester: quest talk" / "is complete" lines.

- `.reset stats` diagnosis (2026-11-13): `.deposit` reaches the mod through the same `say()` (24 of 327 deposits changed the item count, e.g. 50 -> 30), so the chat path works. The bot only watched the unspent point count after `.reset stats`; `Respec.maybeStats` now also prints the stats (str/dex/vit/energy, unspent) before, every 3 s and after, and counts a drop of the base stats as success. Sankt's record deleted so he tries again at his next game start. Untested script change.

- The QOL mod's `.reset stats/skills` now works from any bot (2026-11-13): the old version only ran in the process that hosts the game (a TCP/IP client has no server thread). Changed in the QOL mod's own source (the user's mod; `src/qoltweaks.c`, built to its `build/qol.dll`, NOT installed: the user copies it into the game folder). Qolbot needs no change. The failed tries on Sankt (19:20, 19:21) were that limit. Untested in a game.

- Holy Grail on the Activity page (2026-11-13): `cmd/qolbot/grail.go` reads the mod's own file `<game folder>/quartermaster_grail.txt` (read-only) every 30 s while "Using Quartermaster?" is on and posts a "Holy Grail" entry for each NEW find: `<item> (unique|set) · N of M (P%)`, with the character that found it (the file has `kind|index|name|found on|found by`). The first look only sets the baseline; more than 5 new at once (a `.grail scan` import) is one line. This replaces the plan to read the mod's "Congratulations!" message, which is a client-side print no bot can read (and listening for it might crash a bot): `Quartermaster.js` still has no listener. Fixing the mod's reply printing (they go to the host's window) is NOT needed for this. Test: `grail_test.go`. Not yet seen live: it needs a new find (or an edit of the file) after a manager restart.

- Quartermaster replies to the sender (2026-11-13): the Quartermaster mod's source, `srv_reply` (see its NOTES.md section 14). Server-thread replies (deposit, take, spawn, grail, scan) now go to the player who typed the command as a chat line from "Quartermaster" through the game's own chat routine, instead of printing on the host's screen. Built to its `build/quartermaster.dll`, NOT installed (the user copies it). UNTESTED: the user wants to see whether the replies crash the bots; if a bot crashes after a `.deposit`, this is the first suspect. Monitoring is off on request: no Monitor is running.

- Grail tab (2026-11-13, user request; shown only with "Using Quartermaster?"): `/api/grail` (`grail.go`, `apiGrail`) reads the mod's `quartermaster_grail.txt` read-only and returns every item (`kind|index|name|found on|found by`) plus the found/total of the file's own header (the mod's numbers are the ones the game shows). The page (`index.html`, tab `grail`, `/grail`): a big percentage, a bar, filters (all/found/missing, unique/set, search) and the table, newest finds first. Unique items are gold, set items green (`--uni`, `--set`, light and dark); missing ones are faded. Refreshes every few seconds while the tab is open. Tests: `grail_test.go`. Not yet looked at in a browser (the colours were added after the first deploy: a manager restart shows them).

- Rule check "a bot only touches items it picked up itself" (2026-11-13, user): FINDING: the code only has a mine-or-yours test for charms, tomes, keys, the cube and quest items (`Item.userOwned`, charms via `data/<profile>.charms.json`, which is `[]` for every bot today). Every other item in a pack is identified, sold or stashed unless its KIND is protected (Settings, Protect: `Item.isReplacable`). So the rule is NOT enforced for gear. AUDIT ADDED (logging only, nothing blocked): `Misc.notePicked` writes every pick-up as `classid|quality|ilvl` to `data/<profile>.picked.json` (last 600), and `Misc.auditTouch` (called from `Misc.itemLogger`, so at every "Sold" and "Stashed") prints `Audit: Sold|Stashed <name> (quality N, ilvl N): picked up by me in this game | earlier (the same kind of item is in my record) | NOT IN MY PICK-UP RECORD <-- check`. Gambled items are skipped. Use: grep the bot logs for `<-- check` (items touched with no record: yours, bought, replaced starting gear, or from before the record began), then decide whether to enforce the rule (only touch what is in the record). Untested script change; the record starts empty, so early lines will say "check" for items from earlier games.

- Protection by character level (2026-11-13, user: "the protection list is level limited to X and above ... the level of the character"): Settings, Game tab, Equipment: "Protect these items only once the character is level [X] or higher" (`protect.minLevel`, 0 = always, the old behaviour). Below X nothing on the protect list is protected: `Item.isReplacable` (Misc.js) returns true, so a low-level bot may swap its early uniques, rares and socketed gear for better ones and sell or stash what comes off (every unique and set is kept by the Keeper-tab rules and goes to the stash; the rest follows the pickit rules; a SOLD socketed item takes its gems and runes with it). Class-id protection (Khalim's Will) applies at every level. From level X on the list works as before. The audit lines (`Audit: Sold|Stashed ...`) show what happens to the old pieces. Untested script change; the page needs a manager restart, the script loads at the next game start. `spec.Protect.MinLevel`.

- "Sold X for 0 gold" (2026-11-13: 7 such lines, e.g. Ring, Amulet, Bone Wand): the sale was printed 150 ms after `item.sell()`, before the server's gold update arrived. The three sale sites in `Town.js` (identify case 4, clearInventory, LowGold) now wait up to 1.5 s for the gold to change before printing. Qolbot's "sale" Activity entry reads that number, so it should show real prices now. Untested script change (loads at the next game start).

- Gems and Runes tabs (2026-11-13, user request; shown with "Using Quartermaster?", next to Grail): `/api/storage` (`grail.go`, `apiStorage`) reads the mod's `quartermaster.txt` read-only (`rejuv_halves=N`, `<code>=<count>`). Runes tab: a big total, "N of 33 kinds", the 33 runes El..Zod (`r01`..`r33`) with counts, filter All/Have/Missing. Gems tab: a big total and a table of Amethyst, Topaz, Sapphire, Emerald, Ruby, Diamond, Skull by grade Chipped..Perfect (codes `gcv gfv gsv glv gpv` per colour; the flawless amethyst is `gzv`; skulls `skc skf sku skl skz`). Refreshes every few seconds while open. Test: `TestParseStorage`. Not yet seen in a browser. Also: the grey note under the Equipment level setting was removed (user request).

- A SET ring was sold for 2154 gold (2026-11-13 20:36, Darius, `Audit: Sold Ring (quality 5, ilvl 75)`). Two causes: (1) the user had set the protection level to 70, so for level 24-27 bots nothing on the Equipment list protected sets or uniques any more; (2) `GoldSell.nip` matched rings and amulets with `[quality] >= magic`, which includes set (5) and unique (7), and the FOLLOWERS' pickit lists were only the baseline plus GoldSell.nip (no Keeper-tab rules), so a follower had no idea a set ring was a keeper. FIXED: GoldSell.nip rings and amulets now only match magic, rare and crafted; and followers also load the Keeper tab's generated rules (`generatedNIP`, before GoldSell.nip) in `specplan.go`, so every unique and set (`[quality] == unique/set`) is a keeper for them too (they stash it instead of selling it). A follower still PICKS UP only quest consumables and GoldSell items (questOnly). Correction to an earlier statement: "every unique and set is kept and stashed" was only true for the leader. Needs the manager rebuilt and the team restarted (done).

- Storage tab (2026-11-13, user request; shown only with "Using Quartermaster?"): one top-level tab at `/storage` with sub tabs Grail, Gems, Runes, Essences, Potions (`/storage/grail` ...; the first versions' `/grail`, `/gems`, `/runes` still redirect there). Essences: the four boss essences (`tes`, `ceh`, `bet`, `fed`) from `quartermaster.txt`. Potions: Rejuvenation Potions only (`rejuv_halves`: a full one is two halves, a small one is one), since `.deposit` stores nothing else of the kind. Same pattern as the Settings, Keeper and About sub tabs (`stsub`, `stsubbar`). The page script was edited without a JS engine to run it: if a tab misbehaves in the browser, the pre-change page is the scratchpad copy `index.html.before-storage`.

- Hand-me-downs (2026-11-13, user reminded: a gear piece replaced by an auto upgrade is dropped so other bots may try it on): the rule EXISTS in `Item.equip` (the bot's own swap) and `Item.mercEquip` (the mercenary's): the piece that comes off goes to the stash/pack when a keep rule wants it (pickit result 1 keeper, 2 cube, 3 runeword, 5 craft), else it is DROPPED on the ground; the town chore that looks at gear lying nearby (`Pickit.pickItems` in `Town.doChores`) is the receiving side. Barabas's merc armor (a Quilted Armor, 20:28) was stored and sold an hour later instead of dropped: at the moment it came off some keep rule said "keep" (cube/runeword/craft base?), later it was junk (r0). Both paths now print `Equip: took off <item>: keep rule result N ...: stored|dropped for the others`, so the rule that stored it shows. Untested script change. Open: a dropped piece the others do not want lies in town until the game ends (nobody sells it): fine for the "never sell what you did not pick up" rule.

- Storage sub tabs are ordered Grail, Runes, Gems, Potions, Essences (user request). Needs a manager restart to show.
- Storage tables (Runes, Gems, Potions, Essences): the count columns and their headings are right-aligned (user request). Needs a manager restart to show.
- AutoRespec note on the Automation tab now reads "(only works when using QOL)" (user request). The code still lets a bot respec with PlugY alone (plan.respec = autoSkill && autoRespec && (plugy || qol)); only the label changed.

- Better hunt places (2026-11-13, user: "tombs would prob be better in Normal"; "make sure farms list has it"): `Tombs` (Kolton's script: the seven tombs of Tal Rasha from the Canyon of the Magi, waypoint 46) is now in the farm catalog (`farmAll`, act 2, "Tal Rasha's Tombs"), so it is in the farm list on the page, and the Normal gold and level hunts run `Tombs, AncientTunnels, Countess, Andariel` (was Countess, Andariel; `levelUpFarm`, an untested guess: the monsters of the tombs and tunnels are far higher level than the act 1 bosses, so better drops and jewelry, but the casters have 190-330 life and may chicken more: watch the chicken log). `huntpay.go` logs what every farm place pays: `hunt pay: Tombs 12m: +3400 gold (283/min); Tombs so far: 2 run(s), 25m, +6100 (244/min)` (net team gold from the purse files, leader's "Starting script" and "Run duration"). Use those lines to drop the worst place from the list. Tests: `TestLevelUpFarm`, `TestPerMinute`.
- Runes sub tab (Storage): three columns, Low (El-Dol), Mid (Hel-Ist), High (Gul-Zod) (user request); one column on a narrow window. Needs a manager restart to show.
- Rune colours (Storage, Runes): Low orange, Mid red, High purple (names and group headings; light and dark shades; a rune with none stored is faded). User request. Needs a manager restart to show.

- Xena's "Diablo II Error" loop (2026-11-13 21:12-21:30): from 21:12 every game she joined showed a modal "Halt / Location: , line #151 / Expression: Unrecoverable internal error 6fdbbf67" about 2 s after the "Quest items:" line (screenshot taken from the bots' hidden display, `import -display :N -window root`, N from `/proc/<pid>/environ`). 6fdbbf67 is D2Common (base 6fd50000, RVA 0x6bf67): a txt-table accessor (0xF0-byte rows) halting on an out-of-range id (line 0x97 = 151). Not the map files (deleted, it came back), not her character file (checksum fine), not unspent points (none), not a new equip. UNEXPLAINED; her items/merc/party state are the suspects. Two fixes made on the way: (1) the manager now restarts a bot on an error dialog once per game PROCESS (pid) and not once per minute (a second dialog 31 s after the first left her frozen behind it); (2) `/api/stop` on a bot frozen behind such a dialog hangs in "stopping" (the bridge's kill does not end it): end it by PID (`kill <pid>` of that Game.exe, found by its command line), never `pkill -x Game.exe`. Xena is left STOPPED; the team runs as Barabas, Sankt, Darius. Her map files were copied to the scratchpad (`xena-map-backup/`) and deleted at the user's word (see the memory "map-files-deletable").
- Runes page: the Low/Mid/High column headings are plain (only the rune names are coloured); 20 px of space above the columns, like the Grail page (user request). Needs a manager restart.
- The Quest Progress button moved from the Bots page to the Activity page, under the bot list (user request). It uses the bots checked on the Bots page when two or more are checked, else the running team; shown in Quest mode with two or more bots and nobody playing. Needs a manager restart.
- Quest Progress button on the Activity page did nothing: its dialog (pabox) sat inside the Bots page container, which is hidden on the Activity tab, so showModal() showed nothing. The dialog is now a top-level element before the Activity pane. Needs a manager restart.

- Catacombs instead of Andariel on gold hunts (2026-11-13, user request): new farm run `Catacombs` (`libs/bots/Catacombs.js`, act 1 in the farm catalog): waypoint to Catacombs Level 2, `Attack.clearLevel` there, then Levels 3 and 4 (`moveToExit` 36, 37) cleared the same way; Level 4 holds Andariel's room, so she dies with the rest. The Normal hunt list is now `Tombs, AncientTunnels, Countess, Catacombs` (Andariel stays in the catalog for anyone who picks it). Level 1 is not cleared (the waypoint is on Level 2). Untested script (no JS engine); `hunt pay:` lines will rate it against the others. Test: `TestLevelUpFarm`.

- GOLD HUNT PLACES, the new rules (2026-11-13, the user; replaces the fixed "Tombs, Ancient Tunnels, Countess, Catacombs" list for GOLD hunts; level hunts still use `levelUpFarm`): `huntplaces.go`.
  * No gold hunt while the weakest bot is below level 10 (`huntMinLevel`); the goal is 500 gold per level of the weakest bot, at most 10,000 (`huntGoal`).
  * Full clears only: the leader runs `ClearAnyArea` (plan `huntAreas` -> `Config.ClearAnyArea.AreaList`, AutoSpec.js) over the chosen levels.
  * Act rule: only acts BELOW the team's highest act (the highest act every bot has reached, from the town waypoints in `data/waypoints-<bot>.json`): highest 3 -> acts 1 and 2; in act 5 nothing lies above, so it counts itself.
  * In ACT 1 (no lower act, no waypoints to rely on): whole zones from town, Blood Moor, Cold Plains, Stony Field, Dark Wood, Black Marsh in order, as deep as is safe, skipping zones far below the weakest bot (`act1Zones`).
  * Past act 1 the candidates are the user's picks: the Countess's tower (Tower Cellar 1-5) and the Catacombs (2-4); the Ancient Tunnels and Tal Rasha's tombs; Travincal and Mephisto's lair (Durance 2-3); the Chaos Sanctuary; the Worldstone Keep (levels 2-3). Each starts at a waypoint EVERY bot has, then `Pather.journeyTo` walks the neighbouring levels.
  * Safe: every level's monster level (Levels.txt, expansion column of the difficulty) at most `huntSafety` (4) above the weakest bot. Best: density x monster level, from the game's own Levels.txt (read only). The best TWO places that qualify are cleared one after the other.
  * Real data, Normal, highest act 3: weakest level 10 -> Catacombs + the tower; 20 and up -> Tal Rasha's tombs (2200 x 17) + Ancient Tunnels.
  * The pay lines (`hunt pay:`) are now per place. Next refinement (not done): replace the game's numbers with the measured pay once there is some, and drop places that chicken. Untested in a game: `Catacombs.js` and the area lists through ClearAnyArea. Tests: huntplaces_test.go (`TestRealHuntChoice` runs on the real files when `QOLBOT_GAME_EXE` is set).

- Stash full, graceful stop (2026-11-13, the user: PlugY.ini allows one page of the personal stash, so "/insertpage" fails and that is fine, the bots must shut down gracefully): `Town.stash` no longer carries on after a failed page. When an item has no room (and the cube, the other extra storage, is full too) it asks for a page ONCE; if the item still does not fit (`Storage.Stash.MoveTo` fails) it remembers (`Town.noMorePages`) and calls `Town.stashFullStop`: prints `Stash is full: no room for <item> and no page can be added (PlugY allows one page): stopping.`, `D2Bot.stop(profile, true)` (stops that bot with a save). Qolbot reads the line (`fatal.go`, kind `stashfull`) and stops the whole team the way the Stop button does, with a red box "A stash is full" on the Activity page (dismiss it or start a bot to clear it). Untested in a game (needs a really full stash); `TestFatalStashFull`.

- Hunt places LEARN (2026-11-13 23:0x, overnight): the Ancient Tunnels paid -72 gold/min over two runs (Barabas chickened twice there: repairs, potions, scrolls), the Countess -12/min. `huntpay.go` now keeps a tally per place (runs, time, net gold, chickens during its runs) in `logs/huntpay.json` (survives restarts; a tally older than 24 h is forgotten) and `badPlace` calls a place bad after two runs at less than -30 gold/min or three chickens; the gold hunt chooser (`chooseHuntPlaces`, a `skip` function) and the level hunt list (`levelUpFarm`) skip bad places. A gold hunt now clears ONE place per run (`huntPlacesPerRun = 1`) so that pay and chickens belong to a place. Seeded tonight with the two tallies above. Tests: `TestBadPlaceAndSkip`. Chickens are counted in `rpc.go` (`huntPayChicken`).
- A gold hunt gives up a place that goes bad DURING the hunt (levelUpLoop, every 30 s: placeBad -> pickHuntPlace again; the next game start, which a chicken brings, runs the new place). Before, the hunt kept the first place to its end (the tombs, 2026-11-13 23:20).
- payKey: a level hunt's script name (AncientTunnels) and a gold hunt's place name (Ancient Tunnels) now share one tally key (huntpay.go payKey/payAlias, old files fold in on load): the first version let the hunt pick the Ancient Tunnels right after they had been called bad. Test: TestPayKey.

- Gold hunt EQUALIZING (2026-11-14 02:00, overnight): in the Catacombs hunt the leader (Sankt) looted most of the gold: Sankt 18,737, Xena 6,984, Darius 6,094, Barabas 3,703 (team 35,500) and the hunt ends only when EVERY bot holds the goal (10,000 here), so Barabas's trickle (+580 in 30 min) could keep it going for hours. Now (`Gold.js`): during a hunt (`Config.HuntGoal` from the plan's `huntGoal`, AutoSpec.js) a bot BELOW the goal asks (`ask`: total < goal instead of < 400) when a donor with a surplus is in town (`donorHere`), and a bot ABOVE it gives everything above the goal, at most 3000 per request (`help`, `floor`); outside a hunt nothing changes. Untested in a game. The restart that deployed it ended the running hunt (the team average, 8,900, was above the 1,000 broke threshold, so no new hunt started) and the team went back to the quests.
- Morning summary of the night's hunt data (logs/huntpay.json): Catacombs 13 runs, +23,063 gold in 130 min (176/min), 2 chickens; Tal Rasha's Tombs 1 run -1,285 (8 min), 4 chickens; Ancient Tunnels 2 runs -608 (8 min), 2 chickens; Countess 1 run -39. The fragile bots (200-350 life) lose money in the dense Act 2 dungeons; the Catacombs pay.
- badPlace: chickens alone no longer condemn a place that clearly pays: three chickens make a place bad only if it is not at or above 60 gold/min over at least two runs (the Catacombs hit three chickens at +176/min and were wrongly called bad at 02:01, 2026-11-14).
- Leader choice prefers the sturdier bot (2026-11-14): after quests behind, scrolls and chickens, before gold: the base maximum life from the save (BotQuests.MaxLife, steps of 100), because the quest leader takes the first hits and every leader chicken restarts the game for all four. Real saves: Barabas 351, Xena 269, Sankt 255, Darius 255, so Barabas leads. (Xena, 278 life, led Khalim's Will and chickened in the Spider Cavern.) TestRealMaxLife prints the numbers when QOLBOT_SAVE_DIR is set.

- GOLD DRAIN during the quest run (2026-11-14 02:40-04:08, found overnight, NOT changed): after the Catacombs hunt the team held 35,500 gold; 1.5 h of Khalim's Will (Spider Forest, Spider Cavern, Great Marsh, Flayer Jungle) brought it to 3,506 (a new Catacombs hunt started 04:08). The `Purse:` lines show it goes in lumps of 1,000-4,500 gold per bot per town visit: Barabas 5,050 -> 575 at 04:02 with +33 scrolls (15 -> 48), Xena 2,583 -> 283 at 03:43 with no scroll change, Darius 3,123 -> 23 at 03:57 with +30 scrolls. So potions, repairs, scrolls and mercenary revival. About -270 gold/min for the team while questing, against +176/min while the Catacombs hunt runs (huntpay.json). Candidates to look at in the morning: how many scrolls a bot buys per visit (it restocks to ~40-48 after dropping to 12-15; a tome holds 20), the potion refill per visit (Config.PotionGold, the belt rows), repair at every visit, and the mercenary revival cost per chicken. The sturdier leader (Barabas) cut the chickens: 3 in the first 80 minutes of the quest run against about one every 8 minutes before.

## OVERNIGHT SUMMARY, 2026-11-13 22:15 to 2026-11-14 04:45 (written at 04:45, the team still running)
State at 04:42: all four bots running in the Catacombs on a gold hunt (Darius leading), no fatal event. Gold per bot: Barabas 1,071, Xena 830, Sankt 3,510, Darius 5,456. Khalim's Eye taken (03:46); the Brain and the Heart are still to do. Quests done by all four: 1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 9-14, 19 (the Blade of the Old Religion included).
Changes made overnight (every one is in an entry above; the script ones are UNTESTED in a game unless an entry says otherwise):
 1. Gold hunt places: rules, curated list, learning from pay and chickens, one place per run, a hunt gives up a place that goes bad, tally keys shared by script and place name, chickens do not condemn a place that clearly pays (huntplaces.go, huntpay.go).
 2. Gold hunt equalizing in Gold.js (a bot above the goal gives its surplus) and the leader choice prefers the sturdier bot (max life from the save): Barabas leads the quests.
 3. Stash full: a clean stop of the whole team with a red box (Town.stash, fatal.go).
 4. Earlier in the session: Xena's start-of-game crash (potion belt loop), the manager's error-dialog restart per process, the Storage tab (Grail, Runes, Gems, Potions, Essences), set items no longer sold by followers, the audit of sales and stashes.
Results worth knowing (logs/huntpay.json): Catacombs 16 runs +31,745 gold in 166 min (about 190/min), 3 chickens; Tal Rasha's Tombs -1,285 in 8 min with 4 chickens; Ancient Tunnels -608 in 8 min with 2; the Countess -39 in 3 min. The dense Act 2 dungeons lose money for these 250-350 life characters.
Open for the user (not decided overnight): (a) the gold DRAIN while questing, about -270 gold/min (see the entry above): what to cut; (b) stat/skill respec through the QOL mod works, but Barabas's Whirlwind respec is still "not worth it" by the judge; (c) whether replaced starting gear and the merc's gear should be left alone (the audit shows them as "NOT IN MY PICK-UP RECORD"); (d) the Chain Mail that is stashed as a base and worn as an upgrade 15 s later; (e) the old Sankt unique Scimitar and rare Buckler that were stashed (they were already in his pack); (f) hunts end only when EVERY bot has the goal: the equalizer helps, a lower goal would end them sooner. ## 2026-11-14 00:30 (the log study the user asked for): why the team "had difficulty staying together and moving on, and there was no combat at all" — fixes, 63 simulated tests pass, UNTESTED live
The first live run of the final sync code (11:56-12:00): no kill in three minutes. From the logs: (1) the followers set off only after the leader had left town (they react to `leader.area`), so the leader waited a minute at the far end of each exit; fix: `Team.announceLeaving(area)` (word `leaving`, hooked in `Pather.useWaypoint`/`moveToExit` after the departure wait), `Team.leavingCall()` heard by `Follower.autoFollow` (`dest` replaces `leader.area` in the in-town branch), so they go together. (2) The leader waited on the entrance tile; Sankt, arriving, got 6 instant "move retry" failures and `Pather.sidestep` then stepped him back over the exit into town: four bounces in 40 s. Fix: `Team.stepAside()` (9-15 units off the arrival tile, away from exits, never recursing), `Team.makeRoom()` before every wait (gather, the far-mates wait), and `Pather.sidestep` skips any spot within 12 of an exit (`Team.nearExit`). (3) The exit wait gave up after 45 s and regrouped for mates that were slow: `Team.trackProgress/progressing` (area change or 8 units closer within 30 s) extends the wait by 15 s steps and the regroup is only for the stuck. Not fixed: the leader's act hop for a hunt (the team gathers in the leader's act first, ~30 s per run); the followers' random stand-near point can be unreachable. See docs/TEAM_SYNC.md section 4. Shut down: server and bots are stopped; nothing is running.

## 2026-11-14 01:50 first live test of the final sync: the jitter at the Outer Steppes entrance, two bugs of mine found and fixed (58 simulated tests pass; UNTESTED live)
Live test (12:14-12:17): the departure announcement worked (followers left with the leader at once), but at the Outer Steppes entrance the followers jittered: "move retry" bursts failing within a second, bots bouncing back into town. Counting retries at that spot: 0 before 11:30, then 17, 65, 52: new with the sync code. Bugs: (1) `Team.keepClose` is called by `Pather.moveTo` before every step, including the step of `stepAside`: the nested call started the exit wait before anything had moved, so the leader (and its mercenary) stood on the entrance tile for the whole wait and the arriving bots could not get off it: keepClose now returns at once while `steppingAside`; the simulation's fake `Pather.moveTo` now calls `Team.keepClose` like the real one and records the clock of each move, and the test "stepAside: the step itself must not start the exit wait" fails without the fix (moved at +30000 ms) and passes with it (the first version of that test passed without the fix: it only checked that a move happened). (2) `Pather.sidestep` refused every spot within 12 of an exit, so a follower stuck on the entrance could not unstick itself: now only a step TOWARD a near exit is refused (`Team.towardExit`). The cause of the jitter is my best explanation, not proven: the live run decides.

## 2026-11-14 (session after the model change) zone-to-zone review: where the team gathers, and how a follower follows (UNTESTED live)
A read of the whole zone-change path, leader and follower, before any more live tests:
- Leader, exit: `Pather.moveToExit` gathered the team (`Team.gather(near)`) when it STARTED walking, which can be hundreds of units from the exit, and then crossed without looking again. Same for a waypoint in the field: `Pather.useWaypoint` gathers after walking to the waypoint and before taking it (once per call), not before the walk.
- Follower, field: `checkExit` counted a waypoint as a way to the leader only when `unit.inTown` (the LEADER in town; a party unit has no such property, so never): a follower whose leader took a waypoint from the field found no way and, after 30 s, went "to town to meet up". Now a waypoint counts when my area has one and I have the leader's (`haveWaypoint`), after exits and visible portals; case 3 takes it.
- Follower, in town: the order is now (1) a visible portal to where the leader is (its own, or the Sorceress's it took), (2) a waypoint only if that area has one and I have it (it tried a waypoint first, also for areas without one: "Taking waypoint to Plains Of Despair" before Meir's portal), (3) the portal I came by, (4) ask, after 15 s.
JS syntax balanced; the simulated suite and Go tests pass (they do not cover Pather/Follower: the live run is the check).

- Mercs from the player's own game (the user: "build it so it adjusts to the game data of the player"): `cmd/qolbot/mercs.go` reads the game folder's Data/global/excel/Hireling.txt and Skills.txt (read only, any letter case; the game rebuilds its .bin from them) into the kinds a town sells: an act and a sub-type without its difficulty word (QOL: a1-fire, a1-ice, a2-comb, a2-def, a2-off, a3-fire, a3-cold, a3-ltng, a5-1hs), each with, per difficulty, a skill no other kind of its act has (the "tell" MercHire.js checks on the merc). No Hireling.txt: the 1.13d mercs (`vanillaMercs`, written from memory: Act 2 Prayer/Defiance/Blessed Aim, Thorns/Holy Freeze/Might in Nightmare; Act 3 Fire Ball/Frozen Armor/Charged Bolt; UNCHECKED against a real vanilla game). The bot card's Merc list and `/api/merc` use this table (state `mercTable`); the plan carries it (`mercKinds`). Go tests: parse of the QOL shape, vanilla fallback, and `QOLBOT_GAMEDIR=<game folder> go test ./cmd/qolbot -run TestMercTableRealGame -v` prints the real game's kinds (checked on the user's game: 9 kinds, every tell found).

- MercHire step 1 VERIFIED (15:35:58-15:36:09, Barabas, chose a1-fire, had an Ice Rogue: "tell skills it has: 120" = Meditation, read right). Kashya's hire list: `4f` (list cleared), then one `4e` per merc: 2-byte name string id (little-endian, 0x0d59...) + 4-byte merc id (a seed); the list came twice (the open and the Hire click), 10 mercs. NOTHING in it says the kind, and every kind of an act shares its name range (Hireling.txt NameFirst/NameLast), so the bot cannot pick the kind before hiring. Hiring would be C->S 0x36 (npc gid, merc id). ON HOLD: the user will add the kind to the hire descriptions in the QOL mod ("let me solve that with my mod. i will add descriptions"); step 2 is built on that.

ID: 128". Now the altar is used until activated (object mode 2), the bot waits up to 15 s for the Ancients (Talic 540, Madawc 541, Korlic 542), kills each with no party town trip in between (`PartySync.busy`), logs quest 39's bits and walks into the Worldstone Keep by its exit (`moveToExit(128)`). VERIFIED complete 15:46:37 (Darius).
- Autoequip never replaced Barabas's Gemmed Wirt's Leg (the user: "autoequip didn't choose it, but i would expect it to replace it"). Three causes: melee.nip had no Barbarian melee weapon lines; "socketed" in the protected list made any item with gems a keeper; and the weapon tiers never counted the weapon's own damage (only enhanced damage x4 and stat 24, the two-handed max). Now: a Barbarian line for one-handed axes, swords, maces, clubs, hammers, scepters and knives (two-handed damage: tier 0, kept); every melee weapon line adds (min + max one-handed damage) x (100 + enhanced damage) / 2. The protected list is the player's alone (the user: "you're leeway to change protection settings is not allowed"): a change that let "socketed" pass plain items with gems was undone at once; with "socketed" checked the Gemmed Wirt's Leg stays. Wirt's Leg is a quest item to NTIP (tier 0). UNTESTED live.
- MercHire no longer goes to the seller every game (Barabas did, with a1-fire chosen): `MercHire.listTrip` false, it only logs that the merc is not the chosen kind, until the QOL mod's hire descriptions carry the kind. UNTESTED live.
- GoldSell.nip: magic gear at every item level (the user: "let's amend goldsell.nip so they make some money from level 1"); it was item level 60 and up, so the followers sold next to nothing in Normal (20k-47k each on 2026-11-14). Followers load it always, the leader only on a gold hunt. More town trips for the followers. UNTESTED live.
- MercHire STEP 2 (the user: "i would want to work out how the client derives the kind"): the kind of a hire list entry is not in the packet; the game rolls it from the entry's 4-byte id. From D2MOO (the 1.10 code: Monsters.cpp MONSTERS_HirelingInit, MonsterTbls.cpp DATATBLS_GetNextHirelingTxtRecordFromActAndDifficulty, D2Seed.h): candidates = the Hireling.txt rows with Version exactly 100 of the seller's act and difficulty, the first and then only rows of its Level, in file order (duplicates stay); seed low = the id, high = 666; ONE roll: state = high + 0x6AC690C5 * low (64 bits); pick = roll & (n-1) when n is a power of two, else (uint32)roll % n. Go: `hireCandidates` -> MercTable.Candidates -> plan `mercCandidates` ("act-difficulty"); JS: `MercHire.rollKind` (low 32 bits in 16-bit halves; checked under gjs against Python's 64-bit math on both captured lists). On the user's game: Act 1 [fire, ice], Act 2 [def, off], Act 3 [fire, cold, ltng], Act 5 [1hs, 1hs]; Rogues come down to the id's parity. `MercHire.maybe` (once a game): read the list, print every entry's name and kind, hire the first of the chosen kind (C->S 0x36: seller gid, id), check the new merc's kind by its skills ("the derivation was right" / "is wrong, nothing more hired"), pick up the gear the old merc dropped within 8 (QOL drop_items=1) and run autoMercEquip. Never replaces a merc that wears gear when drop_items is off, never with an item on the cursor. UNTESTED live: 1.13d may differ from D2MOO's 1.10, and that the list id IS the seed is assumed; the first hire tells.
- Bot card modal shows the kind of the hired merc next to its name (the user: "make it show what type of merc is hired"): ToolsThread.js includes MercHire.js and adds `kind` (the label of `MercHire.current()`, from the game's merc table) to the inventory report; a new merc or kind refreshes the report. Blank when the merc is not one of the kinds. UNTESTED live; the page part is built.
- Vanilla merc table checked (the user: "this will work for players who don't have my mods"): the built-in `vanillaMercs`/`vanillaCandidates` now equal what parseHireling/hireCandidates make of the stock 1.13 Hireling.txt and Skills.txt (fabd/diablo2 on GitHub, not in this repo): ids a1-fire, a1-ice, a2-comb, a2-def, a2-off, a3-fire, a3-cold, a3-ltng, a5-1hs; Act 3 tells Inferno 41 / Glacial Spike 55 / Charged Bolt 38 (the memory table had Fire Ball and Frozen Armor). `QOLBOT_STOCKDIR=<folder> go test ./cmd/qolbot -run TestVanillaMercsMatchStock` compares them. The hire descriptions are not used anywhere: only rows, skills and the roll.
- Merc table from the game's archives too (the user: "a problem might be if a player has an mpq file that includes a hirelings.txt"): `internal/mpq` reads MPQ format 0 read only (hash/block tables, encryption, PKWARE implode by a port of blast.c, zlib, bzip2). Order (`mercSource`): a loose Data/global/excel/Hireling.txt (then no archive is opened, the user's rule); else a mod .mpq (any non-stock .mpq in the game folder) holding data\global\excel\hireling.txt, more than one: an error on the console naming them and the first by name used (the user: "if you find more than one put an error in the console"); else Patch_D2.mpq, d2exp.mpq; else the built-in table. Skills.txt from the same place or the next archive. The console says where the table came from each time it is read ("Merc table: Hireling.txt found in ..."). Checked: the user's Patch_D2.mpq and d2exp.mpq read (Hireling.txt, Skills.txt, hireling.bin); a scratch folder with copies of Patch_D2.mpq gave the stock table, and two copies named as mods gave the error; the user's game uses its loose file. The real 1.13d Patch_D2.mpq table has unused Act 4 Barbarian rows (skipped: no Act 4 seller) and ONE Act 5 Barbarian row per difficulty: the built-in table follows it (the GitHub copy differed). Unit tests: the table keys and blast.c's test vector. The reader does not read .bin tables (a mod that ships only hireling.bin gets the stock table).
- MercHire first live run (17:41, Barabas, Nightmare): every entry "= ?" -> AutoSpec.js never set `Config.MercCandidates` (the plan had them). Fixed.
- MercHire second live run (17:42:43, Barabas): the list was read and every entry got a kind (Floria a1-fire, Gaile a1-ice, ...), but "the hire did not happen": 0x36 was sent with the seed. D2MOO (SUnitNpc.cpp, D2GAME_NPC_HireMerc and SUNITNPC_HireMercenaryFromRoster; PlrMsg.cpp handler: 9 bytes, int32 at +1, uint16 at +5): 0x36 = seller gid, merc NAME id; the server checks the seller is the interact unit, the name is in the roster and not hired, gold (+ bank) >= the cost (MONSTERS_HirelingInit nGold), Normal level caps, and that the 0x4E seed IS dwSeed fed to MONSTERS_HirelingInit (the derivation's input confirmed). Fixed: the name id is sent.
- VERIFIED live 17:46:58 (Barabas, Nightmare): the hire with the name id works. Kashya list Aliza = a1-fire (derived); hired Aliza for 6000 gold; it has tell skill 108 (Blessed Aim) = a1-fire, so the kind derivation (rollKind) is right. The old Ice Rogue dropped 3 items (QOL drop_items); all 3 picked up; Cap and Beryl Breast Plate went back on the merc. The third (likely the bow) was not logged as equipped: check it.
- The old merc's bow (live 17:46:58, Barabas): the old Ice Rogue dropped Cap, Beryl Breast Plate and Edge Bow (a plain white bow, ilvl 35); the inventory was full, so the bow stayed at Kashya's feet and was lost with the game. "picked up 3 of 3" was wrong: Pickit.pickItem returns true even when nothing was picked. MercHire now (UNTESTED in a game; the user's design) hires first, then takes each dropped piece (by the old merc's gids, else what lies within 8) onto the cursor (C->S 0x16 with the last DWORD 1: UNTESTED that 1 means the cursor; if the piece does not reach the cursor, Pickit.pickItem takes it to the inventory) and clicks it onto the new merc's empty slot (Item.getBodyLoc). Only a piece the new merc cannot wear goes to Item.putAway (inventory, else the stash); no room at all: dropped. No inventory room is needed for the pieces it wears. It logs "the old merc's N item(s): W put on the new merc, S stored, L left on the ground".
- Activity Auto Equip cards (UNTESTED in the browser): the card title is "AutoEquip: <item>" with the item in its quality colour (the event's quality, else the colour code of its stat text's first line; plain when unknown), the item the unique/set name, else the full name of its report, the line under it says the bot or "<bot>'s mercenary" (parseEvent Detail "Mercenary" for "Equipped X on the mercenary"), and the item window says "Auto Equip: put on ...". An equip line with no item report of its own borrows the stats of the bot's latest entry of that item; the bot's own report, if it comes within 20 s, replaces them.
- MercHire without qol.dll (no drop_items: the game destroys the old merc's gear on a hire; UNTESTED in a game): it no longer keeps the merc for good. stripMerc takes each worn piece off first (clickItem(4, slot) with an empty cursor, then Item.putAway: inventory, else the stash), hires, and autoMercEquip puts the pieces on the new merc from the inventory/stash. A piece with no room goes back on and the merc is kept. The pieces must have a merc tier to be put back on (they had one when autoMercEquip put them on; gear a player put on by hand may not).

Working as designed, with a delay of up to one save interval.

Next: repair single items within the budget.
  - Repairs fixed (UNTESTED in a game): the breakdown (18:27:24) was Assault Helmet 58006 (recharging its charges), Chain Mail 724, Deathspade 647 at 1 of 24, the rest under 300 each. When the repair-all bill is too high, needRepair asks for "repairSome" only if some item below RepairPercent can be paid in full (Town.affordableRepairs), so an unaffordable item never makes an endless "needs repair" (Attack.clearLevel goes to town on it). Town.repairSome repairs those one at a time, the most worn first, with C->S 0x35 (17 bytes: repairer gid, item gid, 0, flags 0 = this item; D2MOO D2GAME_NPC_Repair: negative flags = all; an item that cannot be paid in full gets a partial repair that spends all the gold, so it is never sent), and logs "repaired one at a time: ...; too dear: ...". The server takes gold then stash.
  - Follower quest completions at once (UNTESTED): ToolsThread.watchQuests (every bot) checks me.getQuest(id, 0) for every QuestTable row every 3 s and prints the Quester's own line 'Act N - #M - "Title" is complete.' when one becomes complete; the first look in a game only remembers. The leader's Quester line and this one are one entry (same bot and quest within 30 s), and the save watcher adds none when the entry exists.

Also: "always together, always safe".

Without it, the route is Travincal, the stairs (object 386, by preset), Level 1, then Level 2. The leader gathers the team before the stairs and in Level 1; a Sorceress travelling alone doesn't wait. The waypoint hunt (`Waypoints.sync`, the leader's "Leading waypoint hunt" phase) runs at the start of every game a bot leads (default.dbj after `Team.wait`, not on a gold or level hunt) and on arriving in a new act while questing (`Quester.arrivedInAct`). It shares only waypoints that somebody in the team has; waypoints nobody has are only taken as the quest leader walks past them.

## 2026-11-15 the waypoint hunt fetches waypoints nobody has, in the acts below the team's (UNTESTED in a game)
The user: the waypoint hunt should go and fetch waypoints nobody on the team has, "yes, but only for acts below their current progress". `Waypoints.sync` (the waypoint hunt: start of every game a bot leads, not on a gold or level hunt; and on arriving in a new act while questing) now does two things:
1. It shares, as before.
2. It fetches: `fetchList` = the waypoints of acts below `teamAct` (the lowest of every member's highest open act, from the town waypoints in their lists) that nobody in the team has and that the leader isn't gated from.
`fetch(area)`: the leader goes to the act's town, then `journeyTo(area)`, or `Pather.toDurance()` for 101. It takes the waypoint, waits for the team (`waitForTeam`), and goes back to town by waypoint. A waypoint taken on the way to an earlier one is shared instead of fetched. At the end the leader goes back to the act the game started in.
Log: `Waypoints: nobody in the team has ... (acts below act N): fetching`, `Waypoints: fetching X`, then `Waypoints synced (a of b shared, c of d fetched)`. Risk: a team far ahead with many gaps spends a long time on this at every game start until the gaps are filled.

## 2026-11-15 the inventory window reads the save file (VERIFIED against all 7 profiles' saves; the page itself not seen in a browser)
The user: "lets try to get it to read direct from the save file. that saves on crazy logs from the bots, and let's me manipulte characters even without running any bots".
- `internal/d2items` (new): reads the items of a .d2s and of PlugY's .d2x stash pages. Worn items, inventory, belt, cube, the merc's gear, socketed items, set lists and runeword stats are all covered. It also writes the tooltip text: names, defense, durability, quantity, stats via ItemStatCost's descfunc/descval/descstr and the .tbl strings. Bit layout checked against nokka/d2s (MIT).
- **Tables:** everything comes from the game's own tables, in the order the game finds them (`FolderSource`): loose `Data/global/excel` and `Data/local/lng/eng` (the QOL mod's), then the mods' .mpq, then Patch_D2, d2exp, d2data. Read only.
- **Hard-won facts:**
  - The game numbers ItemStatCost and Skills rows by ROW ORDER and ignores their ID columns. The QOL mod's last two stat rows say 361 and 362, but the compiled itemstatcost.bin and the saves have them as 359 (item_splashonhit) and 360 (item_iscorrupted).
  - Uniques and set items skip the "Expansion" separator row (nokka: Coldkill is unique 129); the affix tables count every row, from 1.
  - Rare names are RareSuffix rows, then RarePrefix rows, from 1. Runeword id = the N of "RunewordN" + 26.
  - Defense and durability are saved by item TYPE (ItemTypes "armo"/"weap" through Equiv), not by the file: the mod's gas potions are in Weapons.txt with type "misc" and have no durability in the save (Darius).
  - The mod's .tbl strings hold the colour byte as UTF-8 (C3 BF), fixed on reading.
  - PlugY keeps the stash page shown in the game in the .d2s too: with a .d2x, the stash comes from its pages only.
- `GET /api/inventory?name=X` now answers from the save (`"source":"save"`, `"at"` = the save's time, `"problem"` when some items could not be read) and falls back to the bot's last live report only when the save cannot be read. The tables are cached and reloaded when the game's data files change. The window says "from the save file, saved X ago", shows each PlugY page, and marks a dead merc.
- Not shown yet: the stats a gem or rune adds to the item it is in (that needs Gems.txt, and runes' stats per item type); jewels' stats are shown. The shared stash (`_LOD_SharedStashSave.sss`) is not shown (`ReadPlugY` can read it).
- The bots still send their live report (the leader choice's `hasCube` reads it).
- Tests: `internal/d2items/items_test.go` (synthetic items, a .tbl); `real_test.go` runs on real saves when D2ITEMS_GAME and D2ITEMS_SAVES are set (copies of saves, never the real folder).

## 2026-11-15 why the Nightmare team life-chickened (UNTESTED in a game when written; quest run started with it the same day)
The user: "try to figure out why they are life chickening -- it might not be about levels, it might be about stupid play tactics". The overnight run had 27 chickens (Barabas, Xena, Sankt, Darius, level 51-52, Nightmare Catacombs), almost all to "Afflicted"/"Tainted" (MonStats bighead1/2/6/7, the lightning-throwing Bighead demons) and Ghouls, at 25-40% life.
- **Rejuvenations were never drunk in time.** `_DefaultConfig.js` had `UseRejuvHP = 40` and `LifeChicken = 40`. ToolsThread drinks the rejuv when life < 40%, then in the same tick chickens when life <= 40%, so the bot always left with rejuvs in the belt. Now `UseRejuvHP = 55`, and ToolsThread waits up to 600 ms after a rejuv before a life chicken, unless life is under 15%.
- **One healing column.** The belt was hp, mp, mp, rv: a melee character in Nightmare had 3 healing potions in the belt and then drank from the pack ("Drinking potion from inventory" before most chickens). With Quartermaster on, Chores keeps no spare potions in the pack. `_DefaultConfig.js` now sets the belt to hp, mp, hp, rv for everyone but a Sorceress (hp, mp, mp, rv). It is in the config file because the leader never runs `Chores.tune` (only Follower.js does); Darius, the leader, still chickened with one column at 09:28. `Chores.tune` gives the second healing column the same refill level.
- **Not the cause:** resistances. From the saves, gear lightning resistance is 43-94 against Nightmare's -40 (Barabas 79, Darius 94, Sankt 43, Xena 67). The team was together when Darius chickened (06:31), so it was not a leader alone ahead. TownHP 50 is close to LifeChicken 40, and some chickens happened while casting the town portal; with the rejuv at 55 the rejuv now comes first.
- **Going out alone** (the quest run of the same morning, 09:41-09:43, three chickens to Hell Clan in the Arcane Sanctuary, all to a bot that was alone):
  - Barabas took the waypoint to the Sanctuary while Darius, the leader, was going to town to regroup (`dest` was read before the leader moved). Follower.js now looks up the leader again right before going out by portal or waypoint, and stays put if the leader is no longer in `dest` (except while answering a "leaving" call, 20 s).
  - Xena made the town portal, gave up waiting after 45 s for Barabas, Sankt and Darius to go out, and went out alone. `PartySync.waitUntilOthersBack` now returns "stay" for a follower; `barrier` sets `PartySync.stayInTown` and `Town.visitTown` returns without taking the portal, so the follower stays in town and follows the leader from there. The leader still goes out (the team follows it).
- **Horadric Staff "Failed to empty cube"** (Darius, 5 failures): Pack stores keepers in the cube once the stash page is full, and `Cubing.emptyCube` could not fit them anywhere. It now adds a PlugY page (`/insertpage`, `/swappage 1`, as Pack does) once when `Config.InfiniteStash` is on, then tries again; it logs which item had no room.
- **The first stash-page fix did not work** (10:18, Darius: "Cubing: the stash is full: adding a stash page", then "no room for Bill"): `/insertpage` adds a page AFTER the one shown, and `/swappage 1` swapped page 1 with itself, so the full page stayed in view. Pack.js had the same sequence. Both now use `Cubing.newStashPage()`: `/page 1`, `/insertpage`, `/swappage 2`, which leaves an empty page 1 in view and the old page 1 as page 2 (PlugY.dll has the commands /page /insertpage /swappage /deletepage /renamepage /togglepage).
- **Potion refill in Nightmare and Hell:** with a 3-row belt the bots only bought when a healing column was down to 1 potion (MinColumn ceil(3/2) = 2), so they often went out with half a belt. The saves at 10:25: Barabas 4 healing, Darius 3, Sankt 4, Xena 5, and no rejuvenations at all (only Meir had some). Now the healing columns are topped up at every visit in Nightmare/Hell: `_DefaultConfig.js` MinColumn[0] and [2] = 4 when `me.diff > 0` (the leader), and `Chores.tune` sets the healing column to the belt's rows (the followers).
- Quest run 09:47-10:25 with all of the above except these last two: 3 chickens in 38 minutes (Tal Rasha's Tombs: Burning Dead, Preserved Dead, Steel Scarab), against 3 in 13 minutes before.
- Quest run 10:26-10:46 with the stash-page and refill fixes: the Horadric Staff was made (the new stash page worked, 10:26:42). Then Darius got 3 chickens in 29 minutes in the Tombs, and Qolbot started its level hunt (Nightmare Catacombs/Andariel until the weakest is 55, Sankt leading). The level hunt had no chickens in its first 35 minutes.

The walking wait in `Team.keepClose` only slept. It now fights (`Attack.clear(15)`) whenever a monster is in range while it waits; `Team.waitFight` keeps Attack's own moves from starting a second wait.

The cause in the game is unknown (maybe a quest access check on the tombs in Nightmare). `Town.visitTown` now walks back (`Pather.journeyTo(preArea)`, the Canyon waypoint) when no portal takes it back, and throws only if that fails too.

## 2026-11-15 the inventory window looks like the game's (VERIFIED: all 121 pictures in the 3 saved characters drawn; the window seen in headless Firefox with Barabas's real items)
The user: "try and use the ingame pictures with mouseover stats (just like the game does it) ... show the merc and player gear in a layout similar to the game". They offered their font copies.
- **Pictures** (`internal/d2items/pictures.go`): `Tables.Picture(item)` picks the game's .dc6 the way the game does: the base's `invfile`; a type with several pictures (ItemTypes `VarInvGfx`, `InvGfx1-6`: rings, amulets, charms, jewels) by the picture number in the save (the 3 bits after the quality, now kept in `Item.Picture`); a unique's/set item's own `invfile` (UniqueItems/SetItems) or the base's `uniqueinvfile`/`setinvfile`.
- **Recolouring:** when the base has `InvTrans` (1 grey, 2 grey2, 3 gold, 4 brown, 5 greybrown, 6 invgrey, 7 invgrey2, 8 invgreybrown: the files in `data\global\items\palette`, 21 tables of 256 bytes each) and the unique/set item (`invtransform`) or a magic/rare affix (`transform`=1, `transformcolor`) gives a colors.txt code, the picture name gets `.<InvTrans>.<colour row>` ("invhlm.8.5"). `PicturePNG` decodes the DC6 (first frame, RLE, rows bottom-up) with `data\global\palette\units\pal.dat` (BGR). `OpenFolder`/`Folder.File(inner path)` reads any game file, loose first then the archives (`FolderSource` is built on it).
- **Report:** each item has `f` (picture), `e` (ethereal), `s` (sockets) and `sk` (the items in its sockets, with their pictures and stats).
- **Server:** `GET /api/itempic?f=<name>` (PNG; the name must match `^[A-Za-z0-9_]+(\.[1-8]\.\d{1,2})?$`; drawn once and kept in memory; the archives stay open while pictures are asked for and close a minute after). `GET /font/<file>` serves the `fonts/` folder: Formal436.ttf (tooltip) and ExocetMedium.ttf/ExocetLight.ttf (headings), copied from the user's own fonts. `/fonts/` is git-ignored (not ours to share); without it the page falls back to Georgia.
- **Page:** a dark game panel holds:
  - the character's paper doll: weapon left, head and amulet, body, shield right; gloves, ring, belt, ring and boots along the bottom;
  - the weapon swap, when there is one;
  - the merc's doll (head, body, weapon, shield);
  - inventory, belt (4 columns, first row at the bottom), cube and stash pages.
  All use 29-px cells with the real pictures. Ethereal items are drawn see-through; hovering a socketed item shows what is in its sockets over it. Hovering any item shows the game-style tooltip (black, centred, the game's text colours); a click still opens the stats window. Items without a picture (the dev demo) show their name.

## 2026-11-15 coordination by keywords in the bots' message log; every public line has three phrasings (UNTESTED in a game; simulated)
The user: "make it so anything required for coordination purposes uses the internal chat, and make sure that internal chat uses keywords instead of full sentences", and "for every public spoken phrase, make it so there are at least 3 variations of that thing and print for me a file -- maybe docs/SPEECH.md".
- **Message log** (`Team.post / command / take`, Team.js): `data/<profile>.tmsg.json` = `{n, msgs: [{n, at, w, a, to}]}`, the last 30 messages. Each reader keeps its own place (a key), so the Follower, QuickTravel and Gold never take each other's messages. Messages from before the script started are skipped. `tsay.json` (one state word per bot) is unchanged.
- **Moved off the public chat:**
  - `Team.holdAndGather` sends "hold"/"go" as commands.
  - QuestScripts sends "redportal" as a command.
  - `QuickTravel.waypoint` sends "waypoint N" / "waypoint go NAME N" and reads the `haswp` answers.
  - `QuickTravel.portal` sends "portal PLACE" and waits up to 150 s for the Sorceress's answer: `portalopen`, `noget`, `noquick` or `noplace`. It reads only her log.
  - `Gold.listen` reads `needgold` (the chatmsg listener is gone).
  - The Follower runs its leader's commands from the log (`readCommands`, through the same `chatEvent`). A player can still type the commands in chat.
- **Speech.js rewritten:**
  - `Speech.say(key)` posts the keyword, then says one of three phrasings if the setting allows: problem words need "Bots report when they have a problem" on; task words need "Bots report when they engage in tasks" on.
  - `Speech.task / problem / reply` dress a line with `Speech.flavor` and say it under the matching setting.
  - A reply to a player's own chat command is always said. A reply to the leader bot's command counts as a task (`Speech.fromLog`).
  - Every rule has at least three phrasings. A line no rule knows is said as it is, or after "Hear me:" / "Mark this:".
- **Lines that now go through Speech:**
  - Follower's raw `say`s.
  - QuickTravel's "Opening portal to ..." (task).
  - QuestScripts' "Getting/Making ..." (task).
  - ActTravel's "Act change failed." (problem).
  - The Baal/Diablo callouts ("Hot TP!", "Star TP up", the Baal portal monster count, "Dolls found! NG." ...) are commented out instead (the user: "comment out any public speech from the legacy kolbot coordinate system -- for other bots"). They were said because `Config.PublicMode` is set, but that setting is only the party-invite mode (AutoSpec sets 3: the leader invites); the callouts were for other players' helper bots. Baal.js's stock "leave on Dolls / Souls" checks stay (only their chat line is commented out): `Config.Baal.DollQuit` / `SoulQuit` are false in the config files.
- **docs/SPEECH.md** lists every phrasing. It is generated by `scripts/speechdoc.sh` (gjs) from Speech.js: run it again after changing a phrasing.
- **Tests:**
  - `scripts/jstest/speech_test.js`: three phrasings each; each line the scripts make has its own rule; the settings gate what is said.
  - Message-log tests in team_test.js.
  - Results: 17 + 6 + 68 pass, and all 142 scripts parse (gjs).
- **Not converted:** the MF leader calls in Mephisto/Andariel/Duriel/Cows (only with `Config.MFLeader`, off) and stock scripts the team does not run.

## 2026-11-15 auto-equip by build (UNTESTED in a game; simulated)
The user: "can you perfect the autoequip such that it includes the appropraiate weapon types and it's sensitive to the spec of the character" and "for example a paladin can play as either a melee or a magic build".
- **One list for every class:** `pickit/Autoequip/gear.nip` replaces caster.nip and melee.nip. Its one rule asks `qolgear.tier(item)` (libs/common/Gear.js, included by NTItemParser.dbl). The NIP parser lowercases its lines, so the name is lowercase.
- **Builds:** `Gear.js` has a build for each template ("Class/Spec" from data/<profile>.spec.json), and a class fallback for bots without a plan. Each build has:
  - a style: melee, ranged, kick, caster or summon (the weights);
  - its skill trees (stat 188 with the tab id) and its skills (stats 107 and 97, the main one weighted more);
  - its weapon types;
  - its hands: "one" (weapon and shield), "two" (two-handed, no shield), "any", or "dual". `dualOk` lets the left hand hold a second weapon instead of a shield.
- **Per build:**
  - Hammerdin is a caster: +skills count, weapon damage does not.
  - Zealot and Smiter use any one-handed melee weapon and a shield. For the Smiter, the shield's damage counts (Smite).
  - Whirlwind and Frenzy use the weapon kind of the mastery with hard points (`masteryTypes`); with none yet, any melee weapon. Barbarians never hold a shield (the user: "a barbarian wouldn't hold a sheild", "a barb might choose a 2H weapon"): Whirlwind holds a two-handed weapon or one in each hand, whichever scores higher (the two-handed one must beat both together); Frenzy and Throw dual-wield. A Barbarian holds a two-handed sword in one hand ("barbs can equip 2h swords in each hand"): `twoHanded` is false for him when the sword has one-handed damage (stat 21), so it counts as a one-handed weapon, in either hand.
  - Bowazon: bows, Amazon bows and crossbows, no shield. Javazon: javelins and Amazon javelins with a shield, not Amazon spears (Lightning Fury is thrown: it needs a javelin). An Amazon without a plan ranks all her kinds, spears too.
  - Kick Assassin: the boots' kick damage counts, claw damage does not. Trapsin is a caster.
  - Druids, Necromancers and Sorceresses rank their own trees. Fury is melee and may use a two-handed weapon. Sorceresses may use staves.
- **Changes from the old lists:**
  - Every quality is scored by its stats now: superior, unique, set, crafted and runewords too, not only normal, magic and rare. The protection list (`Item.isReplacable`) and the keep rules still decide what may be replaced or worn; they are unchanged.
  - Ethereal items score 0 unless indestructible.
  - Each class ranks its own class gear (Paladin shields, Necromancer heads, Barbarian helms, Druid pelts, Sorceress orbs, Assassin claws, Amazon bows and javelins); another class's gear scores 0.
  - Anything the build can wear scores at least 1, so it beats an empty slot.
- **Engine (Misc.js):**
  - `Item.handSlot`: a two-handed weapon must beat the weapon and the shield together, and only if the shield may be replaced. A shield or second weapon cannot go next to a worn two-handed weapon.
  - `Item.equip` takes the shield off first (`Item.takeOff`): a keeper is stored, anything else is held for the team like other replaced gear. Arrows and bolts stay.
  - `getBodyLoc` lets a one-handed weapon into the left hand for dual-wield builds.
  - "Equipping X." goes through `Speech.task`: three phrasings, said only with task reports on.
- **The bots' worn gear** (read from their saves, 2026-11-15):
  - Barabas (Whirlwind) wears a Blade and the unique Deathspade axe (dual) and has no mastery points yet, so any melee weapon counts for him.
  - Darius (Zeal): Jagged Star and Heraldic Shield.
  - Xena (Kick): two claws. The left claw now scores 1, so a shield can take its place.
  - Meir's runewords and Sankt's Gargoyle Head are protected.
- **Not handled:**
  - The weapon-switch slots (11/12).
  - Arrows and quivers.
  - A Necromancer head goes to the right-hand slot (stock `getBodyLoc`: type 69 is listed with the weapons). Left as it is, since Sankt wears his there.
- **Tests:** `scripts/jstest/gear_test.js` (10 tests); Go tests updated (gear.nip only). UNTESTED in a game: the stat calls with a sub-id (188 tab, 107/97 skill), the shield take-off, and dual-wield equipping.

## 2026-11-15 outright keepers stay unidentified (UNTESTED in a game)
The user: "i thought the NIP would qualify all sets and uniques as keepers outright, so no ID is required"; "my keeper settings say all unique set items are keepers".
- **What happened:** the saves showed the bots' stashed uniques and sets mostly identified: Barabas 15 of 17, Darius 17 of 19, Sankt 18 of 18, Xena 12 of 14. Meir (level 80) had 1 of 7.
- **Cause:** below the protection's `minLevel` (70), nothing is protected, so `Town.identify` treated them as ordinary items. Its Qolbot line "identify every unidentified item kept by the rules" then identified them, though Generated.nip's `[quality] == unique` / `[quality] == set` keep them without needing stats.
- **Fix:** `Item.keptOutright(item)` is true for an unidentified item whose keep rule matches without stats (`Pickit.checkItem` result 1). Such an item is never identified, at any level:
  - `Town.identify` skips it;
  - `Town.cainID` is not used while one is in the pack (Cain identifies everything);
  - `Town.fieldID` skips it.
- **isKeeper:** `Item.isKeeper` no longer requires the item to be identified, so an outright keeper is never worn and never identified for an upgrade check (autoEquip, autoMercEquip). Keeper rules trump auto-equip, as the user set on 2026-11-14.
- **Unchanged:** a keep rule that needs stats (rares by their stats) still identifies first. The protection list is unchanged.

## 2026-11-15 14:20-14:27 first live run after the chat move: two fixes
- **The leader no longer answered "cantreach" (a regression of the chat move):**
  - `tools/TownChicken.js` listened in the public chat with `Speech.is(msg, "cannotReach")`, which the Speech.js rewrite removed. The search for leftover callers missed it: the file is ISO-8859 and grep without -a skipped it.
  - Seen at 14:20: at Maggot Lair Level 3 (no waypoint), Darius, Sankt and Xena stayed in town while Barabas and Meir fought.
  - Fix: TownChicken's loop takes the "cantreach" keyword from the message log (`Team.take("townchicken", null, ["cantreach"])`). Only the leader answers: the bot with a plan goal, since the followers' Config.Leader is empty in that thread.
  - A grep -a over every script found no other caller of the removed functions.
- **The leader ignored the team's heal call during a chest trip (old, not a regression):**
  - `Quester.pickAndPlunder` marked the leader busy (`PartySync.busy(60000)`) before the walk to the chest, and TownChicken ignores party calls while busy.

## Shelved idea (2026-11-15): a live video feed of the leader on the web page
The user shelved it "for another time". The plan if it comes back:
- One Xvfb display per bot: all five games share one now, and only the window on top could be recorded.
- `ffmpeg -f x11grab` at 25 fps (Diablo II draws only 25), served as motion JPEG to an `<img>`, and only while the page is open.
- The cost: about 10-20% of a core and 1.5-2 MB/s over 127.0.0.1. For watching from another device, H.264 over a WebSocket with the browser's built-in player.
- Linux first. On Windows, gdigrab records one window but a minimized game draws nothing. Mac needs Screen Recording permission and a visible window.

## 2026-11-15 15:55 keepers went into the cube instead of the stash (fixed, UNTESTED in a game)
- The user: "barabas cube is full of items, so it means when he is in town he is not doing the job of stashing items".
- His save (15:50) has a Partizan (stashed 14:50) and a 4-socket Lance (15:10) in the cube. `Item.cubeRoom` / `Item.toCube` made the cube "extra storage once the stash is full", tried BEFORE a new PlugY page, in `Pack.js` and in `Town.stash`. So the cube filled up while new stash pages were possible, and the cube was not free for recipes and quest cubing.
- Now the cube is storage only when the stash cannot grow (no `Config.InfiniteStash`, or `Town.noMorePages`). `Town.cubeToStash` (start of `Town.stash`) moves what is in the cube to the stash, adding a page when the shown one is full. Quest items and what `Town.canStash` skips stay. Log line: `Town: moved X from my cube to the stash`.
- `Town.stash` added pages with `/insertpage` + `/swappage 1`, which left the full page in view. It now uses `Cubing.newStashPage`, as `Pack.js` does.

Sankt took 15 s: `Pather.moveTo` always applies `Config.ClearPath` (a "method override" at its top), so his walk to Darius's portal fought twice ("Fighting demons" 15:53:47, 15:53:52) and picked up a GoldSell Long Bow.

- A town chicken trip's call is `urgent` (`PartySync.call(area, true)` from `Town.visitTown(true)`): the mates answer it even during a quest step (`PartySync.busy`). Before, a busy mate stayed in the field. A chores trip (`visitTown(false)`) still waits for the quest step.
- TownChicken checks for a call every 1 s (was 1.5 s; the user asked for 1 s).

## 2026-11-15 16:30 the team sync rebuilt around one rule (tested in the simulation, UNTESTED in a game)
- The user's rules and what each maps to are in `docs/TEAM_SYNC.md` section 0. The changes:
- **The town gate** (`Team.waitToLeaveTown(targetArea)`) now holds EVERY bot of a bot-led run, not only the leader, with no time limit: into the field only when every mate in the game is in town and ready (or already out where I go). Town to town is not gated. The 60 s / 5 min give-ups and the `afterParty` bypass are gone.
- **Any bot to town calls the party**: `Town.goToTown` (`Town.teamFollows`) and `Pather.useWaypoint` from the field (a wrapper at the end of Pather.js). Before, only `Town.visitTown` trips called; the ~200 other `goToTown` callers (quest scripts, Quick Travel, regroups, the waypoint sync) left the mates in the field.
- **The trip's end** (`PartySync.barrier`) only says ready: the leader goes back out, the others follow the leader (`stayInTown`). `waitAllReady` (90 s), `waitForProceed` (100 s) and `waitUntilOthersBack` (45 s) are removed: the gate does their job. A mate answers every call (busy or not) within 1 s.
- **The maker goes last** for every bot (`Team.sendOutFirst` under `Pather.usePortal` when I own a portal there), no limit; two makers do not wait for each other.
- **One timer** (`Team.limits.sync`, Settings > Behavior > Auto Regroup, default 30 s): `gather`/`boss`/`far`/`depart`/`sendOut` all read it. After it a split party regroups in town, reported as "Sync broken: ..." (`Report.problem`). The 3-regroups cap and the "progressing" extension are gone.
- **No clearing on the way**: followers going where the leader went (exit, portal, waypoint) use `Follower.flee` (`Pather.fleeing`); a blocked step still fights. A waiting leader fights back (`Attack.clear(15)` in `gather`).
- `PartySync.hold` (the leader waiting in town up to 3 min for a bot out of the game) is switched off: a bot out of the game is not waited for; when it rejoins in town, the existing regroup (`Team.lost`) brings the party to town.
- **The leader names the portal** (the user: "the entire team should always be together in the field. always. no exceptions"): a leader going out of town through somebody else's portal (a Sorceress's Quick Travel portal, a waypoint helper's, a mate's trip portal) says "proceed" with the portal's owner and area (`Pather.usePortal`); its own portal already said it (`Team.sendOutFirst`). A follower in town takes the named portal at once, without clearing, whether the leader is still in town or just went through (`Follower`, before the exit/waypoint choice). Before, only the leader's own portal was announced: at 15:58:33 Xena took Meir's portal to the Spider Forest waypoint and the four followers walked out of Kurast Docks, 550 units from her. Not in the simulation (Pather and Follower are not loaded there); UNTESTED in a game.
- **The far side of an exit** (`Team.keepClose` arrival wait) is on the one timer too: no "on its way" extension, no give-up; after the timer a party regroup (the user: "on any of the seams and transitions between zones and towns, they have X seconds to make it right or a party regroup gets called").
- **The waypoint sync names its portal, and nobody walks after a waypoint departure** (the user: "if one person is missing a waypoint in a waypoint hunt and wanders into the field by themselves ... we have failed"): `Waypoints.share` says `proceed` (owner, area) right after the leader opens its portal at the waypoint (`Waypoints.sendTeam`), so every follower takes it. `Team.announceLeaving(area, by)` says how the leader left; after a `waypoint` departure a follower does not walk a town exit (its leader's portal, the waypoint if it has it, or it waits and asks for a portal). The same holds when the leader is the Sorceress who made the portal (`QuickTravel.selfTrip`: the gate, then `sendOutFirst`). Two tests (team_test); UNTESTED in a game. `docs/TEAM_SYNC.md` rewritten to the current rules and code.
- **A waypoint ride from town that a mate cannot follow**: the leader opens its portal on arrival and names it (`proceed`) when any mate in the game lacks that waypoint (the `Pather.useWaypoint` wrapper; `Waypoints.lacking`). Before, a follower lacking it waited 15 s, asked "cantreach", and the leader's TownChicken opened a portal on its next turn: easily past the 30 s timer, so a needless regroup. Affects a walking waypoint fetch (`journeyTo` starts with a waypoint ride when the leader has one on the way) and quests. `Waypoints.share` no longer opens a second portal when the wrapper did. UNTESTED in a game.

When all quests of a difficulty are done the bots idle until the user restarts them: `dynamicLeaderLoop` no longer switches to the next difficulty (it logs `quests: nothing left to do on X; the bots wait until you restart them` once). Go change, needs a server restart; `QuestTable.json` is read per game. Not verified live. Live on Normal (13:31-13:46): the followers' Anya talk moved quest 38's bit 4 (progress), never bit 0; Barabas logged "no quest is left to do on this difficulty". Open: each follower repeats the Anya talk in three rounds after the bit has moved (~30 s), "quest skipped" lines cost ~1 s each (~30 s a pass), and the leader's exit gate did not hold for Xena in Halls of Anguish (30 s timer broke the sync, 13:40:26).
- 2026-11-17 13:50:54 (live): the idle leader (`Quester.idle`, every quest done) was taken for hung by the town chicken thread's stuck watchdog (6 min without moving in town) and left the game, so "idle" became a restart loop every ~6 minutes. Fix (UNTESTED, loads with the next game): `Quester.idle` writes `data/idle.<profile>.txt` (a timestamp) every second, and `TownChicken.js` skips the stuck exit while that file is under 30 s old. Also seen: `TownChicken.js:313` reads `PartySync.holdForMissing`, which nothing defines (always false, one strict warning every few seconds): left alone, adding `holdForMissing: false` to PartySync would silence it.
- 2026-11-17 14:06:44 (live, Nightmare): a regroup that took more than 2 minutes broke the followers' guard (`PartySync.tripOn`, 2 min from the call): the mates already out through the maker's portal walked back to town "to meet up" (Follower.js, `lostAt`) while the maker waited in town for a slow mate, were sent out again, and so on (Sankt 14:06:44 and 14:07:32, Xena too). A longer `tripOn` (6 min) was tried and REVERTED the same day: the user said to find why the team gets split instead of adding guards. The slow mate was Xena doing a full chores visit (98k gold out, identify, sell, potions, 14:04:45-14:05:38) and then walking back to town again herself. The two regroups (14:01:29 Ancient's Way, 14:04:43 Worldstone Keep 2) were followers busy fighting/looting while the leader waited (30 s timer).
- 2026-11-17 (the user asked for it): a mate that cannot enter the leader's portal in town now says why (`Team.portalRefused`, called from `Follower.js` after "could not take ... portal"): gated by the act quest (`Pather.accessToAct`, act of the area) = a problem for the Activity list; not gated = "cantreach". `Team.sendOutFirst` reads it ("sendout" key) and does not wait for that mate (no more unbounded wait on a mate that cannot get through); `TownChicken.js` keeps the request while the leader is still in town (`portalAsked`, 90 s) and opens the new portal (`Pather.makePortal`) once the leader is out. The mate's way in is then the new portal or, failing that, its walk (Follower). Only the Act gate is checked; other gates (waypoint, area quests) are not.
